# Tight Fine-Grained Bounds for Direct Access on Join Queries

连接查询直接访问的紧致细粒度界

KARL BRINGMANN, Saarland University, Saarbrucken, Germany and Max Planck Institute for Informatics, Saarbrucken, Germany

卡尔·布林克曼，德国萨尔兰大学及马克斯·普朗克信息学研究所，德国萨尔布吕肯

NOFAR CARMELI, Inria, LIRMM, Univ. Montpellier, CNRS, Montpellier, France

诺法尔·卡梅利，法国国家信息与自动化研究所、蒙彼利埃大学LIRMM实验室、法国国家科学研究中心，法国蒙彼利埃

STEFAN MENGEL, Univ. Artois, CNRS, Centre de Recherche en Informatique de Lens (CRIL), Lens, France

斯特凡·门格尔，法国阿尔图瓦大学、法国国家科学研究中心朗斯信息学研究中心(CRIL)，法国朗斯

We consider the task of lexicographic direct access to query answers. That is, we want to simulate an array containing the answers of a join query sorted in a lexicographic order chosen by the user. A recent dichotomy showed for which queries and orders this task can be done in polylogarithmic access time after quasilinear preprocessing, but this dichotomy does not tell us how much time is required in the cases classified as hard. We determine the preprocessing time needed to achieve polylogarithmic access time for all join queries and all lexicographical orders. To this end, we propose a decomposition-based general algorithm for direct access on join queries. We then explore its optimality by proving lower bounds for the preprocessing time based on the hardness of a certain online Set-Disjointness problem, which shows that our algorithm's bounds are tight for all lexicographic orders on join queries. Then, we prove the hardness of Set-Disjointness based on the Zero-Clique Conjecture, which is an established conjecture from fine-grained complexity theory. Interestingly, while proving our lower bound, we show that self-joins do not affect the complexity of direct access (up to logarithmic factors). Our algorithm can also be used to solve queries with projections and relaxed order requirements, though in these cases, its running time is not necessarily optimal. We also show that similar techniques to those used in our lower bounds can be used to prove that, for enumerating answers to Loomis-Whitney joins, it is not possible to significantly improve upon trivially computing all answers at preprocessing. This, in turn, gives further evidence (based on the Zero-Clique Conjecture) to the enumeration hardness of self-join-free cyclic joins with respect to linear preprocessing and constant delay.

我们研究词典序直接访问查询结果的任务，即模拟一个按用户选定词典序排列的查询结果数组。近期二分法揭示了哪些查询和顺序可在拟线性预处理后实现多对数访问时间，但未说明被归类为困难情形的具体耗时。我们确定了所有连接查询和词典序实现多对数访问时间所需的预处理耗时。为此提出基于分解的连接查询直接访问通用算法，并通过基于在线集合不相交问题难度的预处理时间下界证明其最优性——该结果表明我们的算法对所有连接查询词典序都是紧致的。基于细粒度复杂度理论中成立的零团猜想，我们进一步证明了集合不相交问题的难度。值得注意的是，在证明下界过程中，我们发现自连接不影响直接访问的复杂度（对数因子内）。该算法也可处理含投影和宽松顺序要求的查询，尽管这些情况下其运行时间未必最优。类似下界证明技术还可用于表明：对于枚举Loomis-Whitney连接结果，无法显著优于预处理阶段直接计算全部结果。这为零自连接循环连接的枚举难度（基于线性预处理和常数延迟）提供了新的零团猜想证据。

## 1 Introduction

## 1 引言

Algorithms for direct access allow to access answers to a query on a database essentially as if they are materialized and stored in an array: Given an index $j \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,the algorithm returns the $j$ th answer (or an out-of-bounds error) in very little time. To make this possible,the algorithm first runs a preprocessing on the database that then allows answering arbitrary access queries efficiently. As the number of answers to a database query may be orders of magnitude larger than the size of the database, the goal is to avoid materializing all the answers during preprocessing and only simulate the array.

直接访问算法允许像访问物化存储的数组那样获取数据库查询结果：给定索引$j \in  \mathbb{N}$，算法能在极短时间内返回第$j$个结果（或越界错误）。为实现此功能，算法需先对数据库进行预处理，从而高效响应任意访问请求。由于数据库查询结果数量可能远超数据库本身规模，该技术旨在避免预处理阶段物化所有结果，仅模拟数组访问行为。

The direct access task (previously also called $j$ th answer and random access) was introduced by Bagan et al. [10]. They also mentioned that this task can be used for uniform sampling (by first counting and drawing a random index) or for enumerating all query answers (by consecutively accessing all indices). They devised an algorithm that runs in only linear preprocessing time and average constant time per access call for a large class of queries (first-order queries) on databases of bounded degree. Direct access algorithms were also considered for queries in monadic second order logic on databases of bounded treewidth [9]. To reason about general databases and still expect extremely efficient algorithms, another approach is to restrict the class of queries instead. Follow-up work gave linear preprocessing and logarithmic access algorithms for subclasses of conjunctive queries over general databases [13, 19, 26]. There, it was also explained how direct access can be used to sample without repetitions [19].

直接访问任务（先前也被称为$j$答案与随机访问）由Bagan等人[10]提出。他们同时指出该任务可用于均匀抽样（通过先计数再随机抽取索引）或枚举所有查询结果（通过连续访问所有索引）。针对有限度数数据库上的一类广泛查询（一阶查询），他们设计了一种仅需线性预处理时间且每次访问调用平均常数时间运行的算法。直接访问算法也被应用于有限树宽数据库的单子二阶逻辑查询[9]。为在通用数据库上保持算法高效性，另一种方法是限制查询类别。后续研究为通用数据库上的合取查询子类提供了线性预处理和对数访问算法[13,19,26]，其中还阐述了如何利用直接访问实现无重复抽样[19]。

Though all of the algorithms we mentioned above simulate storing the answers in a lexicographic order (whether they state it or not), one shortcoming they have in common is that the specific lexicographic order cannot be chosen by the user (but rather it depends on the query structure). Allowing the user to specify the order is desirable, because then direct access can be used for additional tasks that are sensitive to the order, such as median finding and boxplot computation. Progress in this direction was recently made by Carmeli et al. [18], who identified which combinations of a conjunctive query and a lexicographic order can be accessed with linear preprocessing time and logarithmic access time. They identified an easy-to-check substructure of the query, called disruptive trios, whose (non-)existence distinguishes the tractable cases (w.r.t. the time guarantees we mentioned) from the intractable ones. In particular, if we consider acyclic join queries, they suggested an algorithm that works for any lexicographic order that does not have disruptive trios with respect to the query. If the join query is also self-join-free, they proved conditional lower bounds stating that for all other variable orders, direct access with polylogarithmic direct access requires superlinear time preprocessing. These hardness results assume the hardness of Boolean matrix multiplication. Given the known hardness of self-join-free cyclic joins, if we also assume the hardness of hyperclique detection in hypergraphs, then this gives a dichotomy for all self-join-free join queries and lexicographic orders: They are tractable if and only if the query is acyclic with no disruptive trio with respect to the order.

尽管上述算法都模拟了按字典序存储答案（无论是否明示），其共同缺陷在于用户无法选择特定字典序（该顺序取决于查询结构）。允许用户指定顺序具有重要价值，因为这样直接访问可用于对顺序敏感的任务（如中位数查找和箱线图计算）。Carmeli等人[18]近期在此方向取得进展，他们确定了哪些合取查询与字典序组合能以线性预处理时间和对数访问时间实现访问。通过识别查询中名为"破坏性三元组"的可检测子结构，其存在与否区分了可高效处理（符合所述时间保证）与不可处理的情况。特别地，对于无环连接查询，他们提出的算法适用于查询中不存在破坏性三元组的任意字典序。若连接查询同时不含自连接，其条件性下界证明表明：对于其他变量顺序，要实现多对数直接访问必须进行超线性时间预处理——该硬度结论基于布尔矩阵乘法的困难性假设。结合已知无自连接循环查询的困难性，若进一步假设超图中超团检测的困难性，则对所有无自连接查询与字典序形成二分法：当且仅当查询是无环且对应顺序无破坏性三元组时，该组合可高效处理。

What happens if the query and order we want to compute happen to fall on the intractable side of the dichotomy? Also, what is the role of self-joins in the complexity of direct access to join queries? These questions were left open by previous work, and we aim to understand how much preprocessing is needed to achieve polylogarithmic access time for each combination of query, potentially containing self-joins, and order. To this end, we introduce disruption-free decompositions of a query with respect to a variable order. These can be seen as hypertree decompositions of the queries, induced by the desired variable orders, that resolve incompatibilities between the order and the query. Practically, these decompositions specify which relations should be joined at preprocessing to achieve an equivalent acyclic join query with no disruptive trios. We can then run the known direct access algorithm [18] with linear time preprocessing on the result of this preprocessing to get an algorithm for the query and order at hand with logarithmic access time. The cost of our preprocessing phase is therefore dominated by the time it takes to join the selected relations. We define the incompatibility number of a query and order and show that the preprocessing time of our solution is polynomial where the exponent is this number. Intuitively, the incompatibility number is 1 when the query is acyclic and the order is compatible, and this number grows as the incompatibility between the query and order grows.

若目标查询与顺序恰好属于二分法中不可处理的一侧，该如何应对？自连接在连接查询直接访问复杂度中扮演何种角色？这些遗留问题驱动我们探究：对于可能含自连接的任意查询与顺序组合，要实现多对数访问时间需要多少预处理。为此，我们提出变量顺序下查询的无冲突分解——可视为由目标变量顺序诱导的超树分解，用于化解顺序与查询间的冲突。实际操作中，这些分解指明预处理阶段应连接哪些关系，以生成等效的无环且无破坏性三元组的连接查询。随后应用已知直接访问算法[18]进行线性时间预处理，即可获得对数访问时间的解决方案。因此，我们预处理阶段的时间成本主要消耗于选定关系的连接操作。通过定义查询与顺序的冲突指数，证明解决方案的预处理时间与该指数的多项式成正比：直观而言，当查询无环且顺序兼容时该指数为1，其值随二者不兼容程度增加而增长。

Next, we aspire to know whether our solution can be improved. We show that, somewhat surprisingly, self-joins have no influence whatsoever on the complexity of direct access to join queries: If we change some relation symbols in a join query, then this leaves the preprocessing and access times unchanged up to polylogarithmic factors. Note that this is completely different than the situation for related query evaluation tasks, in particular, enumeration, where self-joins are known to change the complexity $\left\lbrack  {{11},{17}}\right\rbrack$ . The situation for direct access is instead similar to that for counting, where it is known that the complexity depends only on the underlying hypergraph, and thus self-joins play no role in the complexity analysis [22]. In fact, our results regarding the elimination of self-joins in the case of direct access are obtained by adapting similar proofs for counting complexity $\left\lbrack  {{20},{22}}\right\rbrack$ . Having dealt with self-joins,for the rest of our proofs,we can consider only self-join-free queries.

接下来，我们渴望探明现有解决方案是否具有优化空间。研究表明，自连接（self-joins）对连接查询直接访问的复杂度毫无影响——这一结论出人意料：当修改连接查询中的某些关系符号时，预处理时间和访问时间仅受多对数因子影响。值得注意的是，这与相关查询评估任务（特别是枚举查询）的情况截然不同，后者中自连接会显著改变复杂度$\left\lbrack  {{11},{17}}\right\rbrack$。直接访问的情况反而与计数查询类似，其复杂度仅取决于底层超图结构，因此自连接在复杂度分析中不起作用[22]。事实上，我们关于直接访问场景下消除自连接的结论，是通过借鉴计数复杂度领域的类似证明方法$\left\lbrack  {{20},{22}}\right\rbrack$得出的。在解决自连接问题后，后续证明中我们只需考虑无自连接的查询。

Though we can easily show that no other decomposition can be better than the specific decomposition we propose, we can still wonder whether a better algorithm can be achieved using an alternative technique. Thus, we set out to prove conditional lower bounds. Such lower bounds show hardness independently of a specific algorithmic technique, but instead they assume that some known problem cannot be solved significantly faster than by the state-of-the art algorithms. We show that the incompatibility number corresponds in a sense to the number of leaves of the largest star query that can be embedded in our given query. We then prove lower bounds for queries that allow embedding stars through a reduction from online $k$ -Set-Disjointness. In this problem,we are given during preprocessing $k$ sets of subsets of the universe,and then we need to answer queries that specify one subset from each set and ask whether these subsets are disjoint. On a technical level, in case the query is acyclic, the link of these hardness results with the existence of disruptive trios is that the latter correspond exactly to the possibility to embed a star with two leaves.

虽然可以轻易证明其他分解方法无法超越我们提出的特定分解方案，但仍需探究是否存在更优的替代算法技术。为此，我们着手建立条件性下界。这类下界证明不依赖于特定算法技术，而是基于"某些已知问题无法被现有算法显著优化"的前提假设。研究表明，不兼容数在某种意义上等同于给定查询中可嵌入的最大星型查询的叶节点数量。随后，我们通过归约在线$k$-集合互斥性问题，证明了允许嵌入星型结构的查询具有计算下界。该问题的预处理阶段需处理宇宙子集的$k$个集合族，查询阶段则需判定每组集合中各选一个子集是否互不相交。从技术层面看，当查询为非循环结构时，这些硬度结果与破坏性三元组存在的关联在于：后者恰好对应着可嵌入双叶星型结构的可能性。

Using known hardness results for 2-Set-Disjointness, our reduction shows that the acyclic hard cases in the known dichotomy need at least quadratic preprocessing, unless both the 3-SUM Conjecture and the APSP Conjecture fail. These are both central, well-established conjectures in fine-grained complexity theory; see, e.g., the survey in Reference [38]. To have tighter lower bounds for the case that the incompatibility number is not 2,we show the hardness of $k$ -Set-Disjointness through a reduction from the Zero-k-Clique Conjecture. This conjecture postulates that deciding whether a given edge-weighted $n$ -node graph contains a $k$ -clique of total edge weight 0 has no algorithm running in time $O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ for any $\varepsilon  > 0$ . For $k = 3$ this conjecture is implied by the 3SUM Conjecture and the APSP Conjecture,so it is very believable. For $k > 3$ the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture is a natural generalization of the case $k = 3$ ,and it was recently used in several contexts $\left\lbrack  {1,2,7,8,{16},{30}}\right\rbrack$ . Assuming the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture,we prove that the preprocessing time of our decomposition-based algorithm is (near-)optimal.

基于2-集合互斥性问题的已知硬度结果，我们的归约证明：除非3-SUM猜想与APSP猜想同时不成立，否则已知二分法中的非循环困难案例至少需要二次预处理时间。这两个猜想都是细粒度复杂度理论中公认的核心假设，详见参考文献[38]的综述。为获得不兼容数≠2时更精确的下界，我们通过归约零-$k$-团猜想证明了$k$-集合互斥性问题的计算难度。该猜想假定：对于给定边加权$n$节点图，判断是否存在总边权为零的$k$-团需要$O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$时间复杂度（对任意$\varepsilon  > 0$）。当$k = 3$时，该猜想可由3SUM与APSP猜想推导，故可信度极高；对于$k > 3$情形，零-$k$-团猜想是$k = 3$情况的自然推广，近期已在多个研究场景$\left\lbrack  {1,2,7,8,{16},{30}}\right\rbrack$中得到应用。基于零-$k$-团猜想，我们证明所提出的基于分解的算法其预处理时间具有（近似）最优性。

To conclude, our main result is as follows: A join query, potentially with self-joins, and an ordering of its variables with incompatibility number $\iota$ admit a lexicographic direct access algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{l}\right)$ and logarithmic access time. Moreover,if the Zero- $k$ -Clique conjecture holds for all $k$ ,then there is no lexicographic direct access algorithm for this query and order with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \epsilon }\right)$ and polylogarithmic access time for any $\varepsilon  > 0$ .

最终，我们得到的主要结论如下：对于可能含有自连接的连接查询，若其变量排序的不兼容数为$\iota$，则存在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{l}\right)$、对数访问时间的字典序直接访问算法。此外，若零-$k$-团猜想对所有$k$成立，则该查询在任何$\varepsilon  > 0$条件下都不存在具有$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间与多对数访问时间的字典序直接访问算法。

We also consider several extensions of this main result: We show that if we require the answers to be ordered in any lexicographic order-but do not specify which one-then the best running time we can expect is determined by the fractional hypertree width of the query. However, if we make no restriction on the order of the answers (i.e., not requiring it to be lexicographic), then this complexity bound can be improved. We show this by considering the 4-cycle query that has faster direct access than the one determined by its fractional hypertree width for lexicographic order. Finally, we extend our setting to partial lexicographic orders and queries with projections and show how our algorithm can handle these cases. However, we again show that this algorithm is not optimal for certain queries, leaving the identification of optimal runtime bounds open.

我们还考虑了该主要结果的若干扩展：证明若要求答案按任意字典序排列（但不指定具体顺序），则最佳运行时间由查询的分数超树宽度决定。但若不对答案顺序作任何限制（即不要求字典序），该复杂度界限可被突破。通过分析4环查询案例，我们展示了其在非字典序下的直接访问速度优于分数超树宽度决定的复杂度。最后，我们将框架扩展至部分字典序和含投影的查询，阐明算法如何应对这些场景，但同时指出该算法对某些查询并非最优，最优运行时界的确定仍待解决。

As we develop our lower bound results, we notice that our techniques can also be used in the context of constant delay enumeration of query answers. We show that,assuming the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture,the preprocessing of any constant delay enumeration algorithm for the $k$ -variable Loomis-Whitney join is roughly at least $\Omega \left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right) }\right)$ ,which tightly matches the trivial algorithm in which the answers are materialized during preprocessing using a worst-case optimal join algorithm [33, 34, 37]. From the lower bound for Loomis-Whitney joins, we then infer the hardness of other cyclic joins using a construction by Brault-Baron [13]. Specifically, we conclude that the self-join-free join queries that allow constant delay enumeration after linear processing are exactly the acyclic ones,unless the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture fails for some $k$ . This dichotomy was already established based on the hardness of hyperclique detection in hypergraphs [11, 13]; we here give more evidence for this dichotomy by showing it also holds under a different complexity assumption.

在推导下界结果时，我们发现相关技术也可应用于查询答案的常数延迟枚举场景。基于零-$k$-团猜想，我们证明$k$-变量Loomis-Whitney连接的常数延迟枚举算法预处理时间至少需要$\Omega \left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right) }\right)$，这与最坏情况下最优连接算法[33,34,37]预先生成所有答案的朴素算法严格匹配。由此下界出发，通过Brault-Baron[13]的构造方法，我们进一步推导出其他循环连接的困难性。具体而言，除非零-$k$-团猜想对某些$k$不成立，否则仅有无环连接查询能在线性预处理后实现常数延迟枚举。该二分性虽已基于超图中超团检测难度[11,13]建立，我们通过不同复杂性假设下的验证为其提供了新证据。

A preliminary version of this manuscript appeared in a conference proceedings [15]. The current version contains full proofs omitted in the previous version, as well as two new sections: Section 6 and Section 8, discussing the implications of our main result for queries with self-joins, queries with projections, and relaxed order requirements.

本文初稿曾发表于会议论文集[15]。当前版本补充了先前省略的完整证明，并新增第6节与第8节，探讨主要结果对含自连接查询、含投影查询及宽松排序要求的理论意义。

This manuscript is organized as follows: Preliminary definitions and results are given in Section 2. Section 3 defines disruption-free decompositions and the incompatibility number and provides the direct-access algorithm. Section 4 proves the hardness of direct access for self-join-free join queries assuming the hardness of set-disjointness, while Section 5 proves the hardness of set-disjointness assuming the more established Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture. Section 6 proves the equivalence in direct-access complexity between queries with and without self-joins. Then, Section 7 puts the previous sections together and summarizes tight complexity bounds for join queries. Relaxed order requirements and queries with projections are discussed in Section 8. Finally, Section 9 proves the enumeration lower bounds for cyclic queries,assuming the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture.

本文结构如下：第2节给出基础定义与结果；第3节定义无干扰分解、不兼容数及直接访问算法；第4节基于集合不相交假设证明无自连接查询的直接访问困难性；第5节依据更成熟的零-$k$-团猜想证明集合不相交的困难性；第6节论证自连接与否对直接访问复杂度的等价性；第7节整合前述内容，总结连接查询的紧复杂度界限；第8节讨论宽松排序要求与含投影查询；第9节基于零-$k$-团猜想证明循环查询的枚举下界。

## 2 Preliminaries

## 2 预备知识

### 2.1 Databases and Queries

### 2.1 数据库与查询

A join query $Q$ is an expression of the form $Q\left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{\ell }\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{n}\right)$ ,where each ${R}_{i}$ is a relation symbol, ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{n}$ are tuples of variables,and $\overrightarrow{u}$ is a tuple of all variables in $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$ . Each ${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$ is called an atom of $Q$ . We also denote by $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ the set $\overrightarrow{u}$ of all variables appearing in $Q$ . A query is called self-join-free if no relation symbol appears in two different atoms. If the same relation symbol appears in two different atoms, ${R}_{i} = {R}_{j}$ ,then ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$ and ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}$ must have the same arity. A conjunctive query (or,for short,a query) is defined as a join query,with the exception that $\overrightarrow{u}$ may contain any subset of the query variables. We say that the query variables that do not appear in $\overrightarrow{u}$ are projected. Defined this way, join queries are simply conjunctive queries without projections.

连接查询$Q$是形如$Q\left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{\ell }\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{n}\right)$的表达式，其中每个${R}_{i}$是关系符号，${\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{n}$是变量元组，$\overrightarrow{u}$则是$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$中所有变量组成的元组。每个${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$称为$Q$的原子。我们用$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$表示$Q$中出现的所有变量集合$\overrightarrow{u}$。若查询中不存在关系符号出现在两个不同原子的情况，则称该查询为无自连接查询。若同一关系符号出现在两个不同原子${R}_{i} = {R}_{j}$中，则${\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$与${\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}$必须具有相同元数。合取查询（简称查询）定义为连接查询，但$\overrightarrow{u}$可包含查询变量的任意子集。未出现在$\overrightarrow{u}$中的查询变量称为被投影。按此定义，连接查询即无投影的合取查询。

The input to a query $Q$ is a database $D$ ,which assigns every relation symbol ${R}_{i}$ in the query with a relation ${R}_{i}^{D}$ : a finite set of tuples of constants,each tuple having the same arity as ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$ . The domain $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ of $D$ is the set of all constants appearing in tuples in the relations of $D$ . The size $\left| D\right|$ of $D$ is defined as the total number of tuples in all of its relations. (More generally, $\left| E\right|$ denotes the cardinality of a set $E$ .) An answer to a query $Q$ over a database $D$ is determined by a mapping $\mu$ from the variables of $Q$ to the constants of $D$ such that $\mu \left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D}$ for every atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$ in $Q$ . The answer is then $\mu \left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)$ . The set of all answers to $Q$ over $D$ is denoted $Q\left( D\right)$ .

查询$Q$的输入是数据库$D$，它为查询中的每个关系符号${R}_{i}$分配一个关系${R}_{i}^{D}$：由常量元组构成的有限集合，每个元组与${\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$具有相同元数。数据库$D$的域$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$是其所有关系中出现的常量集合。数据库$D$的大小$\left| D\right|$定义为其所有关系中的元组总数（更一般地，$\left| E\right|$表示集合$E$的基数）。查询$Q$在数据库$D$上的答案由从查询变量到数据库常量的映射$\mu$确定，该映射需满足对查询中每个原子${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$都有$\mu \left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D}$。此时答案为$\mu \left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)$。查询$Q$在数据库$D$上的所有答案集合记为$Q\left( D\right)$。

A lexicographic order of a join query $Q$ is specified by a permutation $L$ of the query variables. We assume databases come with a linear order on their constants. Then, $L$ defines a linear order over $Q\left( D\right)$ : The order between two distinct answers is defined to be the order between their assignments to the first variable in $L$ on which their assignments differ.

连接查询$Q$的字典序由查询变量的排列$L$指定。假设数据库常量带有线性序，则$L$定义了$Q\left( D\right)$上的线性序：两个不同答案之间的顺序由它们在$L$中首个赋值不同的变量上的赋值顺序决定。

### 2.2 Query Answering Tasks

### 2.2 查询应答任务

The problems we consider are typically defined by a query $Q$ ,and the input is a database $D$ for $Q$ . We always work in the model of data complexity,meaning the size of the query is considered constant. In particular,the $O$ -notation may hide constant factors that depend on $Q$ . We consider three types of tasks:

我们研究的问题通常由查询$Q$定义，输入是为$Q$构建的数据库$D$。我们始终在数据复杂度模型下工作，即视查询大小为常数。特别地，$O$表示法可能隐藏依赖于$Q$的常数因子。我们考虑三类任务：

- Testing is the task where,after a preprocessing phase,the user can specify a tuple $\overrightarrow{a}$ of constants,and one has to determine whether it is a query answer (i.e.,whether $\overrightarrow{a} \in  Q\left( D\right)$ ).

- 测试任务指在预处理阶段后，用户可指定常量元组$\overrightarrow{a}$，系统需判定其是否为查询答案（即是否满足$\overrightarrow{a} \in  Q\left( D\right)$）。

- Enumeration is the task of listing all query answers in $Q\left( D\right)$ . We measure the efficiency of an enumeration algorithm with two parameters: the time before the first answer, which we call preprocessing time, and the time between successive answers, which we call delay.

- 枚举任务是指在$Q\left( D\right)$中列出所有查询答案。我们通过两个参数衡量枚举算法的效率：首个答案产出前的耗时（称为预处理时间），以及连续答案之间的间隔耗时（称为延迟）。

- Direct-access in lexicographic order is a task defined by a query $Q$ and a permutation $L$ of its free variables. After a preprocessing phase,the user can specify an index $j$ and expects the $j$ th answer in $Q\left( D\right)$ according to the lexicographic order corresponding to $L$ or an out-of-bounds error if there are less than $j$ answers. We call the time it takes to provide an answer given an index the access time.

- 字典序直接访问任务由查询$Q$及其自由变量的排列$L$定义。预处理阶段后，用户可指定索引$j$并期望获取$Q\left( D\right)$中按$L$对应字典序的第$j$个答案，若答案数不足则返回越界错误。我们将根据索引提供答案的耗时称为访问时间。

An exact reduction from a query ${Q}_{1}$ to a query ${Q}_{2}$ is a mapping computable in linear time,which associates to each database ${D}_{1}$ for ${Q}_{1}$ a database ${D}_{2}$ for ${Q}_{2}$ such that there is a bijection $\tau$ from ${Q}_{2}\left( {D}_{2}\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}\left( {D}_{1}\right)$ ,and $\tau$ can be computed in constant time. Consider two queries ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ with the same free variables. An exact reduction via a bijection $\tau$ from ${Q}_{1}$ to ${Q}_{2}$ is called lex-preserving if,for every permutation $L$ of the free variables,and for every pair $\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right)$ of answers in ${Q}_{2}\left( {D}_{2}\right)$ ,we have that ${a}_{1} \prec  {a}_{2}$ if and only if $\tau \left( {a}_{1}\right)  \prec  \tau \left( {a}_{2}\right)$ ,where $\prec$ represents the lexicographic order specified by $L$ . Note that if there is a lex-preserving exact reduction from ${Q}_{1}$ to ${Q}_{2}$ ,and ${Q}_{2}$ has a direct access algorithm for some lexicographic order $L$ with preprocessing time in $\Omega \left( \left| {D}_{2}\right| \right)$ ,then ${Q}_{1}$ has a direct access algorithm for $L$ with the same preprocessing and access time complexities.

从查询${Q}_{1}$到查询${Q}_{2}$的精确归约是指可在线性时间内计算的映射：它将${Q}_{1}$的每个数据库${D}_{1}$关联到${Q}_{2}$的数据库${D}_{2}$，使得存在${Q}_{2}\left( {D}_{2}\right)$到${Q}_{1}\left( {D}_{1}\right)$的双射$\tau$，且$\tau$可在常数时间内计算。考虑两个自由变量相同的查询${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$，若对于自由变量的每个排列$L$及${Q}_{2}\left( {D}_{2}\right)$中的每对答案$\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right)$，当且仅当$\tau \left( {a}_{1}\right)  \prec  \tau \left( {a}_{2}\right)$时满足${a}_{1} \prec  {a}_{2}$（其中$\prec$表示$L$指定的字典序），则称通过双射$\tau$从${Q}_{1}$到${Q}_{2}$的精确归约是保字典序的。注意：若存在从${Q}_{1}$到${Q}_{2}$的保字典序精确归约，且${Q}_{2}$对某字典序$L$具有预处理时间在$\Omega \left( \left| {D}_{2}\right| \right)$内的直接访问算法，则${Q}_{1}$对$L$也具有相同预处理和访问时间复杂度的直接访问算法。

### 2.3 Hypergraphs

### 2.3 超图

A hypergraph $H = \left( {V,E}\right)$ consists of a finite set $V$ of vertices and a set $E$ of edges,i.e.,subsets of $V$ . Given a set $S \subseteq  V$ ,the hypergraph $H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack$ induced by $S$ is $\left( {S,{E}_{S}}\right)$ with ${E}_{S} = \{ e \cap  S \mid  e \in  E\}$ . A super-hypergraph ${H}^{\prime }$ of $H$ is a hypergraph $\left( {V,{E}^{\prime }}\right)$ such that $E \subseteq  {E}^{\prime }$ . By ${N}_{H}\left( v\right)$ ,we denote the set of neighbors of a vertex $v$ in $H$ ,i.e., ${N}_{H}\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ u \in  V \mid  u \neq  v,\exists e \in  E : u,v \in  e\}$ . For $S \subseteq  V$ ,we define ${N}_{H}\left( S\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{v \in  S}}{N}_{H}\left( v\right)  \smallsetminus  S$ . In this notation,we sometimes leave out the subscript $H$ when it is clear from the context.

超图$H = \left( {V,E}\right)$由有限顶点集$V$和边集$E$（即$V$的子集）构成。给定集合$S \subseteq  V$，由$S$诱导的超图$H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack$是满足${E}_{S} = \{ e \cap  S \mid  e \in  E\}$的$\left( {S,{E}_{S}}\right)$。超图${H}^{\prime }$是$H$的超超图，当且仅当$E \subseteq  {E}^{\prime }$。${N}_{H}\left( v\right)$表示顶点$v$在$H$中的邻居集，即${N}_{H}\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ u \in  V \mid  u \neq  v,\exists e \in  E : u,v \in  e\}$。对于$S \subseteq  V$，我们定义${N}_{H}\left( S\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{v \in  S}}{N}_{H}\left( v\right)  \smallsetminus  S$。在此表示法中，当下标$H$明确时可能省略。

A walk between vertices $s$ and $t$ in a hypergraph $H = \left( {V,E}\right)$ is a sequence $s = {v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell } = t$ of vertices such that for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  {\ell  - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,we have that there is an edge $e \in  E$ with ${v}_{i}{v}_{i + 1} \in  E$ . Here, we allow $s = t$ in which case the walk consists of the single vertex ${v}_{1} = s = t$ . The set ${V}^{s}$ of vertices reachable from $s$ in $H$ is defined to consist of all vertices $t$ such that there is a walk between $s$ and $t$ in $H$ . Note that $s$ itself is reachable from $s$ ,so in any case $s \in  {V}^{s}$ . The connected component of $s$ is defined as $H\left\lbrack  {V}^{s}\right\rbrack$ .

超图$H = \left( {V,E}\right)$中顶点$s$与$t$间的行走是指一个顶点序列$s = {v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell } = t$，其中对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {\ell  - 1}\right\rbrack$，都存在包含${v}_{i}{v}_{i + 1} \in  E$的边$e \in  E$。此处允许$s = t$的情况，此时行走仅由单个顶点${v}_{1} = s = t$构成。从$s$出发在$H$中可到达的顶点集合${V}^{s}$定义为：所有存在$s$到$t$行走的顶点$t$。注意$s$自身总是可到达的，因此无论如何$s \in  {V}^{s}$。$s$的连通分量定义为$H\left\lbrack  {V}^{s}\right\rbrack$。

A hypergraph $H$ is called acyclic if we can eliminate all of its vertices by iteratively applying the following two rules ${}^{1}$ :

当超图$H$能通过迭代应用以下两条规则${}^{1}$消除所有顶点时，称为无环超图：

- if there is an edge $e \in  E$ that is completely contained in another edge ${e}^{\prime } \in  E$ ,then delete $e$ from $H$ ,and

- 若存在边$e \in  E$完全被另一条边${e}^{\prime } \in  E$包含，则从$H$中删除$e$；

- if there is a vertex $v \in  V$ that is contained in a single edge $e \in  E$ ,then delete $v$ from $H$ ,i.e., delete $v$ from $V$ and from $e$ .

- 若存在顶点$v \in  V$仅属于单一边$e \in  E$，则从$H$中删除$v$，即从$V$和$e$中移除$v$。

An order in which the vertices can be eliminated in the above procedure is called an elimination order for $H$ . Note that it might be possible to apply the above rules on several vertices or edges at the same moment. In that case, the order in which they are applied does not change the final result of the process.

上述过程中消除顶点的顺序称为$H$的消除序。注意可能存在同时适用多条规则的情况，此时应用顺序不影响最终结果。

Given a hypergraph and a permutation of its vertices, a disruptive trio consists of three vertices ${v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}$ such that ${v}_{3}$ appears after ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ ,the vertices ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ are not neighbors,but ${v}_{3}$ is a neighbor to both ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ . A permutation $L$ of the vertices of a hypergraph $H$ is the reverse of an elimination order for $H$ if and only if $H$ is acyclic and $L$ contains no disruptive trio with $H$ [13].

给定超图及其顶点排列，破坏性三元组指三个顶点${v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}$满足：${v}_{3}$位于${v}_{1}$和${v}_{2}$之后，且${v}_{1}$与${v}_{2}$非邻接，但${v}_{3}$同时与两者邻接。当且仅当超图$H$无环且排列$L$不含破坏性三元组时，该排列是$H$消除序的逆序[13]。

A fractional edge cover of $H = \left( {V,E}\right)$ is defined as a mapping $\mu  : E \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ such that for every $v \in  V$ ,we have $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : v \in  e}}\mu \left( e\right)  \geq  1$ . The weight of $\mu$ is defined as $\mu \left( E\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  E}}\mu \left( e\right)$ . The fractional edge cover number of $H$ is ${\rho }^{ * }\left( H\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\min }\limits_{\mu }\mu \left( E\right)$ where the minimum is taken over all fractional edge covers of $H$ . We remark that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( H\right)$ and an optimal fractional edge cover of $H$ can be computed efficiently by linear programming.

$H = \left( {V,E}\right)$的分数边覆盖定义为映射$\mu  : E \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，使得对每个$v \in  V$满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : v \in  e}}\mu \left( e\right)  \geq  1$。其权重为$\mu \left( E\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  E}}\mu \left( e\right)$。$H$的分数边覆盖数是${\rho }^{ * }\left( H\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\min }\limits_{\mu }\mu \left( E\right)$（取所有分数边覆盖的最小值）。注：${\rho }^{ * }\left( H\right)$且最优分数边覆盖可通过线性规划高效计算。

Every join query $Q$ has an underlying hypergraph $H$ ,where the vertices of $H$ correspond to the variables of $Q$ and the edges of $H$ correspond to the variable scopes of the atoms of $Q$ . We use $Q$ and $H$ interchangeably in our notation.

每个连接查询$Q$都有基础超图$H$，其顶点对应$Q$的变量，边对应原子谓词的变量作用域。本文表示中$Q$与$H$可互换使用。

### 2.4 Known Algorithms

### 2.4 已知算法

Here, we state some results that we will use in the remainder of this article. All running time bounds are in the word-RAM model with $O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ -bit words and unit-cost operations. Note that the values stored in the registers are bounded by ${n}^{c}$ for some constant $c$ ,and this model allows sorting $m$ such values in time $O\left( {c\left( {m + n}\right) }\right)$ using radix sort; see,e.g.,Reference [21,Section 6.3].

在此，我们陈述一些将在本文后续部分使用的结果。所有运行时间界限均基于字长为$O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$位且采用单位成本运算的字-RAM模型。需注意寄存器存储的数值受限于某个常数$c$的${n}^{c}$，该模型允许通过基数排序在$O\left( {c\left( {m + n}\right) }\right)$时间内对$m$个此类数值进行排序；具体可参阅文献[21,第6.3节]。

THEOREM 1 ([18]). If an acyclic join query $Q$ and an ordering $L$ of its variables have no disruptive trios,then lexicographic direct access for $Q$ and $L$ can be solved with $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ preprocessing and $O\left( {\log \left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$ access time.

定理1([18])。若一个无环连接查询$Q$及其变量排序$L$不存在破坏性三元组，则针对$Q$和$L$的字典序直接访问问题可通过$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {\log \left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$访问时间解决。

A celebrated result by Atserias, Marx, and Grohe [6] shows that join queries of fractional edge cover number $k$ ,can,on any database $D$ ,result in query results of size at most $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{k}\right)$ ,and this bound is tight for every query for some databases. The upper bound is made algorithmic by so-called worst-case optimal join algorithms.

Atserias、Marx和Grohe[6]提出的著名结果表明：对于分数边覆盖数为$k$的连接查询，在任何数据库$D$上可能产生的查询结果规模上限为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{k}\right)$，且该界限对某些数据库中的每个查询都是紧致的。通过所谓的最坏情况最优连接算法，该上界可实现算法化。

THEOREM 2 ([33,34,37]). There is an algorithm that for every join query $Q$ of fractional edge cover number ${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$ ,given a database $D$ ,computes $Q\left( D\right)$ in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right) }\right)$ .

定理2([33,34,37])。存在一个算法，对于任何分数边覆盖数为${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$的连接查询$Q$，给定数据库$D$时，可在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内计算出$Q\left( D\right)$。

### 2.5 Fine-grained Complexity

### 2.5 细粒度复杂度

Fine-grained complexity theory aims to find the exact exponent of the best possible algorithm for any problem; see, e.g., Reference [38] for a recent survey. Since unconditional lower bounds of this form are currently far out of reach, fine-grained complexity provides conditional lower bounds that hold when assuming a conjecture about some central, well-studied problem.

细粒度复杂度理论旨在为任何问题寻找最佳算法的精确指数；相关综述可参阅文献[38]。由于目前无法获得此类无条件下限，该理论通过假设某些核心研究问题的猜想，提供条件性下限。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{1}$ This is called the GYO-algorithm; see,e.g.,Algorithm 6.4.4 in the standard textbook [3].

${}^{1}$ 这被称为GYO算法，标准教材[3]中的算法6.4.4节有所阐述。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Some important reductions and algorithms in fine-grained complexity are randomized, i.e., algorithms are allowed to make random choices in their run and may return wrong results with a certain probability; see, e.g., Reference [32] for an introduction. Throughout the article, when we write "randomized algorithm," we always mean a randomized algorithm with success probability at least $2/3$ . It is well known that the success probability can be boosted to any $1 - \delta$ by repeating the algorithm $O\left( {\log \left( {1/\delta }\right) }\right)$ times and returning the majority result. In particular,we can assume to have success probability at least $1 - 1/{n}^{10}$ at the cost of only a factor $O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ . Our reductions typically worsen the success probability by some amount, but using boosting it can be improved back to $1 - 1/{n}^{10}$ ; we do not make this explicit in our proofs.

细粒度复杂度中的某些重要归约和算法是随机化的，即允许算法在运行中进行随机选择，并以特定概率返回错误结果；入门介绍可参阅文献[32]。本文中"随机化算法"特指成功概率至少为$2/3$的算法。众所周知，通过重复执行$O\left( {\log \left( {1/\delta }\right) }\right)$次并取多数结果，可将成功概率提升至任意$1 - \delta$。特别地，我们可仅付出$O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$倍代价获得至少$1 - 1/{n}^{10}$的成功概率。虽然我们的归约通常会降低成功概率，但通过增强技术可将其恢复至$1 - 1/{n}^{10}$；证明过程中将不再赘述此点。

We stress that randomization is only used in our hardness reductions, while all our algorithmic results are deterministic.

需要强调的是，随机化仅用于我们的硬度归约，所有算法结果均为确定性。

We will base our lower bounds on the following problem, which is defined for every fixed constant $k \in  \mathbb{N},k \geq  3$ .

我们将基于以下问题建立下限，该问题针对每个固定常数$k \in  \mathbb{N},k \geq  3$定义。

Definition 3. In the Zero- $k$ -Clique problem,given an $n$ -node graph $G = \left( {V,E}\right)$ with edge-weights $w : E \rightarrow  \left\{  {-{n}^{c},\ldots ,{n}^{c}}\right\}$ for some constant $c \geq  1$ ,the task is to decide whether there are vertices ${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k} \in  V$ such that they form a $k$ -clique (i.e., $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \in  E$ for all $1 \leq  i < j \leq  k$ ) and their total edge-weight is 0 (i.e., $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  i < j \leq  k}}w\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right)  = 0$ ). In this case,we say that ${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}$ is a zero-clique.

定义3. 在零-$k$-团问题中，给定一个具有边权重$w : E \rightarrow  \left\{  {-{n}^{c},\ldots ,{n}^{c}}\right\}$的$n$节点图$G = \left( {V,E}\right)$（其中$c \geq  1$为某常数），任务是判定是否存在顶点${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k} \in  V$构成$k$-团（即对所有$1 \leq  i < j \leq  k$满足$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \in  E$）且其总边权重为零（即$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  i < j \leq  k}}w\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right)  = 0$）。此时，我们称${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}$为零团。

We remark that,by hashing techniques,we can assume $c \leq  {10k}$ ; see,e.g.,Reference [1, Lemma 3.2]. Due to this bound, we may assume that all numbers that we encounter in the remainder of this article have bit-size $O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ and thus fit into a constant number of memory cells. As a further consequence, all arithmetic operations on all numbers can be done in constant time in the RAM model. We thus tacitly ignore the size of numbers and the cost of operations on them in the remainder of this article.

需要说明的是，通过哈希技术，我们可以假定$c \leq  {10k}$（参见参考文献[1，引理3.2]）。基于此界限，本文后续部分涉及的所有数字均可视为具有$O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$位比特长度，因而可存入恒定数量的存储单元。由此进一步推论，在RAM模型中对所有数字的算术运算都可在常数时间内完成。因此本文后续将默认忽略数字大小及其运算成本。

The following conjectures have been used in several places; see, e.g., References [1, 2, 7, 8, 16, 30]. The first conjecture is for a fixed $k$ ,the second postulates hardness for all $k$ .

以下猜想已在多处应用（参见参考文献[1,2,7,8,16,30]）：第一猜想针对固定$k$，第二猜想则断言所有$k$情形下的计算困难性。

Conjecture 1 (Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture). For no constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ Zero-k-Clique has a randomized algorithm running in time $O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ .

猜想1（零-$k$-团猜想）。对于任何常数$\varepsilon  > 0$，零-k-团问题不存在运行时间为$O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$的随机算法。

CONJECTURE 2 (ZERO-CLIQUE CONJECTURE). For every $k \geq  3$ the Zero-k-Clique Conjecture is true.

猜想2（零团猜想）。对于任意$k \geq  3$，零-k-团猜想均成立。

It is known that the Zero-3-Clique Conjecture, also called Zero-Triangle Conjecture, is implied by two other famous conjectures: the 3SUM Conjecture [39] and the APSP Conjecture [40]. Since we do not use these conjectures directly in this article, we do not formulate them here and refer the interested reader to the survey in Reference [38].

已知零-3-团猜想（亦称零三角猜想）可由另外两个著名猜想推导得出：3SUM猜想[39]与APSP猜想[40]。因本文不直接使用这些猜想，故不在此赘述，有兴趣的读者可参阅文献[38]的综述。

We remark that instead of Zero- $k$ -Clique some references work with the Exact-Weight- $k$ -Clique problem,where we are additionally given a target weight $t$ and want to find a $k$ -clique of weight $t$ . Both problems are known to have the same time complexity up to constant factors; see,e.g., Reference [1].

需指出，部分文献采用精确权重-$k$-团问题替代零-$k$-团问题，该问题额外给定目标权重$t$，要求找出权重为$t$的$k$-团。已知这两个问题的时间复杂度在常数因子范围内相同（参见文献[1]）。

A related problem is Min-k-Clique,where we are looking for the $k$ -clique of minimum weight. The Min- $k$ -Clique Conjecture postulates that this problem also cannot be solved in time $O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ . It is known that the Min- $k$ -Clique Conjecture implies the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture; see,e.g., Reference [1].

相关问题是最小-$k$-团，即寻找最小权重的$k$-团。最小-$k$-团猜想断言该问题同样无法在$O\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$时间内解决。已知最小-$k$-团猜想可推出零-$k$-团猜想（参见文献[1]）。

As common in fine-grained complexity,we use $\widetilde{O}$ -notation for many of our results,which is similar to $O$ -notation but suppresses polylogarithmic factors. More precisely,we say that an algorithm runs in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {g\left( n\right) }\right)$ if there is a constant $c$ such that it runs in time $O\left( {{\log }^{c}\left( n\right)  \cdot  g\left( n\right) }\right)$ .

如精细复杂度分析中常见做法，我们使用$\widetilde{O}$-记号表示多数结果，该记号类似于$O$-记号但可忽略多对数因子。更准确地说，若存在常数$c$使得算法在$O\left( {{\log }^{c}\left( n\right)  \cdot  g\left( n\right) }\right)$时间内运行，则称其具有$\widetilde{O}\left( {g\left( n\right) }\right)$时间复杂度。

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01964f2e-6219-78a6-9df6-c3ab3c0bbb52_7.jpg?x=505&y=233&w=546&h=538&r=0"/>

Fig. 1. The hypergraph of Example 5. The original (hyper)graph of the query is drawn in full edges. The edges that we add in the construction are dashed.

图1. 示例5的超图。查询的原始(超)图以实线绘制，构造过程中添加的边以虚线表示。

<!-- Media -->

## 3 Disruption-free Decompositions and the Direct-access Algorithm

## 3 无干扰分解与直接访问算法

In this section, we give an algorithm that, for every join query and desired lexicographic order, provides direct access to the query result on an input database. In particular, we propose to add new atoms to the query such that the resulting query has no disruptive trios with respect to the order. Then, any direct-access algorithm that assumes acyclicity and no disruptive-trios can be applied, provided we can compute a database for the new query that yields the same answers. We show that the new query is essentially a generalized hypertree decomposition of optimal fractional hypertree width out of all decompositions with the required properties. This shows that the suggested solution here is the best we can achieve using a decomposition, but it does not mean we cannot do better using a different method-the latter question is studied in the later sections of this article.

本节提出一种算法，可为任意连接查询和指定字典序提供对输入数据库查询结果的直接访问。具体而言，我们通过向查询添加新原子，使最终查询相对于该序不存在干扰三元组。只要能为新查询计算出产生相同答案的数据库，任何基于无环性和无干扰三元组假设的直接访问算法均可适用。研究表明，新查询本质上是具有所需属性的所有分解中分数超树宽度最优的广义超树分解。这表明当前方案是使用分解法能达到的最佳效果，但并不意味着无法通过其他方法获得更优解——后文将探讨这一问题。

### 3.1 Disruption-free Decompositions

### 3.1 无干扰分解

We describe a process that iteratively eliminates disruptive trios in a query by adding new atoms.

我们描述一种通过添加新原子迭代消除查询中干扰三元组的过程。

Definition 4 (Disruption-free Decomposition). Let $Q$ be a join query and $L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$ be an ordering of its variables. Let ${H}_{\ell }$ be the hypergraph of $Q$ ,and for $i = \ell ,\ldots ,1$ construct hypergraph ${H}_{i - 1}$ from ${H}_{i}$ by adding an edge ${e}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right) }\right\}$ . The disruption-free decomposition of $Q$ for $L$ is then defined to be ${H}_{0}$ .

定义4(无干扰分解)。设$Q$为连接查询，$L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$是其变量的排序。令${H}_{\ell }$表示$Q$的超图，对于$i = \ell ,\ldots ,1$通过添加边${e}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right) }\right\}$从${H}_{i}$构造超图${H}_{i - 1}$。则$Q$关于$L$的无干扰分解定义为${H}_{0}$。

Example 5. Consider the query $Q\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{5}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{v}_{2},{v}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{v}_{3},{v}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right)$ with the order $\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right)$ of its variables. Its hypergraph is shown in Figure 1. In the first step of the construction of Definition 4,we add the edge $\left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}   \cup  N\left( {v}_{5}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ . Similarly,in the second step,we add $\left\{  {{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4}}\right\}$ . For the third step,note that $N\left( {v}_{3}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ due to the edges we have added before. Out of these neighbors,only ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ come before ${v}_{3}$ in the order. So,we add the edge $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$ . Finally,for ${v}_{2}$ ,we add the edge $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ and for ${v}_{1}$ the singleton edge $\left\{  {v}_{1}\right\}$ .

示例5. 考虑变量序为$\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right)$的查询$Q\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{5}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{v}_{2},{v}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{v}_{3},{v}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right)$，其超图如图1所示。在定义4构造的第一步，我们添加边$\left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}   \cup  N\left( {v}_{5}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$；第二步类似地添加$\left\{  {{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4}}\right\}$。第三步时注意到由于先前添加的边，$N\left( {v}_{3}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$成立。这些邻居中仅${v}_{1}$和${v}_{2}$在排序中先于${v}_{3}$，因此添加边$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$。最后对${v}_{2}$添加边$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$，对${v}_{1}$添加单子边$\left\{  {v}_{1}\right\}$。

Proposition 6. The disruption-free decomposition of a join query $Q$ for $L$ is an acyclic super-hypergraph ${H}_{0}$ of the hypergraph of $Q$ without any disruptive trios with respect to $L$ .

命题6. 连接查询$Q$关于$L$的无干扰分解是其超图的无环超超图${H}_{0}$，且相对于$L$不存在任何干扰三元组。

Proof. Let $L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$ . We also use the notation ${e}_{i}$ as defined in Definition 4. To prove that ${H}_{0}$ is acyclic,we will show that the reverse of $L$ is an elimination order. First,set $i = \ell$ . Since ${e}_{i}$ contains all neighbors of ${v}_{i}$ (that were not yet eliminated),we have that ${e}_{i}$ contains all other edges containing ${v}_{i}$ ,and we can eliminate all of these edges. Afterwards, ${v}_{i}$ appears only in ${e}_{i}$ ,so we can eliminate ${v}_{i}$ . Note that we did not eliminate any edges ${e}_{j}$ with $j < i$ ,since all edges we eliminated contain ${v}_{i}$ (and ${e}_{j}$ does not contain ${v}_{i}$ by construction when $j < i$ ). Thus,we can repeat these steps with $i - 1$ instead of $i$ until all vertices are eliminated. It follows that,as claimed, the reverse of $L$ is an elimination order for ${H}_{0}$ ,and thus ${H}_{0}$ is indeed acyclic.

证明。令$L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$。我们采用定义4中定义的记号${e}_{i}$。为证明${H}_{0}$是无环的，将证明$L$的逆序是消元序。首先设$i = \ell$。由于${e}_{i}$包含${v}_{i}$的所有未消元邻点，故${e}_{i}$包含所有含${v}_{i}$的边，可消去这些边。此后${v}_{i}$仅出现在${e}_{i}$中，故可消去${v}_{i}$。注意我们未消去任何满足$j < i$的边${e}_{j}$，因所有被消去的边都包含${v}_{i}$（而根据构造当$j < i$时${e}_{j}$不含${v}_{i}$）。因此可对$i - 1$重复这些步骤直至所有顶点消元。由此得证$L$的逆序是${H}_{0}$的消元序，故${H}_{0}$确为无环。

We next show that ${H}_{0}$ has no disruptive trio with respect to $L$ . By way of contradiction,assume ${H}_{0}$ has a disruptive trio ${v}_{i},{v}_{j},{v}_{k}$ with $i < j < k$ . Then,by definition,in ${H}_{0}$ the vertices ${v}_{i}$ and ${v}_{j}$ are both neighbors of ${v}_{k}$ ,but ${v}_{i}$ and ${v}_{j}$ are not neighbors. In the construction of ${H}_{0}$ from Definition 4, no edge containing ${v}_{k}$ is added after introducing ${e}_{k}$ ,and ${e}_{k}$ does not introduce new neighbors to ${v}_{k}$ . We conclude that ${v}_{i}$ and ${v}_{j}$ were already neighbors of ${v}_{k}$ right before introducing ${e}_{k}$ . But then $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$ ,and thus ${v}_{i}$ and ${v}_{j}$ are neighbors in ${H}_{0}$ . This contradicts the assumption that ${v}_{i},{v}_{j},{v}_{k}$ forms a disruptive trio.

接着证明${H}_{0}$关于$L$不存在破坏性三元组。反设${H}_{0}$存在破坏性三元组${v}_{i},{v}_{j},{v}_{k}$含$i < j < k$。根据定义，在${H}_{0}$中顶点${v}_{i}$与${v}_{j}$都是${v}_{k}$的邻点但彼此不相邻。根据定义4的构造，引入${e}_{k}$后未添加含${v}_{k}$的边，且${e}_{k}$未给${v}_{k}$引入新邻点。故在引入${e}_{k}$前${v}_{i}$与${v}_{j}$已是${v}_{k}$的邻点。但此时$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$，即${v}_{i}$与${v}_{j}$在${H}_{0}$中相邻，这与${v}_{i},{v}_{j},{v}_{k}$构成破坏性三元组的假设矛盾。

It will be useful in the remainder of this section to have a non-iterative definition of disruption-free decompositions. Let ${S}_{i}$ be the vertices in the connected component of ${v}_{i}$ in $Q\left\lbrack  \left\{  {{v}_{i},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right\}  \right\rbrack$ . In particular,we have that ${v}_{i} \in  {S}_{i}$ .

本节后续内容将采用无破坏分解的非迭代定义。设${S}_{i}$为$Q\left\lbrack  \left\{  {{v}_{i},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right\}  \right\rbrack$中${v}_{i}$所在连通分量的顶点集，特别地有${v}_{i} \in  {S}_{i}$。

LEMMA 7. The edges introduced in Definition 4 are ${e}_{i} = \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right) }\right\}$ for $i \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,\ell \}$ .

引理7. 定义4中引入的边对$i \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,\ell \}$而言是${e}_{i} = \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right) }\right\}$。

Example 8. Let us illustrate Lemma 7 with the query of Example 5: For the variable ${v}_{5}$ ,we have ${S}_{5} = \left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}$ . Consequently, ${e}_{5} = \left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}   \cup  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{5}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ ,which is exactly the edge that is added for ${v}_{5}$ . The case for ${v}_{4}$ is analogous. For ${v}_{3}$ ,we have ${S}_{3} = \left\{  {{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ . Consequently, ${e}_{3} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {v}_{3}\right\}   \cup  {N}_{Q}\left( \left\{  {{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}  \right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$ . We have ${S}_{2} = \left\{  {{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ ,so we add the edge ${e}_{2} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ . For ${v}_{1}$ ,we add again the singleton edge $\left\{  {v}_{1}\right\}$ .

示例8. 让我们用例5的查询来说明引理7：对于变量${v}_{5}$，我们有${S}_{5} = \left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}$。因此，${e}_{5} = \left\{  {v}_{5}\right\}   \cup  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{5}\right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$，这正是为${v}_{5}$添加的边。${v}_{4}$的情况类似。对于${v}_{3}$，我们有${S}_{3} = \left\{  {{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$。因此，${e}_{3} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {v}_{3}\right\}   \cup  {N}_{Q}\left( \left\{  {{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}  \right)  = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$。由于${S}_{2} = \left\{  {{v}_{2},{v}_{3},{v}_{4},{v}_{5}}\right\}$，所以我们添加边${e}_{2} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$。对于${v}_{1}$，我们再次添加单例边$\left\{  {v}_{1}\right\}$。

Proof of Lemma 7. Let ${e}_{i}^{\prime } = \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right) }\right\}$ . We prove that ${e}_{i} = {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ by induction on decreasing $i$ . The claim holds by definition for ${e}_{\ell }$ ,since ${S}_{\ell } = \left\{  {v}_{\ell }\right\}$ ,and ${H}_{\ell }$ is the hypergraph of $Q$ ,and so ${N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)  = {N}_{{H}_{\ell }}\left( {v}_{\ell }\right)$ .

引理7的证明。设${e}_{i}^{\prime } = \left\{  {v}_{i}\right\}   \cup  \left\{  {{v}_{j} \mid  j < i\text{and}{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right) }\right\}$。我们通过对$i$递减归纳来证明${e}_{i} = {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。根据定义，该断言对${e}_{\ell }$成立，因为${S}_{\ell } = \left\{  {v}_{\ell }\right\}$，且${H}_{\ell }$是$Q$的超图，因此${N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)  = {N}_{{H}_{\ell }}\left( {v}_{\ell }\right)$。

For the induction step,we first show ${e}_{i} \subseteq  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ . Let ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$ . If $j = i$ ,then we know that ${v}_{j} = {v}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ by definition. Otherwise, $j < i$ and ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$ . If ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$ ,then we have that ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$ , because ${v}_{i} \in  {S}_{i}$ ,and so ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ . Otherwise,the reason ${v}_{j}$ is a neighbor of ${v}_{i}$ in ${H}_{i}$ is because there is some $k > i > j$ such that $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$ . From the induction hypothesis,we have that ${e}_{k}^{\prime } = {e}_{k}$ ,and it follows that ${v}_{i},{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$ . Since ${v}_{i} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$ ,we have that ${S}_{k} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$ . It follows that ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)  \subseteq  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$ and thus ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ . Since in all cases ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ ,we get that ${e}_{i} \subseteq  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ .

在归纳步骤中，我们首先证明${e}_{i} \subseteq  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。设${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$。若$j = i$，则根据定义可知${v}_{j} = {v}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。否则，$j < i$且${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$。若${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$，则因${v}_{i} \in  {S}_{i}$可得${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$，故${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。否则，${v}_{j}$成为${H}_{i}$中${v}_{i}$邻点的原因在于存在某个$k > i > j$使得$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$。根据归纳假设可得${e}_{k}^{\prime } = {e}_{k}$，继而推出${v}_{i},{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$。由于${v}_{i} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$，我们得出${S}_{k} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$。由此可得${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)  \subseteq  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$，因此${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。在所有情况下均有${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$，故证得${e}_{i} \subseteq  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。

It remains to show that ${e}_{i}^{\prime } \subseteq  {e}_{i}$ . To this end,let ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$ . If $j = i$ ,then we know that ${v}_{j} = {v}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}$ by definition. Otherwise, $j < i$ and ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$ . If ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$ ,then also ${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$ ,and so ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$ . Otherwise,if we remove ${v}_{i}$ from ${S}_{i}$ ,then it is split into connected components,one of them containing a neighbor of ${v}_{j}$ . Consider the smallest variable ${v}_{k}$ in such a connected component. Then,this connected component is ${S}_{k}$ ,and we have that ${v}_{i},{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$ where $k > i > j$ . It follows that $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}^{\prime }$ ,and from the induction hypothesis,we get that $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$ . Thus, ${v}_{j}$ is a neighbor of ${v}_{i}$ when introducing ${e}_{i}$ ,and so ${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$ . Consequently, ${e}_{i}^{\prime } \subseteq  {e}_{i}$ .

仍需证明${e}_{i}^{\prime } \subseteq  {e}_{i}$。为此，设${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}^{\prime }$。若$j = i$，则根据定义可知${v}_{j} = {v}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}$。否则，$j < i$且${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{i}\right)$。若${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$，则亦有${v}_{j} \in  {N}_{{H}_{i}}\left( {v}_{i}\right)$，因此${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$。否则，若从${S}_{i}$中移除${v}_{i}$，则其分裂为若干连通分量，其中包含${v}_{j}$的某个邻点。考虑该连通分量中的最小变量${v}_{k}$，则该连通分量即为${S}_{k}$，且存在$k > i > j$使得${v}_{i},{v}_{j} \in  {N}_{Q}\left( {S}_{k}\right)$。由此可得$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}^{\prime }$，根据归纳假设推出$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{k}$。因此，在引入${e}_{i}$时${v}_{j}$成为${v}_{i}$的邻点，故${v}_{j} \in  {e}_{i}$。最终证得${e}_{i}^{\prime } \subseteq  {e}_{i}$。

### 3.2 The Direct-access Algorithm

### 3.2 直接访问算法

The idea of our direct access algorithm is to use the disruption-free decomposition. More precisely, we define a new query ${Q}^{\prime }$ from $Q$ such that the hypergraph of ${Q}^{\prime }$ is the disruption-free decomposition of $Q$ . Then,given an input database $D$ for $Q$ ,we compute a new database ${D}^{\prime }$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ such that $Q\left( D\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ . Since ${Q}^{\prime }$ has no disruptive trio,we can then use the algorithm from Reference [18] on ${Q}^{\prime }$ and ${D}^{\prime }$ to allow direct access. A key component to making this approach efficient is the efficient computation of ${D}^{\prime }$ . To measure its complexity,we introduce the following notion:

我们直接访问算法的核心思想是采用无干扰分解技术。具体而言，我们通过$Q$定义新查询${Q}^{\prime }$，使得${Q}^{\prime }$的超图成为$Q$的无干扰分解。当给定$Q$的输入数据库$D$时，我们为${Q}^{\prime }$计算新数据库${D}^{\prime }$以满足$Q\left( D\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$。由于${Q}^{\prime }$不存在干扰三元组，可借助文献[18]的算法对${Q}^{\prime }$和${D}^{\prime }$实现直接访问。该方法效率的关键在于高效计算${D}^{\prime }$，为此我们引入以下复杂度度量指标：

Definition 9 (Incompatibility Number). Let $Q$ be a join query with hypergraph $H$ and let $L =$ $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$ be an ordering of its variables. Let ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ with $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ be the fractional edge cover number of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$ ,where ${e}_{i}$ is as defined in Definition 4. We call $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i = 1,\ldots ,\ell }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ the incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ .

定义9（冲突数）。设$Q$为具有超图$H$的连接查询，$L =$$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$为其变量序。当$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$时，令${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$表示$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$的分数边覆盖数（其中${e}_{i}$如定义4所述），则称$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i = 1,\ldots ,\ell }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$为查询$Q$与序$L$的冲突数。

Note that we assume that queries have at least one atom, so the incompatibility number of any query and any order is at least 1 . The incompatibility number can also be seen as the fractional width of the disruption-free decomposition, and we can show that this decomposition has the minimum fractional width out of all decompositions with the properties we need; see Section 3.3 for details. We now show that the incompatibility number lets us state an upper bound for the computation of a database ${D}^{\prime }$ with the properties claimed above.

注意我们假定查询至少含一个原子，故任何查询与变量序的冲突数至少为1。该数值亦可视为无干扰分解的分数宽度，且可证明在所有满足需求的分解中，此分解具有最小分数宽度（详见第3.3节）。接下来将展示如何利用冲突数界定满足前述特性的数据库${D}^{\prime }$的计算复杂度上界。

THEOREM 10. Given a join query $Q$ and an ordering $L$ of its variables with incompatibility number $\iota$ ,lexicographic direct access with respect to $L$ can be achieved with $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$ preprocessing and logarithmic access time.

定理10. 给定连接查询$Q$及其变量序$L$（冲突数为$\iota$），针对$L$的字典序直接访问可在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$预处理时间与对数级访问时间内实现。

Proof. We construct ${Q}^{\prime }$ by adding for every edge ${e}_{i}$ an atom ${R}_{i}$ whose variables are those in ${e}_{i}$ . We compute a relation ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ as follows: Let $\mu$ be a fractional edge cover of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$ of weight ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \iota$ . Let ${\bar{e}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{e}}_{r}$ be the edges of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$ that have positive weight in $\mu$ . For every ${\bar{e}}_{j}$ there is an edge ${e}_{j}^{\prime }$ of $H$ such that ${\bar{e}}_{j} = {e}_{j}^{\prime } \cap  {e}_{i}$ . Let ${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\bar{X}}_{j}\right)$ be the projection to ${e}_{i}$ of an atom corresponding to ${e}_{j}^{\prime }$ . Let ${D}^{ * }$ be the extension of $D$ that contains the corresponding projected relations for the ${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\bar{X}}_{j}\right)$ . We set ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {Q}_{i}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$ where ${Q}_{i}\left( {e}_{i}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bar{R}}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\bar{R}}_{r}\left( {X}_{r}\right)$ . Then,clearly for all tuples $\overrightarrow{t}$ in $Q\left( D\right)$ ,the tuple we get by projecting $\overrightarrow{t}$ to ${e}_{i}$ lies in ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ . As a consequence,we can construct ${D}^{\prime }$ by adding all relations ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ to $D$ to get $Q\left( D\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ . Moreover,the hypergraph of ${Q}^{\prime }$ is the disruption-free decomposition of the hypergraph of $Q$ ,so we can apply the algorithm from Reference [18] for direct access.

证明。我们通过为每条边${e}_{i}$添加一个变量与${e}_{i}$相同的原子${R}_{i}$来构造${Q}^{\prime }$。按如下方式计算关系${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$：设$\mu$是权重为${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \iota$的$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$的分数边覆盖。令${\bar{e}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{e}}_{r}$为$\mu$中权重为正的$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$的边。对于每个${\bar{e}}_{j}$，存在$H$的边${e}_{j}^{\prime }$使得${\bar{e}}_{j} = {e}_{j}^{\prime } \cap  {e}_{i}$。设${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\bar{X}}_{j}\right)$为对应于${e}_{j}^{\prime }$的原子在${e}_{i}$上的投影。令${D}^{ * }$为$D$的扩展，包含${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\bar{X}}_{j}\right)$对应的投影关系。我们设定${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {Q}_{i}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$其中${Q}_{i}\left( {e}_{i}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bar{R}}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\bar{R}}_{r}\left( {X}_{r}\right)$。显然，对于$Q\left( D\right)$中的所有元组$\overrightarrow{t}$，将$\overrightarrow{t}$投影到${e}_{i}$得到的元组属于${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$。因此，我们可以通过将所有${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$关系加入$D$来构造${D}^{\prime }$得到$Q\left( D\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$。此外，${Q}^{\prime }$的超图是$Q$超图的无干扰分解，故可应用文献[18]的算法实现直接访问。

It remains to show that all ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ can be constructed in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$ . To this end,consider the join query ${Q}_{i}\left( {e}_{i}\right)$ from before. By definition,its variable set is ${e}_{i}$ ,and $\mu$ is a fractional edge cover of weight ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \iota$ . Thus,we can use a worst-case optimal join algorithm from Theorem 2 to compute ${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$ .

还需证明所有${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$可在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$时间内构造。为此考虑之前的连接查询${Q}_{i}\left( {e}_{i}\right)$。根据定义，其变量集为${e}_{i}$，且$\mu$是权重${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  \iota$的分数边覆盖。因此可采用定理2中的最坏情况最优连接算法，在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$时间内计算出${R}_{i}^{{D}^{\prime }}$。

### 3.3 Disruption-free Decompositions and Fractional Hypertree Width

### 3.3 无干扰分解与分数超树宽度

In this section, we will relate disruption-free decompositions to fractional hypertree decompositions and the incompatibility number to fractional width.

本节将探讨无干扰分解与分数超树分解的关系，以及不兼容数与分数宽度之间的关联。

Let $H = \left( {V,E}\right)$ be a hypergraph. A hypertree decomposition $\mathcal{D}$ of $H$ is defined to be an acyclic hypergraph(V,B)such that for every edge $e \in  E$ there is a $b \in  B$ with $e \subseteq  b \cdot  {}^{2}$ The sets $b \in  B$ are called the bags of the decomposition. The fractional width of $\mathcal{D}$ is defined as $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{b \in  B}}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . The fractional hypertree width of $H$ is defined to be the minimal fractional width of any hypertree decomposition of $H\left\lbrack  {25}\right\rbrack$ .

设$H = \left( {V,E}\right)$为超图。超树分解$\mathcal{D}$的定义是一个无环超图(V,B)，使得对于每条边$e \in  E$都存在$b \in  B$满足$e \subseteq  b \cdot  {}^{2}$。集合$b \in  B$称为分解的包。分数宽度定义为$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{b \in  B}}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$。分数超树宽度定义为$H\left\lbrack  {25}\right\rbrack$所有超树分解中分数宽度的最小值。

Note that the fractional edge cover number is monotone in the sense that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {b}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ whenever $b \subseteq  {b}^{\prime }$ ,and so the fractional width of a hypertree decomposition is determined by its maximal edges with respect to set inclusion. Next, we observe that the incompatibility number is the fractional width of the disruption-free decomposition seen as a hypertree decomposition.

注意分数边覆盖数具有单调性：当$b \subseteq  {b}^{\prime }$时${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {b}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  }\right)$，因此超树分解的分数宽度由其关于集合包含的最大边决定。此外，我们观察到不兼容数正是无冲突分解被视为超树分解时的分数宽度。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{2}$ Hypertree decompositions appear in previous work under several names,such as generalized hypertree decompositions and fractional hypertree decompositions, depending on the way the bags get covered. Usually, the definitions are more involved, as they also contain a tree structure with the bags as nodes and an edge cover of the bags. This is a simplified definition containing only what we need here.

${}^{2}$超树分解在先前研究中以多种名称出现，如广义超树分解和分数超树分解，具体取决于包的覆盖方式。通常定义更为复杂，包含以包为节点的树结构及包的边覆盖。此为简化定义，仅保留本文所需内容。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Proposition 11. Let $Q$ be a join query and $L$ be an ordering of its variables. The disruption-free decomposition of $Q$ and $L$ is a hypertree decomposition of $Q$ ,and its fractional width is the incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ .

命题11. 设$Q$为连接查询，$L$为其变量序。无冲突分解构成$Q$的超树分解，其分数宽度即为$Q$与$L$的不兼容数。

Proof. Consider an edge $e$ of the disruption-free decomposition ${H}_{0}$ . First,we claim that $e$ is contained in some edge ${e}_{i}$ from Definition 4. Let ${v}_{i}$ be the latest vertex (according to $L$ ) in an edge $e$ of ${H}_{0}$ . Then, $e$ could not have been introduced after the introduction of ${e}_{i}$ . Thus,either $e = {e}_{i}$ or $e$ existed right before the introduction of ${e}_{i}$ ,and then $e \subseteq  {e}_{i}$ by the definition of ${e}_{i}$ . Due to the monotonicity of the fractional edge cover number, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  e\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ,and this is bounded by the incompatibility number by its definition. We also have that $\iota  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{j}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ for some $j$ ,and thus $\iota$ is exactly the fractional width of ${H}_{0}$ .

证明. 考虑无冲突分解${H}_{0}$的边$e$。首先断言$e$包含于定义4中的某边${e}_{i}$。设${v}_{i}$为边$e$中（按序$L$）最晚顶点，则$e$不可能在引入${e}_{i}$后才出现。故在引入${e}_{i}$前，要么$e = {e}_{i}$存在，要么$e$已存在，此时根据${e}_{i}$定义有$e \subseteq  {e}_{i}$。由分数边覆盖数的单调性可得${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  e\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack  }\right)$，其值受不兼容数定义限制。又因存在$j$使$\iota  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{j}\right\rbrack  }\right)$，故$\iota$恰为${H}_{0}$的分数宽度。

ObsERVATION 12. Let $Q$ be a join query and $L$ be an ordering of its variables. Then,the incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ is at least the fractional hypertree width of $Q$ .

观察12 设$Q$为连接查询，$L$为其变量序。则$Q$与$L$的不兼容数不小于$Q$的分数超树宽度。

Of course there are other hypertree decompositions $\mathcal{D}$ of $Q$ that we could have used for a direct access algorithm. The only property that we need is that $\mathcal{D}$ has no disruptive trio for $L$ . If this is the case, then inspection of the proof of Theorem 10 shows that we get an alternative algorithm whose preprocessing depends on the fractional width of $\mathcal{D}$ . We will see next that this approach cannot yield a better running time than Theorem 10. To this end, we prove that any alternative decomposition contains the disruption-free decomposition in a way.

当然还存在其他可用于直接访问算法的超树分解$\mathcal{D}$。我们唯一需要的性质是$\mathcal{D}$对于$L$不存在破坏三元组。此时通过检验定理10的证明可知，我们将获得一个预处理阶段依赖于$\mathcal{D}$分数宽度的替代算法。下文将证明该方法的运行时复杂度不可能优于定理10。为此，我们将论证任何替代分解在某种意义上都包含无破坏分解。

LEMMA 13. Let $Q$ be a join query, $L$ be an ordering of its variables,and $\mathcal{D}$ be a hypertree decomposition of $Q$ without disruptive trios with respect to $L$ . Then,for every edge $e$ of the disruption-free decomposition of $Q$ ,there exists a bag $b$ of $\mathcal{D}$ such that $e \subseteq  b$ .

引理13 设$Q$为连接查询，$L$为其变量序，$\mathcal{D}$为$Q$相对于$L$无破坏三元组的超树分解。则对于$Q$无破坏分解的每条边$e$，必存在$\mathcal{D}$的某个包$b$使得$e \subseteq  b$成立。

Proof. Let $H$ be the hypergraph of $Q$ . Assume by way of contradiction that there is an edge of the disruption-free decomposition ${H}_{0}$ of $Q$ for $L$ that is not contained in any bag of $\mathcal{D}$ . Since $\mathcal{D}$ is a hypertree decomposition of $H$ ,for every edge $e$ of $H$ ,the decomposition $\mathcal{D}$ contains,by definition,a bag $b$ such that $e \subseteq  b$ . Thus,the edge of ${H}_{0}$ not contained in any bag of $\mathcal{D}$ must be of the form $P \cup  \{ v\}$ where $P$ consists of the preceding neighbors of $v$ at the moment of creation of the edge; here,when we say "preceding," we mean the neighbors that come before $v$ in $L$ . Consider the non-covered edge $e$ with the largest $v$ . We show next that every pair of vertices in $e$ appears in a common bag in $\mathcal{D}$ . It is known that acyclic hypergraphs are conformal [14]; that is,any set of pairwise neighbors must be contained in an edge. Thus, $\mathcal{D}$ has a bag containing $e$ ,which is a contradiction.

证明 设$H$为$Q$的超图。反设存在$Q$对于$L$的无破坏分解${H}_{0}$的某条边未被$\mathcal{D}$的任何包包含。由于$\mathcal{D}$是$H$的超树分解，根据定义，对于$H$的每条边$e$，分解$\mathcal{D}$必存在包$b$满足$e \subseteq  b$。因此未被任何包包含的边必形如$P \cup  \{ v\}$，其中$P$由创建该边时$v$的先前邻居组成（"先前"指在$L$中排序早于$v$的邻居）。考虑具有最大$v$的未覆盖边$e$。接下来证明$e$中每对顶点都出现在$\mathcal{D}$的某个公共包中。已知无环超图具有共形性[14]，即任何两两相邻的顶点集必被某条边包含。因此$\mathcal{D}$存在包含$e$的包，导致矛盾。

Since $e$ is the largest non-covered edge,we know that $\mathcal{D}$ contains,for all edges ${e}^{\prime }$ that were present at the moment of creation of $e$ ,a bag $b$ with ${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  b$ . Thus,for every vertex ${v}^{\prime }$ in $P$ ,there is a bag of $\mathcal{D}$ that contains both $v$ and ${v}^{\prime }$ . Now consider ${v}_{1},{v}_{2} \in  P$ . If ${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$ are not in a common bag ${b}^{\prime }$ of $\mathcal{D}$ ,then ${v}_{1},{v}_{2},v$ is a disruptive trio of $\mathcal{D}$ ,which is a contradiction to the conditions of the lemma. So, ${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$ must appear in a common bag. It follows that the vertices of $e$ are all pairwise neighbors in $\mathcal{D}$ ,as we wanted to show.

由于$e$是最大的未覆盖边，可知$\mathcal{D}$对于$e$创建时刻存在的所有边${e}^{\prime }$，都存在满足${e}^{\prime } \subseteq  b$的包$b$。因此对于$P$中每个顶点${v}^{\prime }$，$\mathcal{D}$都存在同时包含$v$和${v}^{\prime }$的包。现考察${v}_{1},{v}_{2} \in  P$。若${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$不在$\mathcal{D}$的公共包${b}^{\prime }$中，则${v}_{1},{v}_{2},v$构成$\mathcal{D}$的破坏三元组，与引理条件矛盾。故${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$必出现在公共包中。由此可得$e$的顶点在$\mathcal{D}$中两两相邻，得证。

Due to the monotonicity of the fractional edge cover number, we directly get that the disruption-free decomposition is optimal in the following sense:

根据分数边覆盖数的单调性，可直接推出无破坏分解在以下意义上是最优的：

Proposition 14. Let $Q$ be a join query and $L$ be an ordering of its variables. The disruption-free decomposition of $Q$ and $L$ has the minimal fractional width taken over all hypertree decompositions of $Q$ that have no disruptive trio with respect to $L$ .

命题14 设$Q$为连接查询，$L$为其变量序。在所有相对于$L$无破坏三元组的超树分解中，$Q$与$L$的无破坏分解具有最小的分数宽度。

Note that, in general, finding an optimal fractional hypertree decomposition is known to be hard [24]. However, in our case, decompositions are only useful if they eliminate disruptive trios. If we restrict the decompositions in that way, then it is much easier to find a good decomposition compared to the general case: Since the optimal decomposition is induced by the order, we get it in polynomial time using the procedure of Definition 4.

需要注意的是，一般而言，寻找最优分数超树分解已知是困难的[24]。但在我们的场景中，只有当分解能消除破坏性三元组时才有价值。若以此限制分解条件，相比一般情况就更易获得良好分解：由于最优分解由变量序诱导产生，通过定义4的流程可在多项式时间内获得。

## 4 Set-disjointness-based Hardness for Self-join-free Direct Access

## 4 基于集合互斥性的无自连接直接访问下界证明

In this section, we show lower bounds for lexicographically ranked direct access for all self-join-free queries and all variable orders. We first reduce general direct access queries to the special case of star queries, which we introduce in Section 4.1. Then, we show that lower bounds for direct access to star queries follow from lower bounds for $k$ -Set-Disjointness,see Section 4.3. Later,in Section 5,we prove a lower bound for $k$ -Set-Disjointness based on the Zero-Clique Conjecture, and in Section 6,we remove the assumption that $Q$ is self-join-free.

本节将证明所有无自连接查询和所有变量序的字典序直接访问下界。我们首先将通用直接访问查询归约为星型查询特例（见4.1节），随后证明星型查询直接访问下界源自$k$-集合互斥性下界（见4.3节）。第5节基于零团猜想证明$k$-集合互斥性下界，第6节将移除$Q$无自连接的假设。

### 4.1 From $k$ -Star Queries to Direct Access

### 4.1 从$k$-星型查询到直接访问

A crucial role in our reduction is played by the $k$ -star query ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ :

归约过程中的关键角色由$k$-星型查询${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$承担：

$$
{Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k},z}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},z}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {{x}_{k},z}\right) .
$$

We say that a variable order $L$ is bad for ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ if $z$ is the last variable in $L$ . We will also use the variant ${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$ of this query in which the variable $z$ is projected away:

若变量序$L$将$z$置于末位，则称该序对${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$是劣序。我们也会使用该查询的变体${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$，其中变量$z$被投影消除：

$$
{\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},z}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {{x}_{k},z}\right) .
$$

In the case that the query is acyclic, we can show that the incompatibility number is always an integer, and then the reduction is simpler. We thus start with this case as a warm-up for the general construction.

当查询为非循环时，可证明不兼容数恒为整数，此时归约过程更简单。我们以此作为通用构造的热身案例。

LEMMA 15. Let $Q$ be an acyclic self-join-free join query and $L$ be an ordering of its variables with incompatibility number $\iota$ . Then, $\iota$ is an integer,and if $Q$ has a lexicographic direct access algorithm according to $L$ with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  = \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then so has ${Q}_{i}^{ \star  }$ for a bad lexicographic order.

引理15. 设$Q$为无自连接的非循环连接查询，$L$为其变量序且不兼容数为$\iota$。则$\iota$为整数，若$Q$存在预处理时间$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  = \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$、访问时间$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$的字典序直接访问算法，则劣字典序${Q}_{i}^{ \star  }$亦存在。

Proof. We use the notation ${S}_{i}$ and ${e}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ as in Lemma 7. Recall that ${S}_{i}$ is the vertex set of the connected component of ${v}_{i}$ in $H\left\lbrack  \left\{  {{v}_{i},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right\}  \right\rbrack$ where $H$ is the hypergraph corresponding to $Q$ . Since every induced subhypergraph of an acyclic hypergraph is also acyclic (this is well known and follows easily from the definition of acyclicity by elimination orders),we have that $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$ is acyclic for all $i \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,\ell \}$ . Let $r$ be such that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = \iota  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)$ . In acyclic hypergraphs, the fractional edge cover number is an integer, and there is an independent set of this size [23], i.e., a subset of vertices such that every edge contains at most one vertex of this subset. Let $\left\{  {{u}_{1},\ldots ,{u}_{l}}\right\}$ be such an independent set in $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack$ .

证明. 沿用引理7中$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$的记号${S}_{i}$和${e}_{i}$。${S}_{i}$是超图$H\left\lbrack  \left\{  {{v}_{i},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right\}  \right\rbrack$中${v}_{i}$连通分量的顶点集，其中$H\left\lbrack  \left\{  {{v}_{i},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right\}  \right\rbrack$对应$Q$。由于非循环超图的每个导出子超图仍为非循环（此性质可由消除序定义直接推出），故对所有$i \in  \{ 1,\ldots ,\ell \}$，$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{i}\right\rbrack$均为非循环。取$r$满足${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = \iota  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)$。在非循环超图中，分数边覆盖数为整数，且存在该尺寸的独立集[23]——即顶点子集使得每条边至多包含该子集一个顶点。设$\left\{  {{u}_{1},\ldots ,{u}_{l}}\right\}$为$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack$中这样一个独立集。

We construct a database $D$ for $Q$ ,given an input database ${D}^{ \star  }$ for ${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$ . We first give roles to the variables of $Q$ that correspond to the variables of ${Q}_{i}^{ \star  }$ . First,we assign for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$ the role ${x}_{i}$ to every ${u}_{i}$ . We next add the role $z$ for every $v \in  {S}_{r}$ . Note that,when doing so,the variable ${v}_{r}$ ,the only one in both ${e}_{r}$ and ${S}_{r}$ ,may have both the role ${x}_{i}$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$ and the role $z$ ,while the other variables have at most one role.

我们为$Q$构建一个数据库$D$，给定一个用于${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$的输入数据库${D}^{ \star  }$。首先，我们为$Q$中对应于${Q}_{i}^{ \star  }$变量的变量分配角色。第一步，为每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$赋予每个${u}_{i}$角色${x}_{i}$。接着为每个$v \in  {S}_{r}$添加角色$z$。需注意，在此过程中，变量${v}_{r}$——唯一同时存在于<b11></b1>和${S}_{r}$中的变量——可能对某些$i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$同时具有角色${x}_{i}$和$z$，而其他变量至多拥有一个角色。

To define the relations of $D$ ,consider an atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{w}\right)$ of $Q$ . Since the variables with the roles of the form ${x}_{i}$ are an independent set,the roles of variables in this atom cannot contain ${x}_{i}$ and ${x}_{j}$ with $i \neq  j$ ,and so there is an atom ${R}_{j}\left( {{x}_{j},z}\right)$ in ${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$ whose variables contain all roles played by the variables $\overrightarrow{w}$ . We use ${R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$ to define ${R}^{D}$ . For every tuple $\left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$ ,we add a tuple $\overrightarrow{t}$ to ${R}^{D}$ as follows: For all ${w}_{i}$ in $\overrightarrow{w}$ ,if the variable ${w}_{i}$ has only the role ${x}_{j}$ ,then ${t}_{i} = {c}_{j}$ ; if it has only the role $z$ ,then ${t}_{i} = {c}_{z}$ ; if it has both roles ${x}_{j}$ and $z$ ,then ${t}_{i} = \left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)$ ; if it has no role,then ${t}_{i}$ is set to a constant $\bot$ . Note that $\left| D\right|  = O\left( \left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| \right)$ and that the construction only requires linear time.

为定义$D$的关系，考虑$Q$中的一个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{w}\right)$。由于具有${x}_{i}$形式角色的变量构成独立集，该原子中变量的角色不能同时包含${x}_{i}$和满足$i \neq  j$条件的${x}_{j}$，因此存在${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$中的原子${R}_{j}\left( {{x}_{j},z}\right)$，其变量包含$\overrightarrow{w}$所扮演的所有角色。我们使用${R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$来定义${R}^{D}$。对于每个元组$\left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$，按如下方式向${R}^{D}$添加元组$\overrightarrow{t}$：若$\overrightarrow{w}$中的变量${w}_{i}$仅具角色${x}_{j}$，则${t}_{i} = {c}_{j}$；若仅具角色$z$，则${t}_{i} = {c}_{z}$；若同时具有${x}_{j}$和$z$角色，则${t}_{i} = \left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)$；若无角色，则将${t}_{i}$设为常量$\bot$。注意$\left| D\right|  = O\left( \left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| \right)$，且该构造仅需线性时间。

The construction yields a bijection $\tau$ between $Q\left( D\right)$ and ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ as follows: If a variable with a single role $y$ is assigned a value $c$ in an answer $a \in  Q\left( D\right)$ ,then $\tau \left( a\right)$ assigned $y$ with $c$ ; similarly,if a variable with two roles ${x}_{j},z$ is assigned a value $\left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)$ ,then $\tau \left( a\right)$ assigned ${x}_{j}$ with ${c}_{j}$ and $z$ with ${c}_{z}$ . This bijection is well-defined,because all variables that have the role $z$ must take the same value on the corresponding coordinate by construction,since ${S}_{r}$ is connected in $H$ and the construction assigns different coordinates with the role $z$ in the same atom with the same value. Moreover,for every atom ${R}_{j}\left( {{x}_{j},z}\right)$ of ${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$ ,there is an atom of $Q$ containing (not necessarily different) variables ${v}_{x}$ and ${v}_{z}$ such that ${v}_{x}$ has the role ${x}_{j}$ and ${v}_{z}$ has the role $z$ . By construction,it then follows that the variables ${v}_{x}$ and ${v}_{z}$ take values that are consistent with the relation ${R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$ . This directly gives the desired bijection. Note that computing $\tau \left( a\right)$ given $a \in  Q\left( D\right)$ can be done in constant time.
