该构造在$Q\left( D\right)$与${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$之间建立如下双射$\tau$：若答案$a \in  Q\left( D\right)$中具有单一角色$y$的变量被赋值$c$，则$\tau \left( a\right)$将$y$赋值为$c$；类似地，若具有双重角色${x}_{j},z$的变量被赋值$\left( {{c}_{j},{c}_{z}}\right)$，则$\tau \left( a\right)$将${x}_{j}$赋值为${c}_{j}$，并将$z$赋值为${c}_{z}$。此双射是良定义的，因为根据构造，所有具有$z$角色的变量必须在对应坐标上取值相同——由于${S}_{r}$在$H$中连通，且该构造会将同一原子内具有$z$角色的不同坐标赋予相同值。此外，对于${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }$的每个原子${R}_{j}\left( {{x}_{j},z}\right)$，都存在$Q$的原子包含（不必相异）变量${v}_{x}$和${v}_{z}$，其中${v}_{x}$具有${x}_{j}$角色而${v}_{z}$具有$z$角色。根据构造原理，变量${v}_{x}$与${v}_{z}$的取值必然满足关系${R}_{j}^{{D}^{ \star  }}$，由此直接得到所需双射。注意根据$a \in  Q\left( D\right)$计算$\tau \left( a\right)$可在常数时间内完成。

Next,we claim that this construction yields a bad lexicographic order for ${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ . That is,ordering the solutions to $Q\left( D\right)$ by $L$ and then applying $\tau$ on each solution gives the solutions to ${Q}_{\iota }^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ sorted in a bad lexicographic order. This is the case,since the variables with the role $z$ appear in $L$ after all variables with a role of the form ${x}_{i}$ with some $i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$ ,with the exception of ${v}_{r}$ that may have two roles,in which case the construction assigns it the values corresponding to $z$ after those corresponding to ${x}_{i}$ .

接下来我们主张，该构造为${Q}_{t}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$产生了一个不良词典序。即通过$L$对$Q\left( D\right)$的解进行排序后，对每个解应用$\tau$，将得到按不良词典序排列的${Q}_{\iota }^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$的解集。这是因为具有$z$角色的变量在$L$中会出现在所有形式为${x}_{i}$（其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  \iota \right\rbrack$为某参数）的角色变量之后——除非是可能具有双重角色的${v}_{r}$，此时构造会先赋予其对应${x}_{i}$的值，再赋予对应$z$的值。

Using this construction,a lexicographic direct access for $Q$ and $L$ can be used for lexicographic direct access for ${Q}_{\iota }^{ \star  }$ and a bad lexicographic order.

利用该构造，针对$Q$和$L$的词典序直接访问可转化为对${Q}_{\iota }^{ \star  }$及不良词典序的直接访问。

Example 16. Let us illustrate the role assignment from the proof of Lemma 15 with the query of Example 5. The incompatibility number here is $\iota  = 3$ ,and it is witnessed by the edge ${e}_{3} =$ $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$ . An independent set of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{3}\right\rbrack$ of size 3 is $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$ . Thus,the variables ${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$ ,and ${v}_{3}$ are assigned the roles ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,and ${x}_{3}$ ,respectively. In addition, ${v}_{3},{v}_{4}$ ,and ${v}_{5}$ (these are the variables of ${S}_{3}$ ) are each assigned the role $z$ . Using this role assignment, ${Q}_{3}^{ \star  }$ is reduced to $Q$ .

示例16. 我们用例5的查询说明引理15证明中的角色分配。此处不兼容数为$\iota  = 3$，由边${e}_{3} =$$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$所印证。大小为3的$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{3}\right\rbrack$独立集为$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$，因此变量${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$和${v}_{3}$分别被分配角色${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$和${x}_{3}$。此外，${v}_{3},{v}_{4}$和${v}_{5}$（即${S}_{3}$的变量）均被分配角色$z$。通过此角色分配，${Q}_{3}^{ \star  }$被归约为$Q$。

In general, the incompatibility number may not be an integer; we will see such a case in Example 18. We will next show how we can generalize the construction from Lemma 15 to also handle this case.

一般而言，不兼容数可能为非整数；我们将在示例18中看到这种情况。接下来将展示如何将引理15的构造推广至此类情形。

LEMMA 17. Let $Q$ be a self-join-free join query and $L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$ be an ordering of its variables. Let $\iota$ be the incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ and assume $\iota  > 1$ . If there is an $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is a direct access algorithm for $Q$ and $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ ,then there is a $k \in  \mathbb{N},k > 2$ and ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$ such that for all ${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ there is a direct access algorithm for ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ with respect to a bad ordering with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ .

引理17. 设$Q$为无自连接的连接查询，$L = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$为其变量排序。令$\iota$表示$Q$与$L$的不兼容数，且假设$\iota  > 1$。若存在$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对于所有$\delta  > 0$，存在针对$Q$和$L$的直接访问算法，其预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$、访问时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$，则存在$k \in  \mathbb{N},k > 2$和${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$使得对于所有${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$，存在针对不良排序下${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$的直接访问算法，其预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$、访问时间为$O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$。

Proof. We use a reduction similar to that in the proof of Lemma 15, except we use a fractional independent set to handle the fractional incompatibility number. A fractional independent set in a hypergraph $H = \left( {V,E}\right)$ is a mapping $\phi  : V \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ such that for every $e \in  E$ ,we have that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  e}}\phi \left( v\right)  \leq  1$ . The weight of $\phi$ is defined to be $\phi \left( V\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  V}}\phi \left( v\right)$ . The fractional independent set number of $H$ is defined as ${\alpha }^{ * }\left( H\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\max }\limits_{\phi }\phi \left( V\right)$ where the maximum is taken over all fractional independent sets of $H$ . As already noted in Reference [25],using linear programming duality,we have for every hypergraph $H$ and every vertex set $S$ that ${\alpha }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ; see,e.g.,Reference [36, Chapter 82.2] for details. We use the same notation on join queries, with the meaning of applying it to the underlying hypergraph.

证明. 我们采用与引理15证明类似的归约方法，区别在于使用分数独立集处理分数不兼容数。超图$H = \left( {V,E}\right)$中的分数独立集是指映射$\phi  : V \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$满足：对于每个$e \in  E$，有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  e}}\phi \left( v\right)  \leq  1$。其权重定义为$\phi \left( V\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  V}}\phi \left( v\right)$。$H$的分数独立集数定义为${\alpha }^{ * }\left( H\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\max }\limits_{\phi }\phi \left( V\right)$（最大值取遍所有分数独立集）。如文献[25]所述，利用线性规划对偶性，对任意超图$H$和顶点集$S$均有${\alpha }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }\right)$；详见文献[36第82.2章]。我们将相同符号应用于连接查询，意指作用于底层超图。

Let $r$ be such that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = \iota  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)$ . Then,we know that there is a fractional independent set $\phi  : {e}_{r} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}\phi \left( v\right)  = \iota$ . Since $\phi$ is the solution of a linear program with integer coefficients and weights,all values $\phi \left( v\right)$ are rational numbers. Let $\lambda$ be the least common multiple of the denominators of $\left\{  {\phi \left( v\right)  \mid  v \in  {e}_{r}}\right\}$ . Now,define a new weight function ${\phi }^{\prime } : {e}_{r} \rightarrow  \{ 0,\ldots ,\lambda \}$ by ${\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\lambda \phi }\left( v\right)$ . Let $k \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\lambda \iota }$ ,and consider the query ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ .

设$r$满足${\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left\lbrack  {e}_{r}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = \iota  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( {H\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)$。根据已知条件，存在一个分数独立集$\phi  : {e}_{r} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$使得$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}\phi \left( v\right)  = \iota$。由于$\phi$是具有整数系数和权重的线性规划解，所有值$\phi \left( v\right)$均为有理数。令$\lambda$表示$\left\{  {\phi \left( v\right)  \mid  v \in  {e}_{r}}\right\}$分母的最小公倍数。现定义新权重函数${\phi }^{\prime } : {e}_{r} \rightarrow  \{ 0,\ldots ,\lambda \}$为${\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\lambda \phi }\left( v\right)$。设$k \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\lambda \iota }$，并考虑查询${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$。

As in the proof of Lemma 15,we again assign variables of $Q$ with roles that correspond to the variables of ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ . The difference in this proof is that now every variable may play several roles of the form ${x}_{i}$ . Indeed,every $v \in  {e}_{r}$ takes ${\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)$ roles out of the variables ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$ . More specifically, let $\left\{  {{u}_{1},\ldots ,{u}_{m}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{r}$ be the variables that ${\phi }^{\prime }$ assigns a non-zero value. Then,for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ , ${u}_{i}$ is assigned the roles $\left\{  {{x}_{j} \mid  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t < i}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{t}\right)  < j \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \leq  i}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{t}\right) }\right\}$ . Note that the number of roles we distributed so far is indeed $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \lambda \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}{\phi }_{v} = {\lambda \iota } = k$ . We next add the role $z$ for every $v \in  {S}_{r}$ . Note that,when doing so,the variable ${v}_{r}$ ,the only one in both ${e}_{r}$ and ${S}_{r}$ ,may have both roles of the form ${x}_{i}$ and the role $z$ . For an illustration of this role assignment,see Example 18.

如引理15的证明所示，我们再次将$Q$的变量分配对应于${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$变量的角色。本证明的不同之处在于，现在每个变量可能承担多个${x}_{i}$形式的角色。实际上，每个$v \in  {e}_{r}$从变量${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$中获取${\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)$个角色。具体而言，设$\left\{  {{u}_{1},\ldots ,{u}_{m}}\right\}   \subseteq  {e}_{r}$为${\phi }^{\prime }$赋予非零值的变量，则对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$，${u}_{i}$被分配的角色为$\left\{  {{x}_{j} \mid  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t < i}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{t}\right)  < j \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \leq  i}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {u}_{t}\right) }\right\}$。需注意当前分配的角色总数恰为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( v\right)  = \lambda \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  {e}_{r}}}{\phi }_{v} = {\lambda \iota } = k$。接下来，我们为每个$v \in  {S}_{r}$添加角色$z$。值得注意的是，在此过程中，变量${v}_{r}$——即同时存在于${e}_{r}$和${S}_{r}$中的唯一变量——可能同时具有${x}_{i}$形式的角色和$z$角色。该角色分配示例可参见例18。

We now construct a database $D$ for $Q$ given an input database ${D}^{ \star  }$ for ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ . For every variable $y$ of ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ ,denote by ${\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( y\right)$ its active domain in ${D}^{ \star  }$ . We fix all variables of $Q$ that have no role to a constant $\bot$ ,and we will ignore these variables in the rest of the proof,so we only need to consider variables in ${e}_{r} \cup  {S}_{r}$ . For each variable $v \in  {e}_{r} \cup  {S}_{r}$ ,we define the domain as follows: Let ${y}_{1},\ldots ,{y}_{s}$ be the roles of $v$ given in the order defined by $L$ . Then,the domain of $v$ is $\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$ ${\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( {y}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( {y}_{s}\right)$ . We order $\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)$ lexicographically. To define the relations of $D$ ,let ${x}_{{j}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{j}_{t}}$ be the roles of the form ${x}_{i}$ played by variables in $R\left( {{v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}}\right)$ . Then,we compute the sub-join ${Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ where ${Q}_{J}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{j}_{t}},z}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{{j}_{1}}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{1}},z}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{{j}_{t}}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{t}},z}\right)$ . Note that there is a function $v : {Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {v}_{{i}_{1}}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {v}_{{i}_{s}}\right)$ that consists of "packing" the values for the different variables of ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ into the variables of $Q$ according to the roles in the following sense: For every tuple $\overrightarrow{t}$ ,the function $v$ maps each variable ${v}_{i}$ with role set $\mathcal{R}$ to the tuple we get from $\overrightarrow{t}$ by deleting the coordinates not in $\mathcal{R}$ . The relation ${R}^{D}$ is then simply $v\left( {{Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right) }\right)$ .

现针对给定输入数据库${D}^{ \star  }$（用于${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$）构建数据库$D$（用于$Q$）。对于${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$的每个变量$y$，用${\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( y\right)$表示其在${D}^{ \star  }$中的活跃域。我们将$Q$中无作用的变量固定为常量$\bot$，后续证明将忽略这些变量，故只需考虑${e}_{r} \cup  {S}_{r}$中的变量。对每个变量$v \in  {e}_{r} \cup  {S}_{r}$，其域定义如下：令${y}_{1},\ldots ,{y}_{s}$表示按$L$定义顺序排列的$v$角色集，则$v$的域为$\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$${\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( {y}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\operatorname{dom}}^{ \star  }\left( {y}_{s}\right)$。我们采用字典序排列$\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)$。为定义$D$的关系，设${x}_{{j}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{j}_{t}}$表示$R\left( {{v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}}\right)$中变量所扮演的${x}_{i}$形式角色，随后计算子连接${Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$（其中${Q}_{J}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{j}_{t}},z}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{{j}_{1}}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{1}},z}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{{j}_{t}}\left( {{x}_{{j}_{t}},z}\right)$）。需注意存在函数$v : {Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {v}_{{i}_{1}}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {v}_{{i}_{s}}\right)$，其作用是根据角色将${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$各变量的值"打包"至$Q$的变量中：对每个元组$\overrightarrow{t}$，函数$v$将具有角色集$\mathcal{R}$的变量${v}_{i}$映射为从$\overrightarrow{t}$中删除非$\mathcal{R}$坐标后所得的元组。关系${R}^{D}$即为$v\left( {{Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right) }\right)$。

The construction yields a bijection $\tau$ between $Q\left( D\right)$ and ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ : If a coordinate corresponding to a role $y$ is assigned a value $c$ in an answer $a \in  Q\left( D\right)$ ,then $\tau \left( a\right)$ assigns $c$ to $y$ . The same arguments given in the proof of Lemma 15 show that $\tau$ is well defined and is indeed a bijection and that this construction yields a bad lexicographic order. Note that the bijection $\tau$ can again be computed in constant time.

该构造在$Q\left( D\right)$与${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$之间产生双射$\tau$：若答案$a \in  Q\left( D\right)$中为角色$y$对应的坐标赋值$c$，则$\tau \left( a\right)$将$c$赋予$y$。引理15证明中的相同论证表明$\tau$是良定义的真正双射，且该构造会产生不良字典序。需注意此双射$\tau$仍可在常数时间内计算。

It remains to analyze the time complexity of the construction of $D$ . We show that the size of $D$ and the time required for its construction are in $O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$ . For every atom $A$ in $Q$ ,its variables in total have at most $\lambda$ roles of the form ${x}_{i}$ . To see this,let ${v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}$ be the variables of $A$ . Then, the overall number of non- $z$ -roles is $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1}}^{s}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {v}_{{i}_{j}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1}}^{s}{\lambda \phi }\left( {v}_{{i}_{j}}\right)  \leq  \lambda$ ,where the inequality comes from the fact that $\phi$ is a fractional independent set of $H$ and $\left\{  {{v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}}\right\}$ is an edge of $H$ . Hence, the join ${Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$ involves at most $\lambda$ atoms of ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ ,and it can be computed in time $O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$ .

仍需分析$D$构建的时间复杂度。我们证明$D$的规模及其构建所需时间处于$O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$量级。对于$Q$中的每个原子$A$，其变量总共最多具有$\lambda$个形式为${x}_{i}$的角色。为理解这一点，设${v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}$为$A$的变量集合，则非$z$角色的总数是$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1}}^{s}{\phi }^{\prime }\left( {v}_{{i}_{j}}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j = 1}}^{s}{\lambda \phi }\left( {v}_{{i}_{j}}\right)  \leq  \lambda$，该不等式成立的原因是$\phi$构成$H$的分数独立集，而$\left\{  {{v}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{v}_{{i}_{s}}}\right\}$是$H$的一条边。因此，连接操作${Q}_{J}\left( {D}^{ \star  }\right)$最多涉及$\lambda$个${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$原子，且可在$O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$时间内完成计算。

Assume that for some $\varepsilon  > 0$ and all $\delta  > 0$ there is a direct access algorithm for $Q$ and $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ . We set ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( {{\lambda \varepsilon },\lambda \left( {t - 1}\right) }\right)$ . Note that, indeed, ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$ ,since $\iota  > 1$ . Using the facts that $\left| D\right|  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$ and $k = {\lambda \iota }$ ,we get that the preprocessing is $O\left( {{\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda } + {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {{\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\lambda t} - \lambda \left( {t - 1}\right) } + {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\lambda t} - {\lambda \varepsilon }}}\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ . Then,given ${\delta }^{\prime }$ , we set $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\delta }^{\prime }/\lambda$ . We get that the access time is $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda \delta }\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ .

假设对于某个$\varepsilon  > 0$及所有$\delta  > 0$，存在针对$Q$和$L$的直接访问算法，其预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$，访问时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$。我们设定${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( {{\lambda \varepsilon },\lambda \left( {t - 1}\right) }\right)$。注意到由于$\iota  > 1$，确实满足${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$。基于$\left| D\right|  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda }\right)$和$k = {\lambda \iota }$这两个事实，可得出预处理时间为$O\left( {{\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda } + {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {{\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\lambda t} - \lambda \left( {t - 1}\right) } + {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\lambda t} - {\lambda \varepsilon }}}\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$。给定${\delta }^{\prime }$后，我们设置$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\delta }^{\prime }/\lambda$，此时访问时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{\lambda \delta }\right)  = O\left( {\left| {D}^{ \star  }\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$。

Example 18. Let us illustrate the role assignment from the proof of Lemma 17. Consider the query obtained from that of Example 5 by adding the atoms ${R}_{5}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{6}\left( {{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{7}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{3}}\right)$ . This query does not have disruptive trios,but it is cyclic. The edges ${e}_{i}$ introduced by the disruption-free decomposition are the same as in the previous example. The incompatibility number here is $\iota  = \frac{3}{2}$ . One witness for this incompatibility number is the edge ${e}_{3} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$ using the fractional independent set of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{3}\right\rbrack$ that assigns each of its vertices a weight of $\frac{1}{2}$ . As a result,we take $\lambda  = 2$ , and the variables ${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$ ,and ${v}_{3}$ are assigned the roles $\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ ,and $\left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6}}\right\}$ ,respectively. In addition, ${v}_{3},{v}_{4}$ ,and ${v}_{5}$ (these are the variables of ${S}_{3}$ ) are each assigned the role $z$ . Using this role assignment, ${Q}_{6}^{ \star  }$ is reduced to $Q$ . An alternative choice of witness for the incompatibility number is the edge ${e}_{5} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$ using the fractional independent set of $H\left\lbrack  {e}_{5}\right\rbrack$ that assigns each of its vertices a weight of $\frac{1}{2}$ . Here,the variables ${v}_{1},{v}_{3}$ ,and ${v}_{5}$ are assigned the roles $\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ , and $\left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6},z}\right\}$ ,respectively,while the variables ${v}_{2}$ and ${v}_{4}$ have no roles. This choice gives another way of reducing ${Q}_{6}^{ \star  }$ to $Q$ .

示例18. 让我们阐释引理17证明中的角色分配机制。考虑在示例5查询基础上添加原子${R}_{5}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{6}\left( {{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{7}\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{3}}\right)$后得到的新查询。该查询虽无破坏性三元组，但具有循环性。无破坏分解引入的边${e}_{i}$与前一示例相同。此处不兼容数为$\iota  = \frac{3}{2}$。该数值的一个见证是边${e}_{3} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},{v}_{3}}\right\}$，其采用分数独立集$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{3}\right\rbrack$——该集合为每个顶点分配$\frac{1}{2}$的权重。因此我们取$\lambda  = 2$，变量${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$、${v}_{3}$分别被分配角色$\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6}}\right\}$。此外，${v}_{3},{v}_{4}$与${v}_{5}$（这些是${S}_{3}$的变量）均被分配角色$z$。通过此角色分配，${Q}_{6}^{ \star  }$被简化为$Q$。不兼容数的另一种见证选择是边${e}_{5} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{3},{v}_{5}}\right\}$，其采用为每个顶点分配$\frac{1}{2}$权重的分数独立集$H\left\lbrack  {e}_{5}\right\rbrack$。此时变量${v}_{1},{v}_{3}$与${v}_{5}$分别获得角色$\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6},z}\right\}$，而变量${v}_{2}$和${v}_{4}$无角色分配。该选择提供了将${Q}_{6}^{ \star  }$简化为$Q$的另一途径。

### 4.2 From Projected Stars to Star Queries

### 4.2 从投影星型查询到星型查询

Proposition 19. Let $L$ be a bad order on ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k},z$ . If there is an algorithm that solves the direct access problem for ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k},z}\right)$ and $L$ with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then there is an algorithm for the testing problem for ${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and query time $O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$ .

命题19. 设$L$是${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k},z$上的不良序。若存在算法能以$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)$预处理时间和$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$访问时间解决${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k},z}\right)$和$L$的直接访问问题，则存在算法能以$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$查询时间解决${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$的测试问题。

Proof. If an answer $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$ is in ${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$ ,then answers of the form $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k},b}\right)$ (for any $b$ ) form a contiguous sequence in the set ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$ ordered according to the $L$ -lexicographic order, since the position of $z$ is the last variable in $L$ . Thus,a simple binary search using the direct access algorithm allows to test whether such a tuple $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k},b}\right)  \in  {Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$ exists using a logarithmic number of direct access calls.

证明。若答案$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$存在于${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$中，则形式为$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k},b}\right)$的答案（对于任意$b$）在集合${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$中构成连续序列（按$L$字典序排列），因为$z$的位置是$L$中的最后一个变量。因此，通过直接访问算法进行简单的二分搜索，可用对数次直接访问调用来验证是否存在这样的元组$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k},b}\right)  \in  {Q}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$。

### 4.3 From Set-Disjointness to Projected Stars

### 4.3 从集合非交性到投影星型查询

We observe (Lemma 22) that the testing problem for the projected star query ${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$ is equivalent to the following problem:

我们观察到（引理22），投影星型查询${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$的测试问题等价于以下问题：

Definition 20. In the $k$ -Set-Disjointness problem,we are given an instance $\mathcal{I}$ consisting of a universe $U$ and families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ of subsets of $U$ . We denote the sets in family ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$ by ${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$ . The task is to preprocess $\mathcal{I}$ into a data structure that can answer queries of the following form: Given indices ${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$ ,decide whether ${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} = \varnothing$ .

定义20。在$k$-集合非交性问题中，给定一个由全域$U$及其子集族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$组成的实例$\mathcal{I}$。用${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$表示族${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$中的集合。任务是将$\mathcal{I}$预处理为数据结构，能回答如下查询：给定索引${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$，判定是否${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} = \varnothing$。

We denote the input size by $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$ .

我们用$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$表示输入规模。

Example 21. Since we will see several problems later on that have the same type of inputs, let us take some time for an example. Fix the domain $U \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,\ldots ,5\}$ and say $k = 3$ . Let ${\mathcal{A}}_{1}$ consist of the sets ${S}_{1,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,3,5\}$ and ${S}_{1,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,2,4\}$ . Let ${\mathcal{A}}_{2}$ consist of ${S}_{2,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,4\} ,{S}_{2,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 2,4\}$ , and ${S}_{2,3} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,2,3,4,5\}$ . Finally,let ${\mathcal{A}}_{3}$ consist of ${S}_{3,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 3,4,5\}$ and ${S}_{3,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 4\}$ . The size of the resulting instance $\mathcal{I}$ is then $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = {19}$ .

示例21。鉴于后续将遇到多个具有同类输入的问题，现举例说明。固定域$U \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,\ldots ,5\}$并设$k = 3$。令${\mathcal{A}}_{1}$包含集合${S}_{1,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,3,5\}$和${S}_{1,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,2,4\}$，${\mathcal{A}}_{2}$包含${S}_{2,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,4\} ,{S}_{2,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 2,4\}$与${S}_{2,3} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 1,2,3,4,5\}$，${\mathcal{A}}_{3}$则包含${S}_{3,1} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 3,4,5\}$及${S}_{3,2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ 4\}$。最终实例$\mathcal{I}$的规模为$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = {19}$。

On input(2,3,2),we have to consider ${S}_{1,2} \cap  {S}_{2,3} \cap  {S}_{3,2} = \{ 4\}$ ,so an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness has to report that the queried set intersection is non-empty. For the input(1,1,1), we have that ${S}_{1,1} \cap  {S}_{2,1} \cap  {S}_{3,1} = \varnothing$ ,so the correct answer is that the queried set intersection is empty.

对于输入(2,3,2)，我们需要考虑${S}_{1,2} \cap  {S}_{2,3} \cap  {S}_{3,2} = \{ 4\}$，因此$k$-集合不相交性算法必须报告查询集合的交集非空。对于输入(1,1,1)，我们有${S}_{1,1} \cap  {S}_{2,1} \cap  {S}_{3,1} = \varnothing$，故正确答案是查询集合的交集为空。

LEMMA 22. If ${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$ has a testing algorithm with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  = \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then $k$ -Set-Disjointness has an algorithm with preprocessing time $p\left( {\parallel I\parallel }\right)$ and query time $r\left( {\parallel I\parallel }\right)$ .

引理22. 若${\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }$存在预处理时间$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  = \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$、访问时间$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$的测试算法，则$k$-集合不相交性问题存在预处理时间$p\left( {\parallel I\parallel }\right)$、查询时间$r\left( {\parallel I\parallel }\right)$的算法。

Proof. For each family ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$ ,consider the relation

证明. 对每个族${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$，考虑其关系

$$
{R}_{i}^{D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {j,v}\right)  \mid  v \in  {S}_{i,j}}\right\}  .
$$

Let $D$ be the resulting database,and note that $\left| D\right|  = \parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel$ and that $D$ is computable from $\mathcal{I}$ in linear time. The equivalence $v \in  {S}_{i,j} \Leftrightarrow  \left( {j,v}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D}$ implies the equivalence $v \in  {S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} \Leftrightarrow  D \vDash$ ${R}_{1}\left( {{j}_{1},v}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {{j}_{k},v}\right)$ ,and then for every query $\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$ of the $k$ -Set-Disjointness problem, we have that ${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} \neq  \varnothing$ if and only if $\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)  \in  {\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$ .

设$D$为最终生成的数据库，注意$\left| D\right|  = \parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel$且$D$可由$\mathcal{I}$线性时间计算得出。等价关系$v \in  {S}_{i,j} \Leftrightarrow  \left( {j,v}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D}$蕴含了$v \in  {S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} \Leftrightarrow  D \vDash$${R}_{1}\left( {{j}_{1},v}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {{j}_{k},v}\right)$，因此对于$k$-集合不相交性问题的每个查询$\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$，当且仅当$\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)  \in  {\bar{Q}}_{k}^{ \star  }\left( D\right)$时成立${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}} \neq  \varnothing$。

## 5 Hardness of Set-Disjointness

## 5 集合不相交性的困难性

In this section,we will show lower bounds for $k$ -Set-Disjointness,as defined in Definition 20. In combination with the reductions from the previous section, this will give us lower bounds for direct access in Section 7. The main result of this section is the following:

本节将展示定义20中$k$-集合不相交性的下界。结合前文的规约方法，这将为第7节的直接访问问题提供下界。本节主要结论如下：

THEOREM 23. If there is $k \in  \mathbb{N},k \geq  2$ and $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness that,given an instance $I$ ,answers queries in time $O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ after preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ ,then the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture is false.

定理23. 若存在$k \in  \mathbb{N},k \geq  2$和$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对所有$\delta  > 0$，存在$k$-集合不相交性算法能在预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$后，对给定实例$I$在时间$O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$内应答查询，则零-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想不成立。

Case $k = 2$

情形$k = 2$

Kopelowitz, Pettie, and Porat [29] established hardness of 2-Set-Disjointness under the 3-SUM Conjecture, and Vassilevska Williams and Xu [41] showed that the same hardness holds under the Zero-3-Clique Conjecture (and thus also under the APSP Conjecture).

Kopelowitz、Pettie和Porat[29]基于3-SUM猜想证明了2-集合不相交性的困难性，Vassilevska Williams和Xu[41]则证明该困难性在零-3-团猜想下同样成立（因而在APSP猜想下也成立）。

THEOREM 24 (COROLLARY 3.12 IN [41]). Assuming the Zero-3-Clique Conjecture (or the 3-SUM or APSP Conjecture),for any $0 < \gamma  < 1$ and any $\varepsilon  > 0$ ,on 2-Set-Disjointness instances $\mathcal{I}$ with $n$ sets, each of size $O\left( {n}^{1 - \gamma }\right)$ ,and universe size $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{2 - {2\gamma }}\right)$ ,the total time of preprocessing and $\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$ queries cannot be $O\left( {n}^{2 - \varepsilon }\right)$ .

定理24（文献[41]推论3.12）。在零三元团猜想（或3-SUM猜想、APSP猜想）成立的条件下，对于任意$0 < \gamma  < 1$和任意$\varepsilon  > 0$，在包含$n$个集合（每个集合大小为$O\left( {n}^{1 - \gamma }\right)$）、全域大小为$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{2 - {2\gamma }}\right)$的2-集合不交实例$\mathcal{I}$上，预处理与$\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$次查询的总时间不可能达到$O\left( {n}^{2 - \varepsilon }\right)$。

This implies Theorem 23 for $k = 2$ .

由此可得定理23在$k = 2$时的情形。

COROLLARY 25. If there is $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an algorithm for 2-Set-Disjointness that,given an instance $I$ ,answers queries in time $O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ after preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)$ ,then the Zero-3-Clique Conjecture is false (and thus also the 3-SUM and APSP Conjectures are false).

推论25。若存在$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对于所有$\delta  > 0$，都存在一个2-集合不交问题的算法能在预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)$后，对给定实例$I$以$O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$时间应答查询，则零三元团猜想不成立（进而3-SUM猜想与APSP猜想亦不成立）。

Proof. Assume that we can solve 2-Set-Disjointness with preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)$ and query time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ where $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \varepsilon /{10}$ . Set $\gamma  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1 - {4\delta }$ ,and consider an instance $\mathcal{I}$ consisting of $n$ sets,each of size $O\left( {n}^{1 - \gamma }\right)$ ,so the input size is $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = O\left( {n}^{2 - \gamma }\right)$ . Over $\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$ queries,the total query time is $O\left( {{n}^{1 + \gamma }\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)  \leq  O\left( {n}^{1 + \gamma  + {2\delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2 - {2\delta }}\right)$ . The preprocessing time on $\mathcal{I}$ is $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)  =$ $O\left( {n}^{\left( {2 - \gamma }\right) \left( {2 - {10\delta }}\right) }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{\left( {1 + {4\delta }}\right) \left( {2 - {10\delta }}\right) }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2 - {2\delta }}\right)$ . Hence,the total time of the preprocessing and $\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$ queries on $\mathcal{I}$ is $O\left( {n}^{2 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ for ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {2\delta }$ . This contradicts Theorem 24 (for parameters $\gamma ,{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ ).

证明。假设我们能用预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$（其中$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \varepsilon /{10}$）解决2-集合不交问题。设$\gamma  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1 - {4\delta }$，考虑一个包含$n$个集合（每个集合大小为$O\left( {n}^{1 - \gamma }\right)$）的实例$\mathcal{I}$，其输入规模为$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = O\left( {n}^{2 - \gamma }\right)$。经过$\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$次查询后，总查询时间为$O\left( {{n}^{1 + \gamma }\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)  \leq  O\left( {n}^{1 + \gamma  + {2\delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2 - {2\delta }}\right)$。实例$\mathcal{I}$的预处理时间为$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{2 - \varepsilon }}\right)  =$$O\left( {n}^{\left( {2 - \gamma }\right) \left( {2 - {10\delta }}\right) }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{\left( {1 + {4\delta }}\right) \left( {2 - {10\delta }}\right) }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2 - {2\delta }}\right)$。因此，对$\mathcal{I}$进行预处理及$\Theta \left( {n}^{1 + \gamma }\right)$次查询的总时间为$O\left( {n}^{2 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$（当${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {2\delta }$时），这与定理24（参数为$\gamma ,{\varepsilon }^{\prime }$时）矛盾。

In particular,by combining our previous reductions,for ${Q}_{2}^{ \star  }$ with a bad order $L$ there is no algorithm with polylogarithmic access time and preprocessing $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{2 - \varepsilon }\right)$ ,assuming any of the three conjectures mentioned above.

特别地，结合先前的归约结果，在采用不良序$L$的${Q}_{2}^{ \star  }$情况下，若上述三个猜想中任一成立，则不存在具有对数级别访问时间和$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{2 - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理的算法。

## Generalization to $k \geq  2$

## $k \geq  2$的推广

In the following,we show how to rule out preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ for $k$ -Set-Disjointness for any $k \geq  2$ . To this end,we make use of the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture. Our chain of reductions closely follows the proof of the case $k = 2$ by Vassilevska Williams and Xu [41] but also uses additional ideas required for the generalization to larger $k$ .

下文我们将展示如何排除任意$k \geq  2$下$k$-集合不相交问题的预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$。为此，我们利用零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想。我们的归约链严格遵循Vassilevska Williams和Xu[41]对$k = 2$情形的证明，同时引入了推广到更大$k$时所需的附加思路。

### 5.1 k-Set-Intersection

### 5.1 k-集合交问题

We start by proving a lower bound for the following problem of listing many elements in a set intersection.

我们首先证明下述集合交元素列举问题的下界。

Definition 26. In the $k$ -Set-Intersection problem,we are given an instance $\mathcal{I}$ consisting of a universe $U$ and families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ of subsets of $U$ . We denote the sets in family ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$ by ${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$ . The task is to preprocess $\mathcal{I}$ into a data structure that can answer queries of the following form: Given indices ${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$ and a number $T$ ,compute the set ${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$ if it has size at most $T$ ; if it has size more than $T$ ,then compute any $T$ elements of this set.

定义26. 在$k$-集合交问题中，给定由全集$U$及其子集族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$构成的实例$\mathcal{I}$。记${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$族中的集合为${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$。该问题要求将$\mathcal{I}$预处理为数据结构，使其能应答如下查询：给定索引${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$与数值$T$，若集合${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$元素数不超过$T$则计算该集合；若超过则计算其中任意$T$个元素。

We denote the number of sets by $n \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$ and the input size by $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$ . We call $\left| U\right|$ the universe size.

记集合数量为$n \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$，输入规模为$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$，称$\left| U\right|$为全集规模。

The main result of this section shows that $k$ -Set-Intersection is hard in the following sense. Later, we will use this result to show hardness for $k$ -Set-Disjointness.

本节主要结果表明$k$-集合交问题在以下意义上是难解的。后文将利用该结论证明$k$-集合不相交问题的难解性。

THEOREM 27. Assuming the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture,for every constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ there exists a constant $\delta  > 0$ such that no randomized algorithm solves $k$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets,universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ in preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and query time $O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$ .

定理27. 在零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想成立的前提下，对任意常数$\varepsilon  > 0$存在常数$\delta  > 0$，使得不存在随机算法能在预处理时间$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$内，求解$n$个集合、全集规模$\left| U\right|  = n$且$T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$的$k$-集合交问题。

We prove Theorem 27 in the rest of this section. The idea of this proof is as follows: We take a finite range that is big enough to contain all possible clique weights, and we spread the edge weights evenly over this range using random choices in a way that maintains the zero-cliques. We split this range into many intervals of small size,and we compute the set $S$ of all tuples of $k + 1$ intervals for which there exists a choice of an element from each interval such that all chosen elements sum up to zero. We have that for every zero-clique $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ ,there exists an interval tuple in $S$ such that the weight of the clique formed by $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ falls within the first interval, and the weights of the edges between ${v}_{k + 1}$ and the other vertices of the clique fall within the other $k$ intervals. In this way,all zero-cliques lie within a tuple of intervals in $S$ . For every interval tuple in $S$ ,we use the $k$ -Set-Intersection algorithm to find many vertex tuples that fall within this interval tuple. For each such candidate vertex tuple, we check whether it indeed forms a zero-clique. This algorithm finds a zero-clique if one exists with a constant success probability. If the algorithm does not detect a zero-clique, then it declares that there are none. Repeating this process with different random choices yields an algorithm that succeeds with high probability. We next describe our construction in more detail.

本节剩余部分将证明定理27。该证明的核心思路如下：选取一个足够大的有限范围以涵盖所有可能的团权重，通过随机选择将边权均匀分布在该范围内，同时保持零团性质。将该范围划分为多个微小区间，计算所有$k + 1$区间元组构成的集合$S$，其中存在从每个区间选取一个元素使所选元素之和为零的情况。对于每个零团$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$，必然存在$S$中的某个区间元组，使得$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$形成的团权重落在第一个区间，而${v}_{k + 1}$与团中其他顶点间边的权重分别落在其余$k$个区间。如此，所有零团都对应于$S$中的某个区间元组。对$S$中的每个区间元组，使用$k$-集合交算法寻找符合该元组的顶点元组，并验证其是否构成零团。该算法能以恒定成功率找到存在的零团，若未检测到则声明不存在。通过不同随机选择的重复执行，可获得高成功率的算法。下文将详细描述该构造过程。

Preparations. First note that finding a zero- $k$ -clique in a general graph is equivalent to finding a zero- $k$ -clique in a complete $k$ -partite graph. This can be shown using a reduction in which each vertex $v$ is duplicated $k$ times,giving vertices of the form(v,i)with $1 \leq  i \leq  k$ ,and each edge $\{ u,v\}$ is replaced by the ${k}^{2} - k$ edges $\{ \left( {u,i}\right) ,\left( {v,j}\right) \}$ for $1 \leq  i,j \leq  k$ and $i \neq  j$ ; then,to make this $k$ -partite graph complete,edges of very large weight are added where needed.

准备工作。首先注意到，在一般图中寻找零$k$-团等价于在完全$k$-部图中寻找零$k$-团。可通过归约证明：将每个顶点$v$复制$k$次得到形式为(v,i)的顶点（其中$1 \leq  i \leq  k$），并将每条边$\{ u,v\}$替换为${k}^{2} - k$条边$\{ \left( {u,i}\right) ,\left( {v,j}\right) \}$（其中$1 \leq  i,j \leq  k$且$i \neq  j$）；随后通过添加极大权边使其成为完全$k$-部图。

Observation 28 ([1]). If the Zero-k-Clique Conjecture is true, then it is also true restricted to complete $k$ -partite graphs.

观察28（引自[1]）。若零k团猜想成立，则该猜想在完全$k$-部图限制下亦成立。

Due to this observation,we can assume that we are given a complete $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -partite graph $G = \left( {V,E}\right)$ with $n$ vertices and with color classes ${V}_{1},\ldots ,{V}_{k + 1}$ and a weight function $w$ on the edges. We denote by $w\left( {u,v}\right)$ the weight of the edge from $u$ to $v$ ,and more generally by $w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$ $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  i < j \leq  \ell }}w\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right)$ the total weight of the clique $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$ .

基于此观察，可假定给定一个具有$n$个顶点的完全$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-部图$G = \left( {V,E}\right)$，其着色类为${V}_{1},\ldots ,{V}_{k + 1}$，边权函数为$w$。用$w\left( {u,v}\right)$表示从$u$到$v$的边权，更一般地用$w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  i < j \leq  \ell }}w\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{j}}\right)$表示团$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{\ell }}\right)$的总权重。

For our construction it will be convenient to assume that the edge weights lie in a finite field. Recall that,in the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique problem,we can assume that all weights are integers between $- {n}^{c}$ and ${n}^{c}$ for some constant $c$ . We first compute a prime number between ${10}{\left( k + 1\right) }^{2}{n}^{c}$ and ${100}{\left( k + 1\right) }^{2}{n}^{c}$ by a standard algorithm: Pick a random number in that interval,check whether it is a prime, and repeat if it is not. By the prime number theorem, a random number in that interval is a prime with probability $\Theta \left( {1/\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ . It follows that,in expected time $O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( n\right) }\right)$ ,we find a prime $p$ in that interval.

为便于构建，我们假设边权重位于有限域中。回顾零-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团问题，可设定所有权重为某个常数$c$下介于$- {n}^{c}$与${n}^{c}$之间的整数。首先通过标准算法计算${10}{\left( k + 1\right) }^{2}{n}^{c}$至${100}{\left( k + 1\right) }^{2}{n}^{c}$区间内的素数：随机选取该区间数值，检验其是否为素数，若非素数则重复操作。根据素数定理，该区间内随机数为素数的概率为$\Theta \left( {1/\log \left( n\right) }\right)$。由此可得，在期望时间$O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( n\right) }\right)$内我们可找到该区间内的素数$p$。

Having found the large prime $p$ ,we consider all edge weights as given in the finite field ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ . Note that $p$ is bigger than the sum of weights of any $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -clique in $G$ ,so the $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -cliques of weight 0 are the same over $\mathbb{Z}$ and ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ . Thus,in the remainder,we assume all arithmetic to be done over ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ .

确定大素数$p$后，我们将所有边权重视为有限域${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中的给定值。注意到$p$大于$G$中任何$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团的权重总和，因此权重为0的$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团在$\mathbb{Z}$与${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$上具有一致性。后续计算中我们默认所有算术运算均在${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$上执行。

In our analysis,it will be necessary to assume that the input weights are evenly distributed in ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ . This is generally of course not the case, so we have to distribute them more evenly. To this end, we use a hashing technique that redistributes the weights close to randomly while maintaining the zero-cliques. Concretely,we define a new weight function ${w}^{\prime } : E \rightarrow  {\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ by choosing independently and uniformly at random from ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ :

分析过程中需假设输入权重在${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$内均匀分布。实际情况通常并非如此，因此我们需要使其分布更均匀。为此采用哈希技术对权重进行近随机重分布，同时保持零团特性。具体而言，通过从${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中独立均匀随机选取以下元素来定义新权重函数${w}^{\prime } : E \rightarrow  {\mathbb{F}}_{p}$：

- one value $x$ ,

- 单值$x$，

- for all $v \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ and all $j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ a value ${y}_{v}^{j}$ .

- 对所有$v \in  {V}_{k + 1}$及$j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$生成对应值${y}_{v}^{j}$。

We define a new weight function ${w}^{\prime }$ by setting for every $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  {k + 1}\right\rbrack$ with $i \neq  j$ and every $v \in  {V}_{i}$ and $u \in  {V}_{j}$ :

通过为每个满足$i \neq  j$的$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  {k + 1}\right\rbrack$及每对$v \in  {V}_{i}$与$u \in  {V}_{j}$设定以下关系，定义新权重函数${w}^{\prime }$：

$$
{w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)  = x \cdot  w\left( {v,u}\right)  + \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} {y}_{u}^{1}, & \text{ if }i = 1,j = k + 1 \\  {y}_{u}^{i} - {y}_{u}^{i - 1}, & \text{ if }2 \leq  i < k,j = k + 1 \\   - {y}_{u}^{k - 1}, & \text{ if }i = k,j = k + 1 \\  0, & \text{ otherwise. } \end{array}\right.  \tag{1}
$$

Note that in every $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -clique $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ the sum ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ contains every term ${y}_{{v}_{k + 1}}^{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ once positively and once negatively,so for the overall weight,we have ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  = x \cdot  w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ . In particular,every zero-clique according to $w$ is also a zero-clique according to ${w}^{\prime }$ ,and we continue the construction with the weight function ${w}^{\prime }$ .

注意在任意$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$中，总和${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$对应的${y}_{{v}_{k + 1}}^{i}$项均呈现一次正一次负，故整体权重满足${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  = x \cdot  w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$。特别地，根据$w$定义的零团同样适用于${w}^{\prime }$的定义，后续构造将沿用权重函数${w}^{\prime }$。

We next split ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ into ${n}^{\rho }$ disjoint intervals of size $\left\lceil  {p/{n}^{\rho }}\right\rceil$ or $\left\lfloor  {p/{n}^{\rho }}\right\rfloor$ ,where $\rho$ is a constant that we will choose later such that $p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$ . In the following, ${I}_{i}$ always denotes an interval resulting from this splitting of ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ . We denote by $S$ the set of all tuples $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ of intervals in ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ such that $0 \in  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{k}{I}_{i}$ . Note that there are only $O\left( {n}^{\rho k}\right)$ tuples in $S$ ,and they can be enumerated efficiently: Given ${I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$ ,the set $- \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{{k - 1}}{I}_{i}$ is covered by $\Theta \left( k\right)  = \Theta \left( 1\right)$ intervals ${I}_{k}$ ,and these intervals can be computed efficiently.

接下来，我们将${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$分割为${n}^{\rho }$个互不相交的区间，每个区间大小为$\left\lceil  {p/{n}^{\rho }}\right\rceil$或$\left\lfloor  {p/{n}^{\rho }}\right\rfloor$，其中$\rho$是一个稍后将选择的常数，以满足$p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$的条件。在下文中，${I}_{i}$始终表示由${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$分割得到的区间。我们用$S$表示${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中所有满足$0 \in  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{k}{I}_{i}$的区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$的集合。注意，$S$中仅有$O\left( {n}^{\rho k}\right)$个元组，且可以高效枚举：给定${I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$，集合$- \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{{k - 1}}{I}_{i}$被$\Theta \left( k\right)  = \Theta \left( 1\right)$个区间${I}_{k}$覆盖，这些区间可以高效计算。

For any clique $C = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ there is a unique tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ of intervals such that

对于任何团$C = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$，存在唯一的区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$，使得

$$
\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}\text{ and }{w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}. \tag{2}
$$

We call $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ the weight interval tuple of $C$ . If $C$ is a zero-clique,then its weight interval tuple must be in $S$ .

我们称$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$为$C$的权重区间元组。若$C$是零团，则其权重区间元组必在$S$中。

The Reduction. With these preparations, we are now ready to present our reduction. For every weight interval tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$ ,we construct an instance of $k$ -Set-Intersection as follows: For any $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,we construct

归约过程。经过上述准备，我们现在可以展示归约方法。对于每个权重区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$，我们按如下方式构建一个$k$-集合交实例：对于任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，我们构造

$$
{\mathcal{A}}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {{S}_{i,v} \mid  v \in  {V}_{i}}\right\}  \text{ where }{S}_{i,v} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {u \in  {V}_{k + 1} \mid  {w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}}\right\}  .
$$

Note for every tuple $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots {V}_{k}$ (which plays the role of ${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$ ) the equivalence

注意，对于每个元组$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots {V}_{k}$（其作用相当于${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$），存在等价关系

$$
u \in  {S}_{1,{v}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{v}_{k}} \Leftrightarrow  \forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}.
$$

Moreover, we consider the set of queries

此外，我们考虑查询集

$$
Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k} \mid  {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}}\right\}  .
$$

We first run the preprocessing of $k$ -Set-Intersection on the instance $\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$ and then query each $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$ with parameter $T \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\lceil  {{100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right\rceil$ (so,we compute the entire set ${S}_{1,{v}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{v}_{k}}$ if it has at most $T$ elements,and,otherwise,we compute $T$ elements of this set). For each answer ${v}_{k + 1}$ returned by some query $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$ ,we check whether $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ is a zero-clique with respect to $w$ ,and if it is,then we return this zero-clique.

我们首先在实例$\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$上运行$k$-集合交的预处理，然后以参数$T \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\lceil  {{100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right\rceil$查询每个$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$（因此，若集合${S}_{1,{v}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{v}_{k}}$元素不超过$T$个，则计算整个集合；否则，计算该集合的$T$个元素）。对于每个查询$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$返回的答案${v}_{k + 1}$，我们检查$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$相对于$w$是否为零团，若是，则返回该零团。

After repeating this construction for every weight interval tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$ ,if we never found a zero-clique, then we return "no."

对每个权重区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$重复此构造后，若始终未发现零团，则返回"否"。

Correctness. Let us show correctness of the reduction. Since we explicitly test for each query answer whether it is a zero-clique,if $G$ contains no zero-clique,then our reduction returns "no."

正确性。现证明该归约的正确性。由于我们显式检验每个查询答案是否为零团，若$G$不含零团，则归约返回"否"。

We next show that if there exists a zero-clique, then our reduction returns a zero-clique with probability at least .99 . To this end, we use the following claim:

接下来证明：若存在零团，则我们的归约算法至少有99%概率返回零团。为此需使用以下断言：

CLAIM 1. Let $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ be a zero-clique. Then,with probability at least .99, there are less than ${100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$ vertices $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ such that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique and has the same weight interval tuple as $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ .

断言1。设$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$为零团，则至少有99%概率使得满足以下条件的顶点$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$少于${100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$个：$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$非零团且其权重区间元组与$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$相同。

We first finish the correctness proof and later prove the claim. Fix any zero-clique $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ , and denote its weight interval tuple by $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ . Note that we query $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ in the instance constructed for $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ . With probability at least 0.99,this query has less than ${100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$ "false positives," that is,vertices $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ such that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ has weight interval tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ and $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique. Then,by listing $T = \left\lceil  {{100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right\rceil$ answers for the query $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ ,at least one answer $v$ must correspond to a zero-clique. (That is,we do not necessarily find ${v}_{k + 1}$ ,but we find some vertex $v$ forming a zero-clique together with ${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}$ .)

先完成正确性证明再验证该断言。固定任意零团$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$，记其权重区间元组为$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$。注意我们在为$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$构造的实例中查询$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$。至少有0.99概率，该查询的"假阳性"结果少于${100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$个（即满足$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$具有权重区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$但$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$非零团的顶点$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$）。因此通过列举查询$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$的$T = \left\lceil  {{100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right\rceil$个答案，至少有一个答案$v$必对应零团。（即未必找到${v}_{k + 1}$，但能找到与${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}$构成零团的某顶点$v$。）

Hence, with probability at least .99 , we find a zero-clique if there exists one. This probability can be boosted to high probability (that is,probability $1 - 1/{n}^{d}$ for any desirable constant $d$ ) by repeating the reduction algorithm $O\left( {d\log n}\right)$ times and returning a zero-clique if at least one repetition finds a zero-clique. This repetition incurs a logarithmic factor in the runtime of our algorithm that we will ignore later on, since it is dominated by the polynomial terms we analyze there.

故当存在零团时，我们至少有99%概率找到。通过将归约算法重复$O\left( {d\log n}\right)$次（只要任一轮发现零团即返回），该概率可提升至极高概率（即对任意期望常数$d$达到$1 - 1/{n}^{d}$概率）。此重复操作会在算法运行时间中引入对数因子，后续分析将忽略该因子，因其被多项式项主导。

Proof of CLAIM 1. To ease notation,let $v \mathrel{\text{:=}} {v}_{k + 1}$ . Let $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ be the weight interval tuple of $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ ,that is, ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,and ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}$ . Let $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ such that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique. If $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ has the same weight interval tuple,then for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,we have ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}$ . It then follows that

引理1的证明。为简化符号表示，令$v \mathrel{\text{:=}} {v}_{k + 1}$。设$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$为$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$的权重区间元组，即对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$有${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}$，且${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}$。取$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$使得$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$非零团。若$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$具有相同权重区间元组，则对任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，可得${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}$。由此推出

$$
\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-p{n}^{-\rho },p{n}^{-\rho }}\right\rbrack  . \tag{3}
$$

We want to bound the probability that this happens, over the random choices in the construction of ${w}^{\prime }$ . To this end,write ${w}_{i}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$ and ${w}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} w\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - w\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$ for all $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Expanding the definition of ${w}^{\prime }$ (see Equation (1)),we obtain

我们需要界定该事件在${w}^{\prime }$构造过程中的随机选择下发生的概率。为此，记${w}_{i}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$且对所有$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$设${w}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} w\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - w\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$。展开${w}^{\prime }$的定义（参见方程(1)），可得

$$
{w}_{1}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{1} + \left( {{y}_{v}^{1} - {y}_{u}^{1}}\right) ,
$$

$$
{w}_{i}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{i} - \left( {{y}_{v}^{i - 1} - {y}_{u}^{i - 1}}\right)  + \left( {{y}_{v}^{i} - {y}_{u}^{i}}\right) \text{ for each }i \in  \{ 2,\ldots ,k - 1\} 
$$

$$
{w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{k} - \left( {{y}_{v}^{k - 1} - {y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right) . \tag{4}
$$

We claim that for fixed ${w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$ and ${y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}$ the above equations induce a bijection sending $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ to $\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$ . Indeed,by the Equations (4),we can compute $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ given $\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$ . For the other direction,we use that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ is a zero-clique (that is, $\left. {w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  = 0}\right)$ ,and $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique (that is, $w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)  \neq  0$ ). This implies

我们断言：对于固定${w}_{1},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$和${y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}$，上述方程导出了一个将$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$映射到$\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$的双射。事实上，根据方程(4)，给定$\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$可计算出$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$。对于逆向映射，我们利用$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$是零团（即$\left. {w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  = 0}\right)$），而$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$非零团（即$w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)  \neq  0$）的性质。由此可得

$$
0 \neq  w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  - w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{k}\left( {w\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - w\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{k}{w}_{i}.
$$

By adding up all Equations (4),since each term ${y}_{v}^{i}$ and ${y}_{u}^{i}$ appears once positively and once negatively,we obtain ${w}_{1}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  \left( {{w}_{1} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$ . Hence,given $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,we can compute $x = \left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right) /\left( {{w}_{1} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$ . Note that,here,we do not divide by 0,since $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{k}{w}_{i} \neq  0$ . Next, we can compute ${y}_{u}^{1} = x \cdot  {w}_{1} + {y}_{v}^{1} - {w}_{1}^{\prime }$ by rearranging the first equation of (4) (recall that ${w}_{1}$ and ${y}_{v}^{1}$ are fixed,we are given ${w}_{1}^{\prime }$ ,and we just computed $x$ ,so each variable on the right-hand side is known). Similarly,from $x$ and ${y}_{u}^{i - 1}$ ,we can compute ${y}_{u}^{i} = x \cdot  {w}_{i} - \left( {{y}_{v}^{i - 1} - {y}_{u}^{i - 1}}\right)  + {y}_{v}^{i} - {w}_{i}^{\prime }$ . Iterating over all $i$ computes $\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$ given $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ .

将所有方程(4)相加时，由于每项${y}_{v}^{i}$和${y}_{u}^{i}$都出现一次正一次负，我们得到${w}_{1}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  \left( {{w}_{1} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$。因此，给定$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$时，可以计算出$x = \left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right) /\left( {{w}_{1} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$。注意此处不会出现除以0的情况，因为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{k}{w}_{i} \neq  0$。接着通过重新排列(4)中第一个方程（已知${w}_{1}$和${y}_{v}^{1}$是固定的，给定${w}_{1}^{\prime }$，且已计算出$x$，故等式右侧所有变量均为已知量），可计算出${y}_{u}^{1} = x \cdot  {w}_{1} + {y}_{v}^{1} - {w}_{1}^{\prime }$。同理，根据$x$和${y}_{u}^{i - 1}$可计算出${y}_{u}^{i} = x \cdot  {w}_{i} - \left( {{y}_{v}^{i - 1} - {y}_{u}^{i - 1}}\right)  + {y}_{v}^{i} - {w}_{i}^{\prime }$。对所有$i$进行迭代计算后，即可在给定$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$条件下求出$\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$。

Since we showed a bijection sending $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ to $\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$ ,and,since $x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}$ are all chosen independently and uniformly random from ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ ,we obtain that $\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ is uniformly random in ${\mathbb{P}}_{p}^{k}$ . In particular,each ${w}_{i}^{\prime } = {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$ is independently and uniformly at random distributed in ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ . Thus,since the intervals in expression (3) have size at most ${2p}{n}^{-\rho } + 1$ , the probability of expression (3) being true (which is greater than or equal to the probability that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ and $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ have the same weight interval tuple) is at most

由于我们证明了将$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$映射到$\left( {x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}}\right)$的双射关系，且$x,{y}_{u}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{u}^{k - 1}$都是从${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中独立均匀随机选取的，因此$\left( {{w}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$在${\mathbb{P}}_{p}^{k}$中呈均匀随机分布。特别地，每个${w}_{i}^{\prime } = {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},v}\right)  - {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},u}\right)$都在${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中独立且均匀随机分布。因此，由于表达式(3)中的区间最大尺寸为${2p}{n}^{-\rho } + 1$，该表达式成立的概率（即不小于$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$和$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$具有相同权重区间元组的概率）至多为

$$
{\left( \frac{{2p}{n}^{-\rho } + 1}{p}\right) }^{k} \leq  {3}^{k}{n}^{-{k\rho }}
$$

where we use the fact that $p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$ . Thus,given $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ such that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique,the probability that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ has the same weight interval tuple as $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ is at most ${3}^{k}{n}^{-{k\rho }}$ .

此处我们利用了一个事实：$p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$。因此，给定$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$使得$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$不是零团时，$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$与$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$具有相同权重区间元组的概率至多为${3}^{k}{n}^{-{k\rho }}$。

Let $X$ be the random variable that is the number of vertices $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ satisfying the conditions in the claim (i.e., $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$ is not a zero-clique and has the same weight interval tuple as $\left. \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right) \right)$ . As $\left| {V}_{k + 1}\right|  \leq  n$ and the probability of each $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ to satisfy the conditions is at most ${3}^{k}{n}^{-{k\rho }}$ ,the expectation of $X$ is $E\left( X\right)  \leq  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$ . We can apply Markov’s inequality to get $\Pr \left( {X \geq  {100E}\left( X\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{100}$ ,implying that $\Pr \left( {X \geq  {100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{100}$ . This proves the claim.

设$X$为满足声明条件的顶点数$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$的随机变量（即$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},u}\right)$不是零团且与$\left. \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right) \right)$具有相同权重区间元组）。由于$\left| {V}_{k + 1}\right|  \leq  n$且每个$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$满足条件的概率至多为${3}^{k}{n}^{-{k\rho }}$，故$X$的期望值为$E\left( X\right)  \leq  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}$。应用马尔可夫不等式可得$\Pr \left( {X \geq  {100E}\left( X\right) }\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{100}$，这意味着$\Pr \left( {X \geq  {100} \cdot  {3}^{k}{n}^{1 - {k\rho }}}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{100}$。由此得证。

Running Time. It remains to analyze the running time and to set the parameter $\rho$ . Assume,to the contrary of the theorem statement,that for some $\varepsilon  > {0k}$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets,universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ can be solved with preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and query time $O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$ for $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$ . We assume w.l.o.g. that $\varepsilon  \leq  1$ . Note that our constructed instances have universe size $\left| U\right|  = \left| {V}_{k + 1}\right|  \leq  n$ (which can be embedded into a universe of size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ),and $T = O\left( {n}^{1 - {k\rho }}\right)$ ,so by setting $\rho  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ the $k$ -Set-Intersection algorithm can be applied. Note that, indeed, $p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$ ,as we promised earlier.

运行时间分析。接下来需分析运行时间并设置参数$\rho$。假设与定理陈述相反，对于某些$\varepsilon  > {0k}$集合上的集合交问题，在全集大小$\left| U\right|  = n$和$T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$条件下，能以$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$查询时间求解$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$。不妨设$\varepsilon  \leq  1$。注意到我们构建的实例全集大小为$\left| U\right|  = \left| {V}_{k + 1}\right|  \leq  n$（可嵌入到大小为$\left| U\right|  = n$的全集中），且$T = O\left( {n}^{1 - {k\rho }}\right)$，因此通过设置$\rho  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$可应用$k$集合交算法。如前所述，确实满足$p \geq  {n}^{\rho }$。

Over $O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$ instances (one for every interval tuple in $S$ ),we have that the total preprocessing time is $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 + {k\rho } - \varepsilon }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ ,since $\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ .

在$O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$个实例上（对应于$S$中的每个区间元组），由于$\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$，总预处理时间为$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 + {k\rho } - \varepsilon }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$。

Let us count the total number of queries. Note that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ is queried in the instance for $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$ if and only if ${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}$ . Recall that for every ${I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$ there are $O\left( 1\right)$ choices for ${I}_{k}$ such that $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$ . Hence,each $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ is queried in $O\left( {n}^{\left( {k - 1}\right) \rho }\right)$ instances, as there are $O\left( {n}^{\rho }\right)$ choices for each of ${I}_{1},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$ and $O\left( 1\right)$ choices for ${I}_{k}$ . The total number of queries is thus $O\left( {n}^{k + \left( {k - 1}\right) \rho }\right)$ . Each query runs in time $O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{1 - {k\rho } + \delta }\right)$ ,so the total query time over all our constructed instances is $O\left( {{n}^{k + \left( {k - 1}\right) \rho } \cdot  {n}^{1 - {k\rho } + \delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 + \delta  - \rho }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$ , since we set $\delta  = \rho /2$ .

让我们统计查询的总次数。注意，当且仅当${w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}$时，实例中会查询$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$以获取$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$。回顾可知，对于每个${I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$，存在$O\left( 1\right)$种可能的${I}_{k}$选择使得$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$成立。因此，每个$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$会在$O\left( {n}^{\left( {k - 1}\right) \rho }\right)$个实例中被查询，因为对于${I}_{1},\ldots ,{I}_{k - 1}$有$O\left( {n}^{\rho }\right)$种选择，而对于${I}_{k}$有$O\left( 1\right)$种选择。因此，查询总次数为$O\left( {n}^{k + \left( {k - 1}\right) \rho }\right)$。每次查询运行时间为$O\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{1 - {k\rho } + \delta }\right)$，故我们构建的所有实例的总查询时间为$O\left( {{n}^{k + \left( {k - 1}\right) \rho } \cdot  {n}^{1 - {k\rho } + \delta }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 + \delta  - \rho }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$，因为我们设定了$\delta  = \rho /2$。

In total,we obtain running time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$ for Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique,contradicting the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture. This concludes the proof of Theorem 27.

最终，我们得到Zero-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-Clique问题的运行时间$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$，这与Zero-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-Clique猜想相矛盾。至此，定理27得证。

### 5.2 Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection

### 5.2 唯一$k$-集合交集

As the next step,we show hardness of $k$ -Set-Intersection for $T = 1$ ,and even if we get the promise that there is a unique answer to a query. We formally define this problem as follows:

接下来，我们将证明$k$-Set-Intersection问题对于$T = 1$的困难性，即使我们承诺查询结果具有唯一解。该问题的形式化定义如下：

Definition 29. The Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection problem has the same input $\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{A}}_{i} = \left\{  {{S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }}\right\}   \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ as $k$ -Set-Intersection (cf. Definition 26). In the query,we are given indices ${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$ ,and if the set ${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$ consists of exactly one element,then we return that element; otherwise,we return the error element $\bot$ . Again,we write $n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$ .

定义29. 唯一$k$-集合交集问题的输入格式$\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$与$k$-Set-Intersection相同（参见定义26）。查询时给定索引${j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}$，若集合${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$恰好包含单个元素则返回该元素，否则返回错误元素$\bot$。同样记作$n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$。

We show how to reduce $k$ -Set-Intersection to Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection.

我们将展示如何将$k$-Set-Intersection问题归约到唯一$k$-Set-Intersection问题。

LEMMA 30. For every $0 < \theta  \leq  1$ ,every $\varepsilon  > 0$ and every $\delta  > 0$ ,if there is a randomized algorithm for Unique-k-Set-Intersection on $n$ sets and universe size $O\left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ ,then there is a randomized algorithm for $k$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets,universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \theta }\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$ .

引理30. 对于任意$0 < \theta  \leq  1$、$\varepsilon  > 0$和$\delta  > 0$，若存在针对$n$个集合、全域大小$O\left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$的唯一k-Set-Intersection问题的随机化算法，其预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$且查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$，则存在针对$n$个集合、全域大小$\left| U\right|  = n$的$k$-Set-Intersection问题的随机化算法，其预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$且查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$。

Proof. The proof idea is as follows: We create many instances of Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection by restricting the k-Set-Intersection instance according to subsamples of the universe, and we return the union of the answers returned by the Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection algorithm over these instances. By properly choosing the number of instances and the subsampling probability, we can show that this process generates with high probability enough answers while maintaining the required running time. We next describe this idea in more detail.

证明. 核心思路是：通过对全域进行子采样来构造多个唯一$k$-Set-Intersection实例，并返回这些实例算法输出结果的并集。通过合理设置实例数量与子采样概率，可以证明该过程能以高概率生成足够多的有效答案，同时满足运行时间要求。下面详细阐述该思想。

Let $\mathcal{I}$ be an instance of $k$ -Set-Intersection with families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$ of total size $n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$ over a universe $U$ of size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and let $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \theta }\right)$ be the query size bound. For every $\ell  \in  \{ \log \left( T\right) ,\ldots ,\log \left( {4n}\right) \}$ and every $j \in  \left\lbrack  {{100T}\ln \left( n\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,we create an instance ${I}_{\ell ,j}$ of Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection by subsampling with probability ${2}^{-\ell }$ ,i.e.,starting from $\mathcal{I}$ and keeping each element in the universe $U$ with probability ${2}^{-\ell }$ and otherwise deleting it from all sets in ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$ .

设$\mathcal{I}$为$k$-集合交问题的一个实例，其集合族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$在规模为$\left| U\right|  = n$的全域$U$上的总大小为$n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$，并设$T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - \theta }\right)$为查询大小限制。对于每个$\ell  \in  \{ \log \left( T\right) ,\ldots ,\log \left( {4n}\right) \}$和每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  {{100T}\ln \left( n\right) }\right\rbrack$，我们通过以概率${2}^{-\ell }$进行子采样的方式创建Unique-$k$-集合交的实例${I}_{\ell ,j}$，即从$\mathcal{I}$出发，以概率${2}^{-\ell }$保留全域$U$中的每个元素，否则将其从${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$的所有集合中删除。

Denote by ${U}_{\ell ,j}$ the surviving universe elements of ${I}_{\ell ,j}$ . We want to show that with high probability ${U}_{j,\ell }$ does not contain more than $\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ elements. Clearly,the probability that ${U}_{\ell ,j}$ has more than $\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ elements only increases when the probability that an individual element survives increases, so if $\ell$ decreases. Thus,we get $\Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\ell ,j}\right|  \geq  p}\right)  \leq  \Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right|  \geq  p}\right)$ for any value $p$ .

用${U}_{\ell ,j}$表示${I}_{\ell ,j}$中幸存的全域元素。我们需要证明在大概率下${U}_{j,\ell }$包含不超过$\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$个元素。显然，当单个元素存活的概率增加时（即当$\ell$减小时），${U}_{\ell ,j}$超过$\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$个元素的概率只会增大。因此，对于任意值$p$，我们得到$\Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\ell ,j}\right|  \geq  p}\right)  \leq  \Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right|  \geq  p}\right)$。

Note that in expectation the size of ${U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}$ is $E\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right)  = \left| U\right|  \cdot  {2}^{-\log \left( T\right) } = \frac{n}{T} = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ . We will use the following basic Chernoff bound: Let ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{r}$ be independent Bernoulli random variables and let $X$ be their sum. Then,we have $\Pr \left( {X \geq  \left( {1 + \delta }\right) E\left( X\right) }\right)  \leq  {\left( \frac{{e}^{\delta }}{{\left( 1 + \delta \right) }^{1 + \delta }}\right) }^{E\left( X\right) }$ for any $\delta  > 0$ . Setting $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1$ ,we get in our case

注意${U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}$的期望大小为$E\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right)  = \left| U\right|  \cdot  {2}^{-\log \left( T\right) } = \frac{n}{T} = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$。我们将使用以下基本切尔诺夫界：设${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{r}$为独立伯努利随机变量，$X$为其和。则对于任意$\delta  > 0$有$\Pr \left( {X \geq  \left( {1 + \delta }\right) E\left( X\right) }\right)  \leq  {\left( \frac{{e}^{\delta }}{{\left( 1 + \delta \right) }^{1 + \delta }}\right) }^{E\left( X\right) }$。令$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1$，在本例中可得

$$
\Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\ell ,j}\right|  \geq  {2E}\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right) }\right)  \leq  \Pr \left( {\left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right|  \geq  {2E}\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right) }\right)  \leq  {\left( \frac{e}{{2}^{2}}\right) }^{E\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right) } \leq  {0.7}^{E\left( \left| {U}_{\log \left( T\right) ,j}\right| \right) }.
$$

Using a union bound over all $O\left( {T{\log }^{2}\left( n\right) }\right)$ instances ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ ,we get that with high probability none of the ${U}_{\ell ,j}$ will have more than $\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ elements. So,in the remainder of this proof,we assume that this is the case (if we ever encounter a universe that is too large in the subsampling, then we simply let the algorithm fail; since the probability is small, this is not a problem for the success probability).

通过对所有$O\left( {T{\log }^{2}\left( n\right) }\right)$个实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$进行联合界分析，我们得出大概率下所有${U}_{\ell ,j}$都不会包含超过$\Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$个元素。因此在后续证明中，我们默认该条件成立（若子采样过程中遇到过大的全域，则直接判定算法失败；由于该概率极小，不影响整体成功概率）。

After the subsampling,we use the assumed algorithm for Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection to preprocess all instances ${I}_{\ell ,j}$ . Then,given a query $\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$ ,we run the algorithm for Unique- $k$ - Set-Intersection on all instances ${I}_{\ell ,j}$ ,filter out $\bot$ -answers,remove duplicates,and return up to $T$ of the remaining answers.

在下采样后，我们采用预设的Unique-$k$-Set-Intersection算法对所有实例${I}_{\ell ,j}$进行预处理。当接收到查询$\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$时，我们在所有实例${I}_{\ell ,j}$上运行该Unique-$k$-Set-Intersection算法，过滤掉$\bot$-答案，去除重复项，最终返回不超过$T$个的有效答案。

Let us analyze this algorithm. Since we filter out wrong answers, all answers we return are correct. It remains to show that, with good probability, we return enough answers. To this end, fix a query and assume that the instance $\mathcal{I}$ has $r > 0$ answers for that query. Pick $\ell$ such that $r + T \in  \left\lbrack  {{2}^{\ell  - 1},{2}^{\ell }}\right\rbrack$ and consider what happens in the queries on the instances ${I}_{\ell ,j}$ . For every answer $v$ ,consider the event ${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$ that $v$ is isolated by instance ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ ,that is, $v$ survives the subsampling, but none of the other $r - 1$ solutions survive the subsampling. This event has probability

让我们分析该算法。由于已过滤错误答案，返回的所有答案均正确。关键需证明在较高概率下能返回足够数量的答案。为此，固定一个查询并假设实例$\mathcal{I}$对该查询有$r > 0$个答案。选取满足$r + T \in  \left\lbrack  {{2}^{\ell  - 1},{2}^{\ell }}\right\rbrack$的$\ell$，观察实例${I}_{\ell ,j}$上的查询过程。对于每个答案$v$，考虑事件${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$：即$v$被实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$隔离的情况，这意味着$v$在下采样中保留而其他$r - 1$个解均被剔除。该事件发生的概率为

$$
\Pr \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{v}\right)  = \frac{1}{{2}^{\ell }}{\left( 1 - \frac{1}{{2}^{\ell }}\right) }^{r - 1} \geq  \frac{1}{2\left( {r + T}\right) }{\left( 1 - \frac{1}{r + T}\right) }^{r + T} \geq  \frac{1}{6\left( {r + T}\right) },
$$

where in the first inequality,we used that $r + T \leq  {2}^{\ell } \leq  2\left( {r + T}\right)$ and $T \geq  0$ ,and for the last inequality,we assume that $r + T \geq  {10}$ ,in which case ${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{r + T}\right) }^{r + T} \geq  \frac{1}{3}$ . Assume first that $r \leq  {2T}$ ,so the above probability is at least $\frac{1}{18T} \geq  \frac{1}{20T}$ .
