Then,the probability that the answer $v$ is not isolated in any instance ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ over all $j \in  \left\lbrack  {{100T}\ln \left( n\right) }\right\rbrack$ is at most ${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{20T}\right) }^{{100T}\ln \left( n\right) } = {n}^{{100T}\ln \left( {1 - \frac{1}{20T}}\right) } \leq  {n}^{-5}$ (using the fact $\ln \left( {1 - x}\right)  \leq   - x$ ). By a union bound over all $r \leq  \left| U\right|  = n$ answers,with probability $\geq  1 - {n}^{-4}$ each of the $r$ answers will be isolated in some instance ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ ,so the Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection algorithm will return each answer at least once. Hence, we see all answers and can correctly return $\min \{ r,T\}$ of them.

首项不等式中我们运用了$r + T \leq  {2}^{\ell } \leq  2\left( {r + T}\right)$和$T \geq  0$的关系，末项不等式则基于$r + T \geq  {10}$的假设，此时${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{r + T}\right) }^{r + T} \geq  \frac{1}{3}$。先设$r \leq  {2T}$，则上述概率至少为$\frac{1}{18T} \geq  \frac{1}{20T}$。那么答案$v$在所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  {{100T}\ln \left( n\right) }\right\rbrack$个实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$中未被隔离的概率至多为${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{20T}\right) }^{{100T}\ln \left( n\right) } = {n}^{{100T}\ln \left( {1 - \frac{1}{20T}}\right) } \leq  {n}^{-5}$（依据事实$\ln \left( {1 - x}\right)  \leq   - x$）。通过对所有$r \leq  \left| U\right|  = n$个答案进行联合界分析，以$\geq  1 - {n}^{-4}$的概率确保每个$r$个答案都会在某个实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$中被隔离，因此Unique-$k$-Set-Intersection算法将至少返回每个答案一次。由此我们可获取全部答案并正确返回其中$\min \{ r,T\}$个。

Now,consider the case $r > {2T}$ . We have ${100T}\ln \left( n\right)$ rounds,where in round $j$ ,we make a random experiment,creating an instance ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ as above. This is essentially a coupon collector problem (see, e.g., Reference [32] for more background), which we analyze next. We will show that,with high probability,we found $T$ different answers after all rounds are done. If the event ${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$ happens,then we say that the answer $v$ was found. Since the events ${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$ are disjoint,in each round, we get an answer with probability at least

现在考虑$r > {2T}$的情况。我们进行${100T}\ln \left( n\right)$轮实验，每轮$j$中通过随机试验生成如上所述的实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$。这本质上是个赠券收集问题（详见参考文献[32]），后续将展开分析。我们将证明在所有轮次完成后，有很大概率能找到$T$个不同答案。若事件${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$发生，则称答案$v$被找到。由于事件${\mathcal{E}}_{v}$互斥，每轮获得答案的概率至少为

$$
\frac{r}{6\left( {r + T}\right) } \geq  \frac{r}{6\left( {r + \frac{1}{2}r}\right) } = \frac{1}{9} \geq  \frac{1}{10}.
$$

In case we get an answer,it is a uniformly random one. Since $T < r/2$ ,as long as we have seen less than $T$ distinct answers,the probability that the next answer is a fresh one is at least half. In combination with the bound on getting an answer, in each round, we find an answer that we have not seen before with probability at least $\frac{1}{20}$ . We next treat the ${100T}\ln \left( n\right)$ rounds as $T$ batches of ${100}\ln \left( n\right)$ rounds. The probability of not getting a new answer in a batch of ${100}\ln \left( n\right)$ rounds is at most ${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{20}\right) }^{{100}\ln \left( n\right) } \leq  {n}^{-5}$ . Taking the union bound over $T$ batches,we get at least $T$ distinct answers over all rounds with probability at least $1 - \frac{T}{{n}^{5}} \geq  1 - \frac{1}{{n}^{4}}$ .

若获得应答，则该应答为均匀随机结果。由于$T < r/2$，只要观测到的不同应答数少于$T$，则下一个应答为新结果的概率至少为二分之一。结合获取应答的概率边界，每轮中发现新应答的概率至少为$\frac{1}{20}$。接下来将${100T}\ln \left( n\right)$轮视为$T$批次的${100}\ln \left( n\right)$轮计算。在${100}\ln \left( n\right)$轮批次中未获得新应答的概率至多为${\left( 1 - \frac{1}{20}\right) }^{{100}\ln \left( n\right) } \leq  {n}^{-5}$。对$T$个批次取并集边界，最终获得至少$T$个不同应答的总概率不低于$1 - \frac{T}{{n}^{5}} \geq  1 - \frac{1}{{n}^{4}}$。

In both cases, with high probability, we return the correct answer.

两种情况下，我们都能以高概率返回正确答案。

To analyze the running time,note that we construct ${100T}\ln \left( n\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( T\right)$ instances ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ ,so the preprocessing takes time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ . Also,each query to an instance ${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$ takes time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ and, since we make $\widetilde{O}\left( T\right)$ of them,the total time of all queries is $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$ . Finally,note that the different answers can easily be collected in, say, a binary search tree on which all operations are in time $O\left( {\ln \left( T\right) }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$ ,so the queries to $I$ can overall be answered in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$ .

分析运行时间时需注意：构建${100T}\ln \left( n\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( T\right)$个实例${\mathcal{I}}_{\ell ,j}$的预处理时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$；每个实例查询耗时$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$，执行$\widetilde{O}\left( T\right)$次查询的总时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$；此外，不同应答可通过二叉搜索树高效收集（所有操作耗时$O\left( {\ln \left( T\right) }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$），因此$I$查询的总体应答时间仍为$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{\delta }}\right)$。

### 5.3 k-Set-Disjointness

### 5.3 k-集合互斥性

Recall that the $k$ -Set-Disjointness problem is defined similarly as $k$ -Set-Intersection,except that a query should just decide whether the intersection ${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \ldots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$ is empty or not. Here,an instance $\mathcal{I}$ is given by a universe set $U$ and families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ ,where we write ${\mathcal{A}}_{i} =$ $\left\{  {{S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }}\right\}$ . Again,we denote the number of sets by $n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$ .

$k$-集合互斥问题的定义与$k$-集合交类似，区别在于查询只需判定交集${S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \ldots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$是否为空。给定实例$\mathcal{I}$包含全集$U$及集合族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$（记作${\mathcal{A}}_{i} =$$\left\{  {{S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }}\right\}$），集合数量仍记为$n = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|$。

LEMMA 31. Let $\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$ and $0 < \theta  < 1$ be constants. If there is a randomized algorithm for $k$ - Set-Disjointness on $n$ sets and universe size $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ with preprocessing $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ ,then there is a randomized algorithm for Unique-k-Set-Intersection on $n$ sets and universe size $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ .

引理31. 设$\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$和$0 < \theta  < 1$为常数。若存在针对$k$-集合不相交问题的随机化算法，适用于$n$个集合且宇宙大小为$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$，具有预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$，则存在针对唯一k-集合交问题的随机化算法，适用于$n$个集合且宇宙大小为$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$，具有预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon }\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$。

Proof. The principle of our reduction is given by the following claim:

证明. 我们归约的原理由以下命题给出：

CLAIM 2. Let $E$ be a set of words over $\{ 0,1\}$ of length $\ell$ . For every $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ and $b \in  \{ 0,1\}$ ,let ${E}^{j,b}$ denote the set of words in $E$ whose $j$ th bit is different from $b$ . Then, $E$ contains exactly one word if and only if for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ exactly one of ${E}^{j,0}$ and ${E}^{j,1}$ is empty. Moreover,if $E = \{ a\}$ ,then the ${jth}$ bit ${a}_{j}$ of $a$ is the (unique) $b \in  \{ 0,1\}$ such that ${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$ .

命题2. 设$E$为$\{ 0,1\}$上长度为$\ell$的单词集合。对于每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$和$b \in  \{ 0,1\}$，令${E}^{j,b}$表示$E$中第$j$位与$b$不同的单词集合。那么，$E$仅包含一个单词当且仅当对于所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，${E}^{j,0}$和${E}^{j,1}$中恰有一个为空。此外，若$E = \{ a\}$，则$a$的第${jth}$位${a}_{j}$是满足${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$的唯一$b \in  \{ 0,1\}$。

Proof. There are three cases,depending on whether $E$ contains a single word,is empty,or contains at least two words:

证明. 分三种情况讨论，取决于$E$包含单个单词、为空集或包含至少两个单词：

- If $E = \{ a\}$ ,then for all $j$ and $b$ ,we have ${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$ if $b = {a}_{j}$ ,and $a \in  {E}^{j,b} \neq  \varnothing$ if $b \neq  {a}_{j}$ .

- 若$E = \{ a\}$，则对所有$j$和$b$，当$b = {a}_{j}$时${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$成立，当$b \neq  {a}_{j}$时$a \in  {E}^{j,b} \neq  \varnothing$成立。

- If $E = \varnothing$ ,then for all $j$ and $b$ ,we have ${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$ ,since ${E}^{j,b} \subseteq  E$ by construction.

- 若$E = \varnothing$，则对所有$j$和$b$，由于构造时${E}^{j,b} \subseteq  E$，故${E}^{j,b} = \varnothing$成立。

- If $E$ contains two distinct words that differ on the $j$ th bit,then ${E}^{j,0}$ and ${E}^{j,1}$ are non-empty.

- 若$E$包含两个在第$j$位相异的单词，则${E}^{j,0}$和${E}^{j,1}$非空。

This proves the claim.

由此得证。

We now describe an algorithm for Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection,using an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness,and whose correctness will be justified with the previous claim. Let $\mathcal{I}$ be an instance of Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection consisting of sets ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$ . Assume w.l.o.g. that the universe $U$ of $\mathcal{A}$ is the set $\left\lbrack  \left| U\right| \right\rbrack$ ,so each element of $U$ can be written with $\ell  = \lfloor \log \left( \left| U\right| \right) \rfloor  + 1 = O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ bits, since $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ .

现描述唯一$k$-集合交问题的算法：借助$k$-集合不相交算法，其正确性由前述命题保证。设$\mathcal{I}$为唯一$k$-集合交实例，包含集合${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$。不失一般性，假设$\mathcal{A}$的宇宙$U$为集合$\left\lbrack  \left| U\right| \right\rbrack$，因$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$，故$U$的每个元素可用$\ell  = \lfloor \log \left( \left| U\right| \right) \rfloor  + 1 = O\left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$位表示。

We create $2\ell$ instances ${I}^{j,b}$ of $k$ -Set-Disjointness,for $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ and $b \in  \{ 0,1\}$ ,as follows: For any $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ and $b \in  \{ 0,1\}$ ,remove every $v \in  U$ whose $j$ th bit is $b$ from the universe and from all sets in ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$ ,and call the resulting sets ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$ . Define ${\mathcal{I}}^{j,b}$ to contain the sets ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$ and the same queries as the original instance $I$ . Denote by ${S}_{i,h}^{j,b}$ the sets belonging to ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$ . We run the preprocessing of the $k$ -Set-Disjointness algorithm on each instance ${\mathcal{I}}^{j,b}$ . Now,given a query $q = \left( {{h}_{1},\ldots ,{h}_{k}}\right)$ ,we ask the same query for each instance ${I}^{j,b}$ for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ and $b \in  \{ 0,1\}$ . By definition,each answer ${\alpha }^{j,b}$ we obtain is the answer yes/no to the question whether ${E}^{j,b}$ is empty, where ${E}^{j,b} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b}$ . With Claim 2,we get that the following (two cases of the) answer to $q$ for the original instand $\mathcal{I}$ of Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection is correct:

我们为$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$和$b \in  \{ 0,1\}$创建如下$2\ell$个$k$-集合不相交性实例${I}^{j,b}$：对于任意$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$和$b \in  \{ 0,1\}$，从全域及${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}$的所有集合中移除第$j$位为$b$的每个$v \in  U$，并将结果集合命名为${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$。定义${\mathcal{I}}^{j,b}$包含这些集合${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$及与原实例$I$相同的查询。用${S}_{i,h}^{j,b}$表示属于${\mathcal{A}}_{i}^{j,b}$的集合。我们在每个实例${\mathcal{I}}^{j,b}$上运行$k$-集合不相交性算法的预处理过程。当收到查询$q = \left( {{h}_{1},\ldots ,{h}_{k}}\right)$时，我们对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$和$b \in  \{ 0,1\}$的每个实例${I}^{j,b}$提出相同查询。根据定义，每个获得的答案${\alpha }^{j,b}$即是对"${E}^{j,b}$是否为空集"的肯定/否定回答，其中${E}^{j,b} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b}$。结合声明2，可得出以下针对Unique-$k$-集合交原实例$\mathcal{I}$的查询$q$的两种正确答案情形：

- if for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ ,we have exactly one of ${E}^{j,0}$ or ${E}^{j,1}$ is empty,i.e.,either ${\alpha }^{j,0} =$ yes or ${\alpha }^{j,1} =$ yes,

- 若对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，${E}^{j,0}$与${E}^{j,1}$中有且仅有一个为空集，即要么${\alpha }^{j,0} =$为肯定，要么${\alpha }^{j,1} =$为肯定，

then ${S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b} = \{ a\}$ where the $j$ th bit of $a$ is the unique $b$ such that ${\alpha }^{j,b} =$ yes and we return $a$ ;

则${S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b} = \{ a\}$中$a$的第$j$位即为使得${\alpha }^{j,b} =$为肯定的唯一$b$，此时返回$a$；

- otherwise, ${S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b}$ does not contain a single element,and we return $\bot$ .

- 否则，${S}_{1,{h}_{1}}^{j,b} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{h}_{k}}^{j,b}$不包含单一元素，此时返回$\bot$。

We have thus justified that the algorithm is correct. It is clear that it satisfies the claimed time bounds.

由此我们验证了算法的正确性。显然该算法满足所声称的时间复杂度界限。

So far,we measured running times in terms of the number of sets $n$ . Next,we show how to obtain lower bounds in terms of the input size $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$ ,which is at most $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \cdot  \left| U\right|$ .

此前我们以集合数量$n$衡量运行时间。接下来我们将展示如何根据至多为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \cdot  \left| U\right|$的输入规模$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  {\mathcal{A}}_{i}}}\left| S\right|$获得下界。

LEMMA 32. For any constants $\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$ ,if there is an algorithm that solves $k$ -Set-Disjointness with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ ,then for any constant $0 < \theta  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ there is an algorithm that solves $k$ -Set-Disjointness on $n$ sets and universe size $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + \theta }\right) }\right)$ .

引理32. 对于任意常数$\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$，若存在算法能在预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$内求解$k$-集合不相交问题，则对于任意常数$0 < \theta  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$，都存在算法能在预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + \theta }\right) }\right)$内求解$n$个集合上、论域大小为$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$的$k$-集合不相交问题。

Proof. For every instance $I$ of $k$ -Set-Disjointness on universe size $\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$ ,we have that $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = O\left( {n}^{1 + \theta }\right)$ ,because $\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {A}_{i}\right|  \cdot  \left| U\right|  \leq  n\left| U\right|$ . Now,on $\mathcal{I}$ ,the assumed algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness takes preprocessing time

证明. 对于论域大小为$\left| U\right|  = \Theta \left( {n}^{\theta }\right)$的$k$-集合不相交问题的每个实例$I$，我们有$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  = O\left( {n}^{1 + \theta }\right)$，因为$\parallel \mathcal{I}\parallel  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {A}_{i}\right|  \cdot  \left| U\right|  \leq  n\left| U\right|$。现在，在$\mathcal{I}$上，假设的$k$-集合不相交问题算法所需预处理时间为

$$
\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k + {k\theta } - \varepsilon  - {\theta \varepsilon }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}\right) ,
$$

where we used $\theta  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ in the last step. Observing that the query time is $\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + \theta }\right) }\right)$ completes the proof.

其中最后一步我们使用了$\theta  \leq  \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$。观察到查询时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + \theta }\right) }\right)$即完成证明。

Finally,we are ready to prove the main result of this section: the hardness of $k$ -Set-Disjointness for all $k \geq  2$ based on the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture.

最终，我们准备证明本节主要结论：基于零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想，对所有$k \geq  2$的$k$-集合不相交问题难度。

Proof of THEOREM 23. Almost all algorithms discussed in this proof are randomized; we omit this word for readability.

定理23的证明. 本证明中讨论的算法几乎都是随机化的；为可读性起见我们省略此限定词。

Assume that there is a constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness with preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ and query time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ .

假设存在常数$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对所有$\delta  > 0$，都存在算法能以预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$求解$k$-集合不相交问题。

For ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ ,Theorem 27 shows that there is a constant ${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ such that $k$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets,universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$ has no algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ and query time $O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$ if we assume the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture.

对于${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$，定理27表明：若假设零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想成立，则存在常数${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$使得在$n$个集合、论域大小$\left| U\right|  = n$且$T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$条件下的$k$-集合相交问题不存在预处理时间$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$的算法。

Using this ${\delta }^{\prime }$ ,we set $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{{\delta }^{\prime }}{2\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right) }$ . Since we can solve $k$ -Set-Disjointness with preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$ and query time $O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$ ,by Lemma 32 for $\theta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2$ there is an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Disjointness on $n$ sets and universe size $\Theta \left( {n}^{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right) }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }/2}\right)$ . By Lemma 31,we get an algorithm with the same time bounds for Unique- $k$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets and universe size $\Theta \left( {n}^{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$ . Then,using Lemma 30,we get an algorithm for $k$ -Set-Intersection on $n$ sets,universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ ,and $T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$ with preprocessing time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }/2}}\right)  = O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$ . We simplify the preprocessing time to $\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2 - \varepsilon /2}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ ,where we used $\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{2} < {\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k} < \frac{\varepsilon }{2}$ . The obtained preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ and query time $O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$ contradict the statement that we got from Theorem 27 in the last paragraph,so the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture is false.

利用此${\delta }^{\prime }$，我们设定$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{{\delta }^{\prime }}{2\left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right) }$。由于我们能在预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{k - \varepsilon }}\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{\delta }}\right)$内解决$k$-集合不相交问题，根据引理32对于$\theta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2$，存在一个算法能在$n$个集合及宇宙大小$\Theta \left( {n}^{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$上，以预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{\delta \left( {1 + {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right) }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }/2}\right)$处理$k$-集合不相交问题。通过引理31，我们得到一个具有相同时间界限的算法，用于在$n$个集合及宇宙大小$\Theta \left( {n}^{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$上的唯一$k$-集合交集问题。接着，利用引理30，我们得到一个算法，用于在$n$个集合、宇宙大小$\left| U\right|  = n$及$T = \Theta \left( {n}^{1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2}\right)$条件下，预处理时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}}\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }/2}}\right)  = O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$的$k$-集合交集问题。我们将预处理时间简化为$\widetilde{O}\left( {T{n}^{k - \varepsilon /2}}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }/2 - \varepsilon /2}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$，其中使用了$\frac{{\varepsilon }^{\prime }}{2} < {\varepsilon }^{\prime } = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k} < \frac{\varepsilon }{2}$。所获得的预处理时间$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$和查询时间$O\left( {T{n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)$与上一段从定理27得出的陈述相矛盾，因此零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想不成立。

## 6 Removing Self-joins in Direct Access

## 6 直接访问中消除自连接

In Section 4.1,we showed how to simulate a $k$ -star query by embedding it into another query $Q$ of high incompatibility number. The construction required that $Q$ was self-join-free. However,we would like to show lower bounds also for queries that have self-joins. To this end, in this section, we show how to simulate direct access to queries without self-joins by direct access to queries with self-joins in such a way that the incompatibility number-and, in fact, also the underlying hypergraph-does not change. This will allow us to show hardness of queries with self-joins later on. To state our result formally,we start with a definition. Let $Q$ be a join query,possibly with self-joins. We say that a query ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ is a self-join-free version of a query $Q$ if it can be obtained from $Q$ by replacing the relation symbols such that each relation symbol appears at most once in ${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$ . The main result of this section is the following:

在4.1节中，我们演示了如何通过将$k$星型查询嵌入到另一个具有高不兼容数（incompatibility number）的查询$Q$中进行模拟。该构造要求$Q$不含自连接（self-join-free）。但我们希望针对含自连接的查询同样给出下界证明。为此，本节将展示如何通过含自连接查询的直接访问（direct access），来模拟无自连接查询的直接访问，且保持不兼容数及底层超图结构不变。这将为后续证明含自连接查询的复杂性奠定基础。为形式化表述结果，我们首先给出定义：设$Q$为可能含自连接的连接查询（join query），若查询${Q}^{\text{sf }}$可通过替换$Q$中的关系符号使得每个符号在${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$中至多出现一次而获得，则称其为$Q$的无自连接版本（self-join-free version）。本节主要结论如下：

THEOREM 33. Let ${Q}^{sf}$ be a self-join-free version of a join query $Q$ ,and let $L$ be a variable order for $Q$ and ${Q}^{sf}$ . If there is a lexicographic direct access algorithm for $Q$ by $L$ with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in  \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then there is a lexicographic direct access algorithm for ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ by $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) {\log }^{2}\left| D\right| }\right)$ . The same sentence holds after exchanging $Q$ and ${Q}^{sf}$ .

定理33. 设${Q}^{sf}$为连接查询$Q$的无自连接版本，$L$为$Q$和${Q}^{sf}$的变量序（variable order）。若存在针对$Q$的字典序直接访问算法（lexicographic direct access algorithm），其预处理时间为$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in  \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$、访问时间为$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$，则存在针对${Q}^{\text{sf }}$的字典序直接访问算法，其预处理时间为$O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$、访问时间为$O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) {\log }^{2}\left| D\right| }\right)$。该命题在交换$Q$与${Q}^{sf}$后依然成立。

One of the directions of Theorem 33 is trivial, as an algorithm for a self-join-free query can be used to solve queries with self-joins with only linear overhead at preprocessing by duplicating the relevant relations,and the ${\log }^{2}\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ factor of the access time is not required. However,the other direction is by no means trivial. In particular, the same claim for the task of enumeration instead of direct access does not hold [11, 17]. We devote the rest of this section to proving this direction.

定理33的一个方向证明是平凡的——无自连接查询算法只需通过复制相关关系，以线性预处理开销即可处理含自连接查询，且无需访问时间的${\log }^{2}\left( \left| D\right| \right)$因子。然而反方向证明绝非易事。特别地，对于枚举任务（enumeration）而非直接访问的相同主张并不成立[11,17]。本节余下部分将致力于证明该方向。

### 6.1 Colored and Self-join-free Versions

### 6.1 着色版本与无自连接版本

To prove that direct access for $Q$ implies direct access for ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ ,it will be convenient to work with an intermediate query. We define the colored version ${Q}^{c}$ of a query $Q$ to be the query obtained from $Q$ by adding the unary atom ${R}_{x}\left( x\right)$ for each variable $x$ . The colored version is useful,because it is equivalent to any self-join-free version:

为证明$Q$的直接访问蕴含${Q}^{\text{sf }}$的直接访问，使用中间查询将更为便利。我们定义查询$Q$的着色版本（colored version）${Q}^{c}$为：对每个变量$x$添加一元原子（unary atom）${R}_{x}\left( x\right)$后得到的查询。着色版本的实用性在于其等价于任意无自连接版本：

LEMMA 34. Let ${Q}^{c}$ and ${Q}^{sf}$ be the colored version and a self-join-free version of the same join query $Q$ . There are lex-preserving exact reductions in both directions between ${Q}^{c}$ and ${Q}^{sf}$ .

引理34. 设${Q}^{c}$和${Q}^{sf}$为同一连接查询$Q$的着色版本和无自连接版本。在${Q}^{c}$与${Q}^{sf}$之间存在双向的字典序保持精确归约（lex-preserving exact reduction）。

Proof. Without loss of generality,let ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ denote the self-join-free version of $Q$ obtained by replacing each atom $R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ by ${R}_{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ (where ${R}_{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}$ is a new relation symbol associated to $R$ and the list of variables ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$ ). The easy direction is the following exact reduction from ${Q}^{c}$ to ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ . Given a database ${D}^{c}$ for ${Q}^{c}$ ,we construct in linear time the database ${D}^{\text{sf }}$ as follows: For every atom $R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ of $Q$ ,we set

证明。不失一般性，令${Q}^{\text{sf }}$表示通过将每个原子$R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$替换为${R}_{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$（其中${R}_{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}$是与$R$及变量列表${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$关联的新关系符号）而得到的$Q$的无自连接版本。简单方向是从${Q}^{c}$到${Q}^{\text{sf }}$的精确归约：给定${Q}^{c}$的数据库${D}^{c}$，我们线性时间内构造数据库${D}^{\text{sf }}$如下：对于$Q$的每个原子$R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$，我们设定

$$
{R}_{\underline{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}}^{{D}^{\text{sf }}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \mid  {D}^{c} \vDash  R\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{R}_{{x}_{i}}\left( {a}_{i}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

Obviously,this entails the equality ${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  = {Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)$ ,which shows the first direction.

显然，这意味着等式${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  = {Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)$成立，从而证明了第一方向。

Let us construct the reduction from ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ to ${Q}^{c}$ . (Note that this is the reduction we will need in the following.) Given a database ${D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$ for ${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$ ,we construct in linear time the database ${D}^{c}$ defined as follows: We take $\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\text{sf }}\right)$ . For each $x \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,we set

让我们构建从${Q}^{\text{sf }}$到${Q}^{c}$的归约。（注意这是后续所需的归约。）给定${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$的数据库${D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}$，我们线性时间内构造如下定义的数据库${D}^{c}$：取$\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\text{sf }}\right)$。对每个$x \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$，我们设定

$$
{R}_{x}^{{D}^{c}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {x,b}\right)  \mid  b \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right) }\right\}  ,
$$

and for each atom $R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ of $Q$ ,we include in ${R}^{{D}^{c}}$

且对于$Q$的每个原子$R\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$，我们将其包含在${R}^{{D}^{c}}$中

$$
\left\{  {\left( {\left( {{x}_{1},{a}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{x}_{k},{a}_{k}}\right) }\right)  \mid  {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}} \vDash  {R}_{\underline{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}}\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

That is, ${R}^{{D}^{c}}$ is defined by the union of the corresponding relations in ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ .

即${R}^{{D}^{c}}$由${Q}^{\text{sf }}$中对应关系的并集定义。

For every tuple $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {\left( \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right) \right) }^{k}$ ,we get the equivalence

对于每个元组$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {\left( \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right) \right) }^{k}$，我们得到等价关系

$$
{D}^{\text{sf }} \vDash  {R}_{\underline{{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}}\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  {D}^{c} \vDash  R\left( {\left( {{x}_{1},{a}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{x}_{k},{a}_{k}}\right) }\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{R}_{{x}_{i}}\left( \left( {{x}_{i},{a}_{i}}\right) \right) .
$$

This immediately implies for every assignment $a$ of the variables of ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ in $\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\text{sf }}\right)$ the equivalence $a \in  {Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  \bar{a} \in  {Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$ where $\bar{a}$ is the mapping that assigns to every $x \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ the value $\left( {x,a\left( x\right) }\right)$ . It is easy to verify that the function $a \mapsto  \bar{a}$ is a bijection between ${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)$ and ${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$ , computable in constant time and preserving any lexicographic order.

这直接表明：对于$\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{\text{sf }}\right)$中${Q}^{\text{sf }}$变量的每个赋值$a$，存在等价关系$a \in  {Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  \bar{a} \in  {Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$，其中$\bar{a}$是将每个$x \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$映射为值$\left( {x,a\left( x\right) }\right)$的映射。易证函数$a \mapsto  \bar{a}$是${Q}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\left( {D}^{\mathrm{{sf}}}\right)$与${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$之间的双射，可在常数时间内计算并保持任何字典序。

Lemma 34 implies that,if there is a direct-access algorithm for ${Q}^{c}$ in some lexicographic order $L$ ,then there is a direct-access algorithm for ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ in order $L$ with the same time guarantees. With this at hand, it is left to prove that efficient direct access for any query implies the same for its colored version.

引理34表明，若存在按某字典序$L$对${Q}^{c}$的直接访问算法，则存在具有相同时间保证、按序$L$对${Q}^{\text{sf }}$的直接访问算法。由此出发，只需证明：任何查询的高效直接访问能力对其着色版本同样成立。

### 6.2 Counting under Prefix Constraints and Direct Access

### 6.2 前缀约束下的计数与直接访问

A natural approach for lexicographic direct access is to decide on the assignment to the variables one at a time in the required order. This approach has a strong connection to the task of counting the possible assignments when a prefix of the variables is already determined, and it will be convenient in our proof to go through this intermediate task.

字典序直接访问的自然方法是按所需顺序逐个确定变量的赋值。这种方法与在变量前缀已确定时计算可能赋值的任务密切相关，通过这个中间任务进行证明将更为便利。

Consider the following notion of constraints on answers: Let $Q$ be a join query, $L$ an order of its variables,and $D$ a database. We define a prefix constraint $\mathfrak{c}$ on a prefix ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}$ of $L$ to be a mapping $\mathfrak{c}$ on variables $\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}}\right\}$ such that for $i \in  \left\lbrack  {r - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,we have $\mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ and $\mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{r}\right)$ is an interval in $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ . We denote by $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ the set $\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}}\right\}$ of variables on which $\mathfrak{c}$ is defined. To make the notation for prefix constraints more symmetric,we treat elements of $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ as intervals of length 1,so $c$ maps all ${x}_{i}$ in the prefix to intervals,but for $i \in  \left\lbrack  {r - 1}\right\rbrack$ these intervals have length 1. We say that an answer $a \in  Q\left( D\right)$ satisfies the prefix constraint $\mathfrak{c}$ if and only if for all $i \in  \left\lbrack  r\right\rbrack$ , we have $a\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \in  \mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{i}\right)$ . Counting under prefix constraints is a task defined by a query and an order where,after a preprocessing phase on a database,the user can specify a prefix constraint $c$ and expect the number of query answers that satisfy $\varepsilon$ . We call the time it takes to provide an answer given a prefix constraint the counting time.

考虑以下关于答案约束的概念：设$Q$为连接查询，$L$是其变量顺序，$D$为数据库。我们定义$L$的前缀${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}$上的前缀约束$\mathfrak{c}$为变量$\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}}\right\}$上的映射，使得对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  {r - 1}\right\rbrack$，有$\mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$且$\mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{r}\right)$是$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$中的区间。用$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$表示$\mathfrak{c}$定义的变量集$\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}}\right\}$。为使前缀约束表示更对称，将$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$元素视为长度为1的区间，因此$c$将前缀中所有${x}_{i}$映射为区间，但对$i \in  \left\lbrack  {r - 1}\right\rbrack$这些区间长度为1。当且仅当对所有$i \in  \left\lbrack  r\right\rbrack$满足$a\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \in  \mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{i}\right)$时，称答案$a \in  Q\left( D\right)$满足前缀约束$\mathfrak{c}$。前缀约束下计数是由查询和顺序定义的任务，在数据库预处理阶段后，用户可指定前缀约束$c$并获取满足$\varepsilon$的查询答案数量。给定前缀约束时提供答案所需时间称为计数时间。

Prefix constraints will be useful in the proof of Theorem 33 due to the following equivalence between direct access and counting:

前缀约束在定理33的证明中很有用，因为直接访问与计数存在以下等价关系：

Proposition 35. Let $Q$ be a join query and $L$ an ordering of its variables.

命题35. 设$Q$为连接查询，$L$是其变量顺序。

- If lexicographic direct access for $Q$ and $L$ can be done with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in  \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then counting under prefix constraints for $Q$ and $L$ can be done with preprocessing time $O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$ and counting time $O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left| D\right| }\right)$ .

- 若$Q$和$L$的字典序直接访问可实现预处理时间$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in  \Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$和访问时间$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$，则$Q$和$L$的前缀约束下计数可实现预处理时间$O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$和计数时间$O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left| D\right| }\right)$。

- If counting under prefix constraints for $Q$ and $L$ can be done with preprocessing time $p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in$ $\Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and counting time $r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,then lexicographic direct access for $Q$ and $L$ can be done with preprocessing time $O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left| D\right| }\right)$ .

- 若$Q$和$L$的前缀约束下计数可实现预处理时间$p\left( \left| D\right| \right)  \in$$\Omega \left( \left| D\right| \right)$和计数时间$r\left( \left| D\right| \right)$，则$Q$和$L$的字典序直接访问可实现预处理时间$O\left( {p\left( \left| D\right| \right) }\right)$和访问时间$O\left( {r\left( \left| D\right| \right) \log \left| D\right| }\right)$。

Proof. To go from a direct access algorithm to a counting algorithm, note that the answers satisfying a prefix constraint appear consecutively in the ordered list of query answers. We use binary search to find the indices of the first and the last answers satisfying the given prefix constraint. The difference between these indices is the count we are looking for.

证明。要将直接访问算法转化为计数算法，需注意满足前缀约束的答案在查询结果有序列表中连续出现。我们采用二分查找定位首个及末个满足给定前缀约束的答案索引，二者之差即为所求计数。

For the other direction, given an index we want to access, we iteratively fix the assignments to the query variables in the specified order $L$ . For each variable,we perform binary search on $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ by using prefix constraints to count the answers in the two halves of the interval and choose the relevant half.

逆向推导时，给定待访问索引，我们按指定顺序$L$迭代固定查询变量的赋值。对每个变量，通过前缀约束在$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$上执行二分查找，统计区间两半部分的答案数量并选择相关半区间。

### 6.3 Counting under Prefix Constraints for the Colored Version

### 6.3 着色版本的前缀约束计数

The final and most involved component of our proof of Theorem 33 is to use an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for any join query to solve the same task for its colored version.

定理33证明中最复杂的关键环节是：利用任意连接查询的前缀约束计数算法，解决其着色版本的相同任务。

LEMMA 36. Let $Q$ be a join query and $L$ an ordering of its variables. If there is an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for $Q$ and $L$ ,then there is an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for the colored version ${Q}^{c}$ and $L$ with the same time guarantees.

引理36。设$Q$为连接查询，$L$是其变量序。若存在针对$Q$和$L$的前缀约束计数算法，则着色版本${Q}^{c}$与$L$在相同时间复杂度下也存在相应算法。

Notice that, by combining Lemma 34 with Lemma 36, we get that an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for a join query implies an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for any of its self-join-free versions.

注意结合引理34与引理36可得：连接查询的前缀约束计数算法意味着其任意无自连接版本也存在相应算法。

We remark that Lemma 36 cannot simply be proven by constructing an equivalent database for $Q$ given a database for ${Q}^{c}$ by filtering the original relations using the new unary relations and then removing the unary relations,due to self-joins. As an example,if the query is $Q\left( {x,y}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right)$ , then the colored version is $Q\left( {x,y}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right) ,{R}_{x}\left( x\right) ,{R}_{y}\left( y\right)$ ,and we cannot simply eliminate the last two atoms of the colored version by filtering the other atoms, because we cannot have two copies of $R$ each filtered according to a different variable. We next prove Lemma 36.

需说明的是，由于自连接存在，引理36无法通过为$Q$构建等价数据库来简单证明（即通过新一元关系过滤原关系后删除一元关系）。例如查询为$Q\left( {x,y}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right)$时，着色版本是$Q\left( {x,y}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right) ,{R}_{x}\left( x\right) ,{R}_{y}\left( y\right)$，此时无法通过过滤其他原子来消除着色版本的最后两个原子——因为无法获得根据不同变量过滤的$R$的两个副本。下文将证明引理36。

It will be convenient to have a more symmetric perspective on join queries. To this end, we remind the reader of some basics on homomorphisms between finite structures. Let $A$ and $B$ be two finite structures over the same vocabulary. Then, $h : \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$ is called a homomorphism from $A$ to $B$ if and only if,for every relation ${R}^{A}$ of $A$ and every tuple $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{A}$ ,we have $\left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{B}$ . To simplify notation,we make the convention $h\left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)$ for $a = \left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$ . An automorphism of $A$ is a bijective homomorphism from $A$ to itself. We will use the fact that homomorphisms compose,i.e.,if ${h}_{1}$ is a homomorphism from $A$ to $B$ and ${h}_{2}$ is a homomorphism from $B$ to $C$ ,then $\left( {{h}_{2} \circ  {h}_{1}}\right)$ defined for every $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ by $\left( {{h}_{2} \circ  {h}_{1}}\right) \left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}_{2}\left( {{h}_{1}\left( a\right) }\right)$ is a homomorphism from $A$ to $C$ .

采用更对称的视角看待连接查询将更为便利。为此，我们回顾有限结构间同态的基本概念。设$A$和$B$是同词汇表下的两个有限结构，当且仅当对于$A$的每个关系${R}^{A}$及每个元组$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{A}$都满足$\left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{B}$时，称$h : \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$为从$A$到$B$的同态。为简化记法，约定$h\left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)$表示$a = \left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)$。$A$的自同构是指从$A$到自身的双射同态。我们将运用同态的可组合性：若${h}_{1}$是从$A$到$B$的同态，且${h}_{2}$是从$B$到$C$的同态，则对每个$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$按$\left( {{h}_{2} \circ  {h}_{1}}\right) \left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}_{2}\left( {{h}_{1}\left( a\right) }\right)$定义的$\left( {{h}_{2} \circ  {h}_{1}}\right)$构成从$A$到$C$的同态。

Every query $Q$ is assigned a finite structure ${A}_{Q}$ on domain $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ as follows: For every $k$ -ary relation symbol $R$ of $Q$ ,the structure ${A}_{Q}$ has a $k$ -ary relation ${R}^{{A}_{Q}}$ ,which contains exactly the tuples $\left( {{x}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{i}_{k}}}\right)$ such that $R\left( {{x}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{i}_{k}}}\right)$ is an atom of $Q$ . Then, $h$ is an answer to $Q$ on a database $D$ if and only if it is a homomorphism from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ . The notions on prefix constraints carry over from query answers to homomorphisms in the obvious way.

每个查询$Q$都被分配一个定义域$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$上的有限结构${A}_{Q}$，其构建方式如下：对于$Q$的每个$k$元关系符号$R$，结构${A}_{Q}$都包含一个$k$元关系${R}^{{A}_{Q}}$，该关系精确包含满足$R\left( {{x}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{i}_{k}}}\right)$是$Q$原子的元组$\left( {{x}_{{i}_{1}},\ldots ,{x}_{{i}_{k}}}\right)$。当且仅当$h$是从${A}_{Q}$到数据库$D$的同态时，它才是$Q$在数据库$D$上的答案。前缀约束的概念从查询答案到同态的转移方式显而易见。

Example 37. Consider the join query $Q\left( {x,y,z}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right) ,R\left( z\right)$ ,which we will use as a running example in this section. The associated structure ${A}_{Q}$ has the domain $\{ x,y,z\}$ and the single relation ${R}^{{A}_{Q}} = \{ \left( x\right) ,\left( y\right) ,\left( z\right) \}$ . There are ${3}^{3}$ homomorphisms from ${A}_{Q}$ to itself (in this example,any variable can map to any variable), but there are only 3 ! automorphisms. In particular, if we consider the order $L = \left( {x,y,z}\right)$ of the variables and we denote by(a,b,c)the mapping $\{ x \mapsto  a,y \mapsto  b,z \mapsto  c\}$ for any values $a,b,c$ ,then we get that the automorphisms correspond to the vectors of permutations of the vertices.

示例37. 考虑连接查询$Q\left( {x,y,z}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( x\right) ,R\left( y\right) ,R\left( z\right)$，本节将以此作为贯穿性示例。其关联结构${A}_{Q}$具有定义域$\{ x,y,z\}$和单一关系${R}^{{A}_{Q}} = \{ \left( x\right) ,\left( y\right) ,\left( z\right) \}$。存在${3}^{3}$个从${A}_{Q}$到自身的同态（本例中任意变量可映射至任意变量），但仅有3!个自同态。特别地，若考虑变量顺序$L = \left( {x,y,z}\right)$并用(a,b,c)表示任意值$a,b,c$的映射$\{ x \mapsto  a,y \mapsto  b,z \mapsto  c\}$，则可得出自同态对应于顶点排列的向量。

Notation. For a join query $Q$ to a database $D$ ,an order $L$ of $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,and a prefix constraint $\mathfrak{c}$ ,we let $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ denote the set of homomorphisms from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ (corresponding to the set of answers in $Q\left( D\right)$ ) that satisfy $\mathfrak{c}$ .

符号约定。对于数据库$D$上的连接查询$Q$，给定$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$的序关系$L$及前缀约束$\mathfrak{c}$，我们用$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}}\right)$表示从${A}_{Q}$到$D$的满足$\mathfrak{c}$的同态集合（对应$Q\left( D\right)$中的答案集）。

Let ${D}^{c}$ be a database on which we want to solve the counting problem for ${Q}^{c}$ . We construct a new database $D$ over the relations of $Q$ (without the additional unary relations) as follows by tagging the values of the database by variable names. For every relation symbol $R$ of arity $k$ of $Q$ , the database $D$ contains the relation:

设${D}^{c}$为待求解计数问题${Q}^{c}$的数据库。我们通过用变量名标记数据库值，在$Q$的关系模式（不含额外一元关系）上构造新数据库$D$：对于$Q$中每个元数为$k$的关系符号$R$，数据库$D$包含如下关系：

$$
{R}^{D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {\left( {{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{k},{b}_{k}}\right) }\right)  \mid  \left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{A}_{Q}},\left( {{b}_{1},\ldots ,{b}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{D}^{c}},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {b}_{i} \in  {R}_{{a}_{i}}^{{D}^{c}}}\right\}  .
$$

The domain is $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  \mid  b \in  {R}_{a}^{{D}^{c}}}\right\}$ ,which we order lexicographically, so $\left( {a,b}\right)  \prec  \left( {{a}^{\prime },{b}^{\prime }}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  a{ \prec  }_{L}{a}^{\prime } \vee  \left( {a = {a}^{\prime } \land  b \prec  {b}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,where ${ \prec  }_{L}$ is the order relation of $L$ .

定义域为按字典序排列的$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)  \mid  b \in  {R}_{a}^{{D}^{c}}}\right\}$，即$\left( {a,b}\right)  \prec  \left( {{a}^{\prime },{b}^{\prime }}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  a{ \prec  }_{L}{a}^{\prime } \vee  \left( {a = {a}^{\prime } \land  b \prec  {b}^{\prime }}\right)$，其中${ \prec  }_{L}$表示$L$的序关系。

Example 38. Consider again the query from Example 37. The structure ${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$ for the colored version of the query has the relations ${R}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( x\right) ,\left( y\right) ,\left( z\right) \} ,{R}_{x}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( x\right) \} ,{R}_{y}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( y\right) \}$ ,and ${R}_{z}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}} = \{ \left( z\right) \}$ . Given a database ${D}^{c}$ with ${R}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( b\right) ,\left( c\right) \} ,{R}_{x}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( c\right) \} ,{R}_{y}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( b\right) \}$ , and ${R}_{z}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) \}$ ,we construct the database $D$ with ${R}^{D} = \{ \left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {x,c}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) \}$ .

示例38. 再次考察示例37的查询。该查询着色版本对应的结构${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$包含关系${R}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( x\right) ,\left( y\right) ,\left( z\right) \} ,{R}_{x}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( x\right) \} ,{R}_{y}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}} = }\{ \left( y\right) \}$和${R}_{z}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}} = \{ \left( z\right) \}$。给定含有${R}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( b\right) ,\left( c\right) \} ,{R}_{x}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( c\right) \} ,{R}_{y}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) ,\left( b\right) \}$和${R}_{z}^{{D}^{c}} = \{ \left( a\right) \}$的数据库${D}^{c}$，可构造出具有${R}^{D} = \{ \left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {x,c}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) \}$的数据库$D$。

Let ${\pi }_{1} : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ be the projection on the first coordinate,i.e.,for all $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ , we have ${\pi }_{1}\left( {a,b}\right)  = a$ . Analogously,we define ${\pi }_{2} : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$ as the projection to the second coordinate. We will use the following simple observation throughout the remainder of this proof:

设${\pi }_{1} : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$为第一坐标投影，即对所有$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$满足${\pi }_{1}\left( {a,b}\right)  = a$。类似地，定义${\pi }_{2} : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$为第二坐标投影。本证明后续部分将使用以下基本结论：

Observation 39. Let $h$ be a homomorphism from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ . Then,we have $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  =$ $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ if and only if ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$ is an automorphism.

观察39. 设$h$为从${A}_{Q}$到$D$的同态映射。则当且仅当${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$是自同构时，我们有$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  =$$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$。

Example 40. Consider ${h}_{1} = \left( {\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right)$ and ${h}_{2} = \left( {\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right)$ in our running example,following the homomorphism notation given in Example 37. We have that ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1}$ is an automorphism,while ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2}$ is not.

示例40. 在连续示例中考虑${h}_{1} = \left( {\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right)$和${h}_{2} = \left( {\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right)$，遵循示例37给出的同态记法。可知${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1}$是自同构，而${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2}$不是。

Now,let $c$ be a prefix constraint on a prefix ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}$ of $L$ ,i.e.,we want to count the number of answers $h$ in ${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$ that satisfy $c$ . Note that this subset of the answers can equivalently be seen as the set of homomorphisms $h$ from ${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$ to ${D}^{c}$ that satisfy $c$ . We denote this set by $\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},c}\right)$ . We also denote by $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ the set of homomorphisms ${h}^{\prime }$ from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ for which ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ is the identity,denoted id,on $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,and ${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ satisfies $c$ . We show that there is a bijection between these two sets of homomorphisms.

现设$c$为$L$的前缀${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{r}$上的约束条件，即我们需要统计${Q}^{c}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$中满足$c$的答案$h$数量。注意该答案子集可等价视为从${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$到${D}^{c}$且满足$c$的同态映射$h$集合，记作$\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},c}\right)$。另记$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$为从${A}_{Q}$到$D$的同态映射${h}^{\prime }$集合，其中${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$在$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$上为恒等映射（记作id），且${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$满足$c$。我们将证明这两组同态映射之间存在双射关系。

CLAIM 3.

$$
\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}}}\right| .
$$

Example 41. In our running example,consider the prefix constraint $c$ assigning $a$ to $x$ . We have that $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }} = \{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) \}$ ,and for the colored version,we have that $\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)  = \{ \left( {a,a,a}\right) ,\left( {a,b,a}\right) \}$ .

示例41. 在连续示例中，考虑将$a$赋给$x$的前缀约束$c$。此时$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }} = \{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) \}$成立，对于着色版本则有$\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)  = \{ \left( {a,a,a}\right) ,\left( {a,b,a}\right) \}$。

Proof of Claim 3. We show a bijection between $\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ and $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ . For every $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,define $P\left( h\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}^{\prime }$ where for every $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,we set ${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)$ . We claim that $P$ is the required bijection.

命题3的证明. 我们构建$\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$与$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$之间的双射关系。对每个$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，定义$P\left( h\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}^{\prime }$：对于任意$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$，设定${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)$。我们断言$P$即为所求的双射映射。

We first show that $P$ is a mapping from $\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ to $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ . We show that ${h}^{\prime }$ is a homomorphism from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ . Consider any relation ${R}^{{A}_{Q}}$ of ${A}_{Q}$ and a tuple $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$ . Then, ${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  = \left( {\left( {{a}_{1},h\left( {a}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{k},h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right) }\right)$ by definition. We have that $\left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in$ ${R}^{{D}^{c}}$ ,because $h$ is a homomorphism. By definition of ${Q}^{c}$ ,we have that ${a}_{i} \in  {R}_{{a}_{i}}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}}$ for all $1 \leq  i \leq$ $k$ . As $h$ is a homomorphism from ${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$ to ${D}^{c}$ ,we have that $h\left( {a}_{i}\right)  \in  {R}_{{a}_{i}}^{{D}_{c}}$ . By definition of $D$ ,we conclude that ${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{D}$ ,and thus ${h}^{\prime }$ is indeed a homomorphism. By definition of ${h}^{\prime }$ , we have ${\pi }_{1}\left( {{h}^{\prime }\left( a\right) }\right)  = a$ for every $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,so ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ is the identity on $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ . Moreover,since $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,we have that $h$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ and with $h = {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ it follows that ${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ satisfies c. So,we have that ${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ .

我们首先证明$P$是从$\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$到$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$的映射。通过定义可知，对于${A}_{Q}$的任意关系${R}^{{A}_{Q}}$和元组$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$，有${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  = \left( {\left( {{a}_{1},h\left( {a}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{k},h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right) }\right)$。由于$h$是同态映射，故$\left( {h\left( {a}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in$${R}^{{D}^{c}}$。根据${Q}^{c}$定义，对所有$1 \leq  i \leq$$k$都满足${a}_{i} \in  {R}_{{a}_{i}}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}}$。由于$h$是从${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$到${D}^{c}$的同态，可得$h\left( {a}_{i}\right)  \in  {R}_{{a}_{i}}^{{D}_{c}}$。根据$D$定义可推出${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{D}$，因此${h}^{\prime }$确为同态。由${h}^{\prime }$定义可知，对每个$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$都存在${\pi }_{1}\left( {{h}^{\prime }\left( a\right) }\right)  = a$，故${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$在$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$上构成恒等映射。此外，由于$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，可证$h$满足$\mathfrak{c}$，结合$h = {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$可推出${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$满足条件c。综上，我们得出${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$。

It remains to show that $P$ is a bijection. To show that $P$ is injective,let ${h}_{1},{h}_{2} \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},c}\right)$ be two different functions. There is $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ such that ${h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)$ . We get that $P\left( {h}_{1}\right) \left( a\right)  =$ $\left( {a,{h}_{1}\left( a\right) }\right)  \neq  \left( {a,{h}_{2}\left( a\right) }\right)  = P\left( {h}_{2}\right) \left( a\right)$ ,so $P\left( {h}_{1}\right)  \neq  P\left( {h}_{2}\right)$ ,and thus $P$ is indeed injective. To show that $P$ is surjective,let ${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ . Then,by definition of $D$ ,there is a function $h : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$ such that for every $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,we have ${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)$ . We claim that $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ . This will prove surjectivity,since $P\left( h\right)  = {h}^{\prime }$ . First note that $h = {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ and,by definition of $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}},{\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ ,so $h$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ . To see that $h$ is a homomorphism,consider first the unary atoms of ${Q}^{c}$ of the form ${R}_{a}\left( a\right)$ . Since for every $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ we have ${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ ,we have by definition of $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ that $h\left( a\right)  \in  {R}_{a}^{{D}^{c}}$ and thus the requirement for homomorphisms is verified for ${R}_{a}$ . Now,let $R$ be one of the other relations of ${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$ ,so $R$ is a relation of ${A}_{Q}$ and ${R}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}} = {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$ . Let $\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$ ,then ${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  = \left( {\left( {{a}_{1},h\left( {a}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{k},h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{D}$ . From the definition of $D$ ,it follows then directly that $h\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{D}^{c}}$ ,which shows that $h$ is a homomorphism as claimed.

尚需证明$P$是一个双射。为证$P$是单射，设${h}_{1},{h}_{2} \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},c}\right)$为两个不同函数，存在$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$使得${h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)$。由此可得$P\left( {h}_{1}\right) \left( a\right)  =$$\left( {a,{h}_{1}\left( a\right) }\right)  \neq  \left( {a,{h}_{2}\left( a\right) }\right)  = P\left( {h}_{2}\right) \left( a\right)$，故$P\left( {h}_{1}\right)  \neq  P\left( {h}_{2}\right)$，因此$P$确为单射。为证$P$是满射，设${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$。根据$D$定义，存在函数$h : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {D}^{c}\right)$使得对任意$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$都有${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)$。我们断言$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$。因$P\left( h\right)  = {h}^{\prime }$，这将证明满射性。首先注意$h = {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$，且由$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}},{\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$定义可知满足$\mathfrak{c}$，故$h$满足$\mathfrak{c}$。验证$h$是同态映射时，先考虑${Q}^{c}$中形如${R}_{a}\left( a\right)$的一元原子。由于对任意$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$都有${h}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = \left( {a,h\left( a\right) }\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$，根据$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$定义可得$h\left( a\right)  \in  {R}_{a}^{{D}^{c}}$，因此${R}_{a}$的同态要求得证。现设$R$为${A}_{{Q}^{c}}$的其他关系之一，则$R$是${A}_{Q}$的关系且${R}^{{A}_{{Q}^{c}}} = {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$。令$\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{A}_{Q}}$，则${h}^{\prime }\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  = \left( {\left( {{a}_{1},h\left( {a}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{k},h\left( {a}_{k}\right) }\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{D}$。由$D$定义可直接推出$h\left( {{a}_{1},\ldots ,{a}_{k}}\right)  \in  {R}^{{D}^{c}}$，这表明$h$确为所声称的同态映射。

We conclude that it is enough to compute $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  = \left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right|$ to answer the counting problem for $\varepsilon$ . To do this,we find its connection to other counting tasks that we can directly compute. Let aut $\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ denote the set of automorphisms of ${A}_{Q}$ that are the identity on all $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ . Moreover,let $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ be the set of homomorphisms ${h}^{\prime }$ from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ such that ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ and ${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ . In particular,if ${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ ,then $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) .$

我们得出结论，计算$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  = \left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right|$足以解决$\varepsilon$的计数问题。为此，我们找到了它与可直接计算的其他计数任务之间的关联。设aut$\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$表示${A}_{Q}$上所有$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$保持恒等的自同构集合。此外，令$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$为满足${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$且${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$符合$\mathfrak{c}$条件的、从${A}_{Q}$到$D$的同态${h}^{\prime }$集合。特别地，若${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$，则$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) .$

CLAIM 4.

$$
\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  = \left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {\operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right| .
$$

Example 42. We have seen that $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  = \{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) \}$ in our running example. We also have that aut $\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)  = \{ \left( {x,y,z}\right) ,\left( {x,z,y}\right) \}$ ,and $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ is $\{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) }\right) \}$ . We get that $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  = 4 = 2 \cdot  2 = \left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {\operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right| .$

示例42. 在运行示例中我们已观察到$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  = \{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) \}$。同时可得aut$\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)  = \{ \left( {x,y,z}\right) ,\left( {x,z,y}\right) \}$，且$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$为$\{ \left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) ,\left( {y,a}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {z,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) }\right) \}$。由此我们得出$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  = 4 = 2 \cdot  2 = \left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {\operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right| .$

Proof of Claim 4. We first show $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }} = \left\{  {h \circ  g \mid  h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }},g \in  }\right.$ $\left. {\operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right\}$ .

引理4的证明。我们首先证明$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }} = \left\{  {h \circ  g \mid  h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }},g \in  }\right.$ $\left. {\operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right\}$。

For the containment " $\supseteq$ ,let $h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ and $g \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ . Note first that $h \circ  g$ is a homomorphism from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ . Next,as ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$ is the identity,we have that ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h \circ  g \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ . By definition of aut $\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,we have ${\left. g\right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) } = \mathrm{{id}}$ ,so ${\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h \circ  g\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }$ ,and ${\pi }_{2} \circ  h \circ  g$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ ,because $h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}}$ . Thus, $h \circ  g \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ ,which proves the containment.

对于包含关系"$\supseteq$"，设$h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$且$g \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$。首先注意到$h \circ  g$是从${A}_{Q}$到$D$的同态映射。其次，由于${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$是恒等映射，可得${\pi }_{1} \circ  h \circ  g \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$。根据自同构(aut)的定义$\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，我们有${\left. g\right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) } = \mathrm{{id}}$，因此${\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h \circ  g\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }$，且${\pi }_{2} \circ  h \circ  g$满足$\mathfrak{c}$，因为$h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}}$。故$h \circ  g \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$，得证包含关系成立。

For the direction " $\subseteq$ ,let ${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ . We define $g \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,so ${g}^{-1}$ is well defined,and ${g}^{-1} \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,since the inverse of an automorphism is an automorphism as well. Now,define $h \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}^{\prime } \circ  {g}^{-1} = {h}^{\prime } \circ  {\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) }^{-1}$ . We claim that $h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,c\right) }^{\text{id }}$ . First,as a composition of homomorphisms, $h$ is a homomorphism as well. Moreover, ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h = \left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }}\right)  \circ  {\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) }^{-1} = \mathrm{{id}}$ . Also,we have that ${g}^{-1}$ is the identity on $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right)$ ,so ${\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \circ  {g}^{-1}\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) }$ . Since ${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ ,we have that ${\pi }_{2} \circ  h$ does too. It follows that $h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ ,as claimed, which proves the containment in the second direction.

对于方向“$\subseteq$”，令${h}^{\prime } \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$。我们定义$g \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，因此${g}^{-1}$是良定义的，且${g}^{-1} \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，因为自同构的逆也是自同构。现定义$h \mathrel{\text{:=}} {h}^{\prime } \circ  {g}^{-1} = {h}^{\prime } \circ  {\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) }^{-1}$。我们断言$h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,c\right) }^{\text{id }}$。首先，作为同态映射的复合，$h$同样是一个同态映射。此外，${\pi }_{1} \circ  h = \left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }}\right)  \circ  {\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) }^{-1} = \mathrm{{id}}$。同时，由于${g}^{-1}$在$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right)$上是恒等映射，故${\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  h\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime } \circ  {g}^{-1}\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) } = {\left. \left( {\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }\right) \right| }_{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) }$。既然${\pi }_{2} \circ  {h}^{\prime }$满足$\mathfrak{c}$，那么${\pi }_{2} \circ  h$亦然。由此可得$h \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$，如所断言，这证明了第二个方向的包含关系。

To prove the claim,we show that for ${h}_{1},{h}_{2} \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$ and ${g}_{1},{g}_{2} \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$ ,whenever ${h}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2}$ or ${g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2}$ ,then ${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$ . Assume first that ${g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2}$ . Since ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1} = {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2} = \mathrm{{id}}$ , we have ${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} = {g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2} = {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$ . So, ${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1}$ and ${h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$ differ in the first coordinate. Now,assume that ${g}_{1} = {g}_{2} = g$ but ${h}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2}$ . Let $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ be such that ${h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)$ . Since $g$ is a bijection,the inverse ${g}^{-1}$ exists and is a bijection as well. Set ${a}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {g}^{-1}\left( a\right)$ . Then, $\left( {{h}_{1} \circ  g}\right) \left( {a}^{\prime }\right)  =$ $\left( {{h}_{1} \circ  g \circ  {g}^{-1}}\right) \left( a\right)  = {h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)  = \left( {{h}_{2} \circ  g \circ  {g}^{-1}}\right) \left( a\right)  = \left( {{h}_{2} \circ  g}\right) \left( {a}^{\prime }\right)$ . So,again, ${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$ .

为证明该主张，我们展示对于${h}_{1},{h}_{2} \in  \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{id }}$和${g}_{1},{g}_{2} \in  \operatorname{aut}\left( {{A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}}\right)$，当${h}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2}$或${g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2}$时，则${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$。首先假设${g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2}$。由于${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1} = {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2} = \mathrm{{id}}$，我们得到${\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} = {g}_{1} \neq  {g}_{2} = {\pi }_{1} \circ  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$。因此，${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1}$与${h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$在第一坐标上存在差异。现假设${g}_{1} = {g}_{2} = g$但${h}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2}$。令$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$满足${h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)$。因$g$为双射，其逆映射${g}^{-1}$存在且同样为双射。设${a}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {g}^{-1}\left( a\right)$，则$\left( {{h}_{1} \circ  g}\right) \left( {a}^{\prime }\right)  =$$\left( {{h}_{1} \circ  g \circ  {g}^{-1}}\right) \left( a\right)  = {h}_{1}\left( a\right)  \neq  {h}_{2}\left( a\right)  = \left( {{h}_{2} \circ  g \circ  {g}^{-1}}\right) \left( a\right)  = \left( {{h}_{2} \circ  g}\right) \left( {a}^{\prime }\right)$。故再次得到${h}_{1} \circ  {g}_{1} \neq  {h}_{2} \circ  {g}_{2}$。

Next,we show that we can compute $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ efficiently.

接下来，我们证明可高效计算$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$。

CLAIM 5. Given an algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ for counting under prefix constraints for $Q$ ,there is an algorithm computing $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ with the following properties:

主张5. 给定针对$Q$的前缀约束计数算法$\mathcal{A}$，存在满足以下性质的算法计算$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$：

- The preprocessing phase is given $D$ (but not $\mathfrak{c}$ ) and runs in time $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ ,making $O\left( 1\right)$ calls to the preprocessing of $\mathcal{A}$ on databases of size $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ .

- 预处理阶段接收$D$（但不含$\mathfrak{c}$），运行时间为$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$，期间调用$\mathcal{A}$的预处理过程$O\left( 1\right)$次，数据库规模为$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$。

- The query phase is given a prefix constraint $\mathfrak{c}$ and computes $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ in time $O\left( 1\right)$ , making $O\left( 1\right)$ calls to the query of $\mathcal{A}$ .

- 查询阶段接收前缀约束$\mathfrak{c}$，并在时间$O\left( 1\right)$内计算出$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$，期间对$\mathcal{A}$的查询进行了$O\left( 1\right)$次调用。

Proof. Given $\mathfrak{c}$ ,let ${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$ be the prefix constraint on the same prefix ${x}_{1},\ldots {x}_{r}$ as $\mathfrak{c}$ ,defined by ${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\lbrack  {\left( {{x}_{i},{y}_{i}^{1}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{i},{y}_{i}^{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack$ where $\mathfrak{c}\left( {x}_{i}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{y}_{i}^{1},{y}_{i}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ . Note that,since we assume $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ to be lexicographically ordered, ${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$ is indeed a prefix constraint for the database $D$ . By definition, $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ is the set of homomorphisms $h$ from ${A}_{Q}$ to $D$ that satisfy ${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$ ,or,equivalently,such that ${\pi }_{2} \circ  h$ satisfies $\mathfrak{c}$ and ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$ is the identity over $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ .

证明。给定$\mathfrak{c}$，令${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$为与$\mathfrak{c}$具有相同前缀${x}_{1},\ldots {x}_{r}$的前缀约束，其定义为<b3></b4>。需注意，由于我们假定$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$是字典序的，${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$确实构成了数据库$D$的前缀约束。根据定义，$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$是从${A}_{Q}$到$D$且满足${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$的同态映射$h$集合，等价于满足$\mathfrak{c}$且${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$在$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$上恒等的映射。

Note that $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }} \subseteq  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ ,but there are homomorphisms $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ that are not in $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ ,because ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$ is not a bijection (see Example 43). This motivates for every subset $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ the definition

注意$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }} \subseteq  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$成立，但存在不属于$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$的同态映射$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$，这是因为${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$不是双射（参见示例43）。这引出了对任意子集$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$的定义动机

$$
{\mathcal{N}}_{T} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)  \mid  \left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \subseteq  T}\right\}  .
$$

The set $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ consists exactly of homomorphisms $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ for which $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right)$ is a bijection,which by Observation 39 is equivalent to $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ .

集合$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$恰好由满足$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right)$为双射的同态映射$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$构成，根据观察39，这等价于$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$。

Example 43. We continue our running example. Given $\mathfrak{c}$ as before,we have that ${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$ is the prefix constraint that assigns(x,a)to $x$ . An example for a homomorphism in $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ but not in $\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$ is $\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {y,c}\right) }\right)$ . This homomorphism is also in ${\mathcal{N}}_{\{ x,y\} }$ and ${N}_{\{ x,y,z\} }$ .

示例43. 我们延续先前的运行示例。给定$\mathfrak{c}$如前所述，我们有${\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }$作为将(x,a)分配给$x$的前缀约束。在$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$中但不在$\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}$中的同态映射示例是$\left( {\left( {x,a}\right) ,\left( {y,b}\right) ,\left( {y,c}\right) }\right)$。该同态映射也存在于${\mathcal{N}}_{\{ x,y\} }$和${N}_{\{ x,y,z\} }$中。

Using the fact that for all $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ ,the restriction of ${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$ to $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ is by definition the identity,we first compute the value $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ based on the $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$ values. To this end, we use the abbreviations $V \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ and $C \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ ,the obvious equality $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  =$ ${\mathcal{N}}_{V} \smallsetminus  \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{x \in  V \smallsetminus  C}}{\mathcal{N}}_{V\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}\right)$ ,and the inclusion-exclusion principle. We obtain

利用对所有$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$而言，${\pi }_{1} \circ  h$到$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$的限制按定义即为恒等式的性质，我们首先根据$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$值计算$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$值。为此，我们采用缩写$V \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$和$C \mathrel{\text{:=}} \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$、明显等式$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  =$${\mathcal{N}}_{V} \smallsetminus  \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{x \in  V \smallsetminus  C}}{\mathcal{N}}_{V\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}\right)$，以及容斥原理。最终得到

$$
\left| {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{x \in  V \smallsetminus  C}}{\mathcal{N}}_{V\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\left| V \smallsetminus  C\right| }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{X \subseteq  V \smallsetminus  C : \left| X\right|  = i}}{\left( -1\right) }^{i - 1}\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{V \smallsetminus  X}\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{C \subseteq  T \varsubsetneq  V}}{\left( -1\right) }^{\left| {V \smallsetminus  T}\right|  - 1}\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right| 
$$

and

及

$$
\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{N}}_{V}\right|  - \left| {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{x \in  V \smallsetminus  C}}{\mathcal{N}}_{V\smallsetminus \{ x\} }}\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{C \subseteq  T \subseteq  V}}{\left( -1\right) }^{\left| V \smallsetminus  T\right| }\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right| .
$$

Setting $v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$ ,this gives

设$v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$，可得

$$
\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }}{\left( -1\right) }^{v - \left| T\right| }\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right| . \tag{5}
$$
