(For the sake of simplicity,in the rest of the proof,we will continue using the notation $v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$ and $r \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }\right|$ . Of course,we have $0 \leq  r \leq  v$ .)

（为简化起见，在证明剩余部分将继续使用记号$v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$和$r \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }\right|$。显然我们有$0 \leq  r \leq  v$。）

The number of summands is bounded by an integer depending only on $v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$ ,so if we can efficiently compute each $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$ for $T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ with $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T$ ,then we are done.

求和项的数量仅取决于$v \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) }\right|$的整数上界，因此若能对满足$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T$条件的$T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$高效计算每个$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$，则问题得解。

Now,for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  {0,v}\right\rbrack$ ,define ${\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ to be the set of homomorphisms $h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$ such that for exactly $i$ elements $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ,we have that $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$ . With this notation, ${\mathcal{N}}_{T} = {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}$ . Then,for every $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$ ,construct a new database ${D}_{T,j}$ over the same relation symbols as $D$ . To this end,for every $a \in  T$ ,introduce $j$ clones ${a}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{a}^{\left( j\right) }$ . Then,the domain of ${D}_{T,j}$ is

对于每一个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {0,v}\right\rbrack$，定义${\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$为同态映射集$h \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},D,{\mathfrak{c}}^{ * }}\right)$，其中恰好存在$i$个元素$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$满足$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$。根据此记法，${\mathcal{N}}_{T} = {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}$。接着，对每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$，构造一个与$D$具有相同关系符号的新数据库${D}_{T,j}$。为此，对每个$a \in  T$引入$j$个克隆体${a}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{a}^{\left( j\right) }$。此时${D}_{T,j}$的定义域为

$$
\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {{a}^{\left( k\right) },b}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right) ,a \in  T,k \in  \left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }\right\}   \cup  \{ \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \mid  a \notin  T\} ,
$$

where we assume the lexicographic order between the new elements as follows:

其中我们假定新元素间按如下字典序排列：

$$
\left( {{a}^{\left( i\right) },b}\right)  \prec  \left( {{c}^{\left( \ell \right) },d}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  i < \ell  \vee  \left( {i = \ell  \land  a{ \prec  }_{L}c}\right)  \vee  \left( {i = \ell  \land  a = c \land  b \prec  d}\right) .
$$

We add the elements in $\{ \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \mid  a \notin  T\}$ in an arbitrary way to this order.

将$\{ \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \mid  a \notin  T\}$中的元素以任意方式加入该排序。

We then define a mapping $B : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \mathcal{P}\left( {\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right) }\right)$ where $\mathcal{P}\left( {\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right) }\right)$ is the power set of $\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right)$ as follows:

随后定义映射$B : \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \rightarrow  \mathcal{P}\left( {\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right) }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{P}\left( {\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right) }\right)$是$\operatorname{dom}\left( {D}_{T,j}\right)$的幂集，定义如下：

$$
B\left( {a,b}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} \left\{  {\left( {{a}^{\left( k\right) },b}\right)  \mid  k \in  \left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }\right\}  , & \text{ if }a \in  T \\  \{ \left( {a,b}\right) \} , & \text{ otherwise. } \end{array}\right. 
$$

Then,for every relation symbol $R$ of $Q$ of arity $s$ ,define

对于每个$Q$的关系符号$R$（其元数为$s$），定义

$$
{R}^{{D}_{T,j}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\left( {\left( {{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{a}_{s},{b}_{s}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{D}}}B\left( {{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  B\left( {{a}_{s},{b}_{s}}\right) .
$$

Define a new prefix constraint ${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$ on the variables $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ by setting for every $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ the intervals ${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }\left( a\right)  = \left\lbrack  {\left( {{a}^{\left( 1\right) },{y}^{1}}\right) ,\left( {{a}^{\left( 1\right) },{y}^{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack$ where $\mathfrak{c}\left( a\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{y}^{1},{y}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ .

通过为每个$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$设置区间${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }\left( a\right)  = \left\lbrack  {\left( {{a}^{\left( 1\right) },{y}^{1}}\right) ,\left( {{a}^{\left( 1\right) },{y}^{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack$（其中$\mathfrak{c}\left( a\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{y}^{1},{y}^{2}}\right\rbrack$），在变量$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$上定义新的前缀约束${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$。

Note that for every $h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ ,since $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T$ ,there are exactly $i - r$ elements $a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ with $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$ . For every $i \in  \left\lbrack  {0,v}\right\rbrack$ and $h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ ,each variable $a \in$ $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ with $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$ can be mapped to one of $j$ copies to get a homomorphism in $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$ . Thus,every $h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ corresponds to exactly ${j}^{i - r}$ homomorphisms ${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$ in $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$ for $\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \in  {\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$ ,defined by the following expression where $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {i - r}\right\rbrack$ :

注意，对于每个$h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$，由于$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T$，恰好存在$i - r$个元素$a \in  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$满足$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$。对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {0,v}\right\rbrack$和$h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$，每个变量$a \in$$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$若满足$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  h}\right) \left( a\right)  \in  T$，可映射到$j$个副本之一，从而在$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$中获得同态。因此，每个$h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$对应恰好${j}^{i - r}$个同态${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$在$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$中（针对$\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \in  {\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$），由以下表达式定义（其中$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {i - r}\right\rbrack$）：

$$
{h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\left( x\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} \left( {{x}^{\left( 1\right) },b}\right) , & \text{ if }x \in  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) \text{ and }h\left( x\right)  = \left( {x,b}\right) , \\  \left( {{a}^{\left( {k}_{\ell }\right) },b}\right) & \text{ if }x\text{ is the }\ell \text{ th element of }{\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  h\right) }^{-1}\left( T\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathrm{c}\right) \text{ and }h\left( x\right)  = \left( {a,b}\right) , \\  h\left( x\right) , & \text{ otherwise,i.e.,if }x \notin  {\left( {\pi }_{1} \circ  h\right) }^{-1}\left( T\right) . \end{array}\right. 
$$

The following two points are immediate:

以下两点显而易见：

- for all homomorphisms $h,{h}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ and tuples $\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)$ and $\left( {{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}\right)$ in ${\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$ such that $\left( {h,{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \neq  \left( {{h}^{\prime },{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,the resulting homomorphisms ${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$ and ${h}_{{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}^{\prime }$ in $\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$ are not equal;

- 对于所有同态$h,{h}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$及元组$\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)$和$\left( {{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}\right)$在${\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$中，若满足$\left( {h,{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \neq  \left( {{h}^{\prime },{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}\right)$，则生成的同态${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$与${h}_{{k}_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}^{\prime }}^{\prime }$在$\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$中不相等；

- every homomorphism $H \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$ is a homomorphism ${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$ for exactly one $i \in  \left\lbrack  {r,v}\right\rbrack$ ,one $h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$ and one tuple $\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \in  {\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$ . (To show this,we have $H = {h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$ ,with each ${k}_{\ell },\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {i - r}\right\rbrack$ determined as follows: If the $\ell$ th element of $\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  }\right.$ $h{)}^{-1}\left( T\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$ is $x$ with $H\left( x\right)  = \left( {{a}^{\left( k\right) },b}\right)$ ,then ${k}_{\ell } \mathrel{\text{:=}} k$ .)

- 每个同态$H \in  \hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right)$都恰好对应一个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {r,v}\right\rbrack$、一个$h \in  {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}$和一个元组$\left( {{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}\right)  \in  {\left\lbrack  j\right\rbrack  }^{i - r}$的同态${h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$。（为证明这一点，我们设$H = {h}_{{k}_{1},\ldots ,{k}_{i - r}}$，其中每个${k}_{\ell },\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {i - r}\right\rbrack$按如下方式确定：若$\left( {{\pi }_{1} \circ  }\right.$$h{)}^{-1}\left( T\right)  \smallsetminus  \operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)$的第$\ell$个元素是$x$且满足$H\left( x\right)  = \left( {{a}^{\left( k\right) },b}\right)$，则${k}_{\ell } \mathrel{\text{:=}} k$。）

Together,the points (1) and (2) imply,for every $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$ ,the equality:

综合来看，要点(1)和(2)意味着对于每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$，都成立以下等式：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = r}}^{v}{j}^{i - r}\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}\right|  = \left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right| . \tag{6}
$$

This is a linear system of $v - r + 1$ equations with $v - r + 1$ unknowns $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}\right|$ ; the coefficients ${j}^{i - r}$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  {r,v}\right\rbrack$ and $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$ ,form a square Vandermonde matrix,which is therefore invertible. For each fixed $T$ ,this allows to compute the values $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}\right|$ (and,in particular,the value $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$ ,which interests us) in time $O\left( {\left( v - r + 1\right) }^{3}\right)$ ,which is constant time,if the $v - r + 1$ values $\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$ are known.

这是一个包含$v - r + 1$个方程和$v - r + 1$个未知数$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}\right|$的线性系统；其系数${j}^{i - r}$（其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  {r,v}\right\rbrack$和$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$）构成可逆的范德蒙方阵。对于每个固定的$T$，这使我们能在$O\left( {\left( v - r + 1\right) }^{3}\right)$时间内计算出$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,i}\right|$的值（特别地，我们关注的值$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$），若已知$v - r + 1$个$\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$值，则该计算时间为常数级。

Since the values $\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$ are just counting queries under a prefix constraint,we can compute them using the algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ . We note that,by the definition of the relations ${R}^{{D}_{T,j}}$ ,each database ${D}_{T,j}$ with $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$ has size at most ${j}^{\alpha }\left| D\right|$ where $\alpha$ is the maximum arity of the relations, so $\left| {D}_{T,j}\right|  = O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and can be easily constructed from $D$ in linear time.

由于$\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$值仅是前缀约束下的计数查询，我们可以使用算法$\mathcal{A}$进行计算。需注意的是，根据关系${R}^{{D}_{T,j}}$的定义，每个满足$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$的数据库${D}_{T,j}$其大小至多为${j}^{\alpha }\left| D\right|$（其中$\alpha$是关系的最大元数），因此$\left| {D}_{T,j}\right|  = O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$且可从$D$在线性时间内轻松构建。

To summarize,we can compute the required value $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,c\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ by the following two-phase algorithm:

综上所述，我们可通过以下两阶段算法计算出所需值$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,c\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$：

- Preprocessing (independent of the prefix constraint): Compute the databases ${D}_{T,j}$ for all $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ and $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$ ,and apply the preprocessing phase of $\mathcal{A}$ to each ${D}_{T,j}$ ;

- 预处理阶段（独立于前缀约束）：为所有$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq  T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$和$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v + 1}\right\rbrack$计算数据库${D}_{T,j}$，并对每个${D}_{T,j}$应用$\mathcal{A}$的预处理流程；

- Query phase: Read the prefix constraint $\mathfrak{c}$ and turn it into ${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$ ; compute $\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$ by applying the query phase of $\mathcal{A}$ to ${D}_{T,j}$ and ${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$ for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$ and all $\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq$ $T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$ ; for each such $T$ ,compute (from the $\left| {\hom \left( {A}_{Q}\right) ,{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right|$ values) the (unique) solution $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$ of the linear system of Equations (6); finally,from the $\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$ values, compute $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ by Equation (5).

- 查询阶段：读取前缀约束$\mathfrak{c}$并转换为${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$；通过将$\mathcal{A}$的查询流程应用于${D}_{T,j}$和${\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }$，计算所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  {v - r + 1}\right\rbrack$与$\operatorname{var}\left( \mathfrak{c}\right)  \subseteq$$T \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)$对应的$\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{Q},{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right) }\right|$；针对每个$T$，根据$\left| {\hom \left( {A}_{Q}\right) ,{D}_{T,j},{\mathfrak{c}}^{* * }}\right|$值求解线性方程组(6)的（唯一）解$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T,v}\right|  = \left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$；最后根据$\left| {\mathcal{N}}_{T}\right|$值，通过公式(5)计算$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$。

Let us analyze the complexity of the above algorithm: The number of calls to the preprocessing phase and the query phase of $\mathcal{A}$ made by the above algorithm is the number of pairs(T,j)involved, which depends only on $Q$ . Therefore,the preprocessing time of the algorithm is $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and its query time is constant (both when ignoring the runtime of the calls to $\mathcal{A}$ ).

算法复杂度分析：上述算法调用$\mathcal{A}$预处理和查询阶段的次数等于涉及的(T,j)对数，该数值仅取决于$Q$。因此算法预处理时间为$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$，查询时间为常数（均不考虑$\mathcal{A}$调用的耗时）。

We can now complete the proof of Lemma 36. From Claims 3 and 4, we get that what we need to compute is

现在可以完成引理36的证明。根据断言3和4，我们需要计算的是

$$
\left| {\hom \left( {{A}_{{Q}^{c}},{D}^{c},\mathfrak{c}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{id}}}}\right|  = \frac{\left| \hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\mathrm{{aut}}}\right| }{\left| \operatorname{aut}\left( {A}_{Q},\mathfrak{c}\right) \right| }.
$$

From Claim 5, $\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$ can be computed efficiently. Since we work in data complexity, we can easily compute aut $\left( {{A}_{Q},c}\right)$ : The value only depends on ${A}_{Q}$ and the length $r$ of the prefix that $c$ is on,so we can simply compute it for every possible value of $r$ . This completes the proof of Lemma 36.

由断言5可知，$\left| {\hom {\left( {A}_{Q},D,\mathfrak{c}\right) }^{\text{aut }}}\right|$可被高效计算。由于在数据复杂度框架下，aut$\left( {{A}_{Q},c}\right)$的计算是直接的：该值仅取决于${A}_{Q}$及$c$所处前缀的长度$r$，因此只需枚举所有可能的$r$值计算即可。至此完成引理36的证明。

### 6.4 Assembling the Proof

### 6.4 整合证明

With all the components at hand, we can now prove Theorem 33.

整合所有组件后，现在可以证明定理33。

Proof of Theorem 33. By Proposition 35,a lexicographic direct access algorithm for $Q$ and $L$ implies an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for $Q$ and $L$ . By Lemma 36,this implies an algorithm for counting under prefix constraints for ${Q}^{c}$ and $L$ . By Proposition 35,this implies a lexicographic direct access algorithm for ${Q}^{c}$ and $L$ . By Lemma 34,this finally implies a lexicographic direct access algorithm for ${Q}^{\text{sf }}$ and $L$ . Each step increases the preprocessing time only by constant factors, and the access/counting is increased by a logarithmic factor whenever we use Proposition 35. This proves the non-trivial direction of the theorem.

定理33证明。根据命题35，针对$Q$和$L$的字典序直接访问算法可推导出前缀约束下的计数算法；通过引理36，该算法可推广至${Q}^{c}$和$L$；再次应用命题35，可得到${Q}^{c}$和$L$的字典序直接访问算法；最后由引理34推导出${Q}^{\text{sf }}$和$L$的字典序直接访问算法。各步骤仅以常数倍增加预处理时间，而使用命题35时访问/计数复杂度增加对数因子。由此证得定理33的非平凡方向。

## 7 Tight Bounds for Direct Access

## 7 直接访问的紧确界

In this section, we formulate and prove the main result of this article on direct access, showing that the incompatibility number determines the required preprocessing time, even for queries with self-joins.

本节阐述并证明本文关于直接访问的核心结论：即使存在自连接查询，不兼容数仍决定所需的预处理时间。

THEOREM 44. Let $Q$ be a join query,let $L$ be an ordering of its variables,and let $\iota$ be the incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ .

定理44. 设$Q$为一个连接查询，$L$为其变量的排序，$\iota$表示$Q$与$L$的不兼容数。

- There is an algorithm that allows lexicographic direct access with respect to the order induced by $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$ and logarithmic access time.

- 存在一种算法，在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$和对数访问时间下，可实现基于$L$诱导顺序的字典序直接访问。

- Assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture,there is no constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an algorithm allowing lexicographic direct access with respect to the order induced by $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ .

- 在零团猜想成立的前提下，不存在常数$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对于所有$\delta  > 0$，都存在算法能在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$和访问时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$内实现基于$L$诱导顺序的字典序直接访问。

Proof. The first part was shown in Theorem 10. Let us now prove the second part first for $\iota  > 1$ . If there is an $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ we have direct access for $Q$ and $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ ,then by Theorem 33,there is a similar algorithm for a self-join-free version of $Q$ . By Lemma 17,there is a $k \geq  2$ and ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$ such that for all ${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ the star query ${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$ with respect to a bad order admits direct access with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ . Combining Proposition 19 and Lemma 22,we obtain that $k$ -Set-Disjointness has an algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\right)$ and query time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)  = O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{{\delta }^{\prime \prime }}}\right)$ for ${\delta }^{\prime \prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} 2{\delta }^{\prime }$ . The quantifiers on ${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$ and ${\delta }^{\prime \prime }$ match Theorem 23,which implies that the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ - Clique Conjecture is false, in particular, the Zero-Clique Conjecture is false.

证明. 第一部分已在定理10中证明。现首先针对$\iota  > 1$证明第二部分。若存在$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对所有$\delta  > 0$，我们能在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$和访问时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$内实现对$Q$和$L$的直接访问，则由定理33可知，存在针对$Q$无自连接版本的类似算法。根据引理17，存在$k \geq  2$和${\varepsilon }^{\prime } > 0$使得对所有${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$，劣序下的星型查询${Q}_{k}^{ \star  }$可在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right)$和访问时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$内实现直接访问。结合命题19和引理22，我们得出$k$-集合不相交问题存在预处理时间$O\left( {\parallel I{\parallel }^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}}\right)$和查询时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}}\right)  = O\left( {\parallel \mathcal{I}{\parallel }^{{\delta }^{\prime \prime }}}\right)$的算法（针对${\delta }^{\prime \prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} 2{\delta }^{\prime }$）。量词${\varepsilon }^{\prime }$与${\delta }^{\prime \prime }$的表述与定理23相符，这意味着零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想不成立，特别地，零团猜想被证伪。

Since the incompatibility number is always at least 1,it remains to prove the second part for $\iota  = 1$ . Here, we start from the well-known fact that searching in an unordered array requires linear time. More precisely,given an array $A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$ with $\{ 0,1\}$ -entries,deciding whether $A$ contains a 0 requires time $\Omega \left( n\right)$ ,and this holds for deterministic algorithms as well as for randomized algorithms (with success probability at least $2/3$ ). We reduce this problem to direct access for $Q$ to show a linear lower bound on the preprocessing time. To this end,given an array $A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$ with $\{ 0,1\}$ -entries,we construct a database $D$ with $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  = \left\lbrack  {2n}\right\rbrack$ . Set $B \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ i + A\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   \cdot  n \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \}$ . For any relation symbol $R$ of $Q$ ,we set ${R}^{D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ \left( {b,\ldots ,b}\right)  \mid  b \in  B\}$ . Note that every $\left( {b,\ldots ,b}\right)$ with $b \in  B$ is a solution to $Q\left( D\right)$ ,but there can also be other solutions. In any case,the lexicographically smallest solution to $Q\left( D\right)$ is the tuple $\left( {{b}^{ * },\ldots ,{b}^{ * }}\right)$ with ${b}^{ * } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( B\right)$ . Observe that ${b}^{ * } \leq  n$ if and only if $A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$ has a 0 -entry,so we can read off whether array $A$ has a 0 -entry from ${b}^{ * }$ . Hence,by preprocessing $D$ and then using one direct access to determine the lexicographically smallest solution, we can decide whether the array $A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$ contains a 0 -entry. Note that this reduction is implicit,meaning we do not need to explicitly compute the database $D$ . Instead,when the preprocessing or direct access algorithm reads an entry of a relation ${R}^{D}$ ,we can compute this entry on the fly in constant time by reading an entry $A\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$ . Therefore,a direct access algorithm for $Q$ with preprocessing time $o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ would imply that we can search in an unordered array in time $o\left( n\right)$ . Since the latter is impossible,there is no direct access algorithm for $Q$ with preprocessing time $o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ and access time $o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ . In particular,for any $\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ and $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1 - \varepsilon$ there is no direct access algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ . This lower bound is unconditional, proving the second part for $\iota  = 1$ .

由于不兼容数始终至少为1，接下来只需证明$\iota  = 1$的第二部分。此处我们从公认事实出发：无序数组搜索需要线性时间。更准确地说，给定含有$\{ 0,1\}$个元素的数组$A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$，判断$A$是否包含0需要$\Omega \left( n\right)$时间，这适用于确定性算法和随机算法（成功概率至少为$2/3$）。我们将此问题归约到$Q$的直接访问，以证明预处理时间的线性下界。具体而言，给定含$\{ 0,1\}$个元素的数组$A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$，我们构建含$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  = \left\lbrack  {2n}\right\rbrack$的数据库$D$。设$B \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ i + A\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   \cdot  n \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \}$。对于$Q$的任何关系符号$R$，设${R}^{D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ \left( {b,\ldots ,b}\right)  \mid  b \in  B\}$。注意每个满足$b \in  B$的$\left( {b,\ldots ,b}\right)$都是$Q\left( D\right)$的解，但也可能存在其他解。无论如何，$Q\left( D\right)$的字典序最小解是含${b}^{ * } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( B\right)$的元组$\left( {{b}^{ * },\ldots ,{b}^{ * }}\right)$。观察到${b}^{ * } \leq  n$当且仅当$A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$含有0元素，因此我们可以从${b}^{ * }$读取数组$A$是否含0元素。因此，通过预处理$D$后执行一次直接访问确定字典序最小解，即可判断数组$A\left\lbrack  {1..n}\right\rbrack$是否含0元素。注意该归约是隐式的——我们无需显式计算数据库$D$，当预处理或直接访问算法读取关系${R}^{D}$的条目时，可通过读取$A\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$在常数时间内动态计算该条目。因此，若存在预处理时间$o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$且访问时间$o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$的$Q$直接访问算法，则意味着我们能在$o\left( n\right)$时间内完成无序数组搜索。由于后者不可能实现，故不存在预处理时间$o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$且访问时间$o\left( \left| D\right| \right)$的$Q$直接访问算法。特别地，对任意$\varepsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$和$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} 1 - \varepsilon$，都不存在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$且访问时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$的直接访问算法。该下界是无条件的，由此证得$\iota  = 1$的第二部分。

Note that for every $\delta ,{\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ ,we have $O\left( {\left( \log \left| D\right| \right) }^{\delta }\right)  \leq  O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ . Therefore,if for some $\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$ there was an algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left( \log \left| D\right| \right) }^{\delta }\right)$ ,then for every ${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ there would also be an algorithm with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ . Thus,Theorem 44 implies the lower bound for polylogarithmic query time stated in the introduction.

注意到对于每个$\delta ,{\delta }^{\prime } > 0$，都存在$O\left( {\left( \log \left| D\right| \right) }^{\delta }\right)  \leq  O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$。因此，若对某个$\varepsilon ,\delta  > 0$存在预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 - \varepsilon }\right)$、访问时间为$O\left( {\left( \log \left| D\right| \right) }^{\delta }\right)$的算法，则对任意${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$也都存在预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{t - \varepsilon }\right)$、访问时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$的算法。由此可见，定理44直接导出了引言中所述关于多对数查询时间的下界。

## 8 Relaxed Orders and Projections

## 8 松弛序与投影查询

So far, we have considered the case where the underlying order of the results is lexicographic and specified by the user as part of the problem definition. We now consider the relaxation of this requirement, when the order is either: (1) completely flexible; (2) has to be lexicographic, but the ordering of the variables is flexible; or (3) the answers must be ordered lexicographically by some of the variables, and breaking ties originating from the assignments to the other variables can be done in an arbitrary order. We will see that such relaxations allow to reduce the preprocessing time. We also briefly discuss how to support queries with projections.

此前我们讨论的情况要求结果必须遵循用户指定的字典序作为问题定义的一部分。现在我们将放宽该要求，考虑以下情形：(1) 顺序完全自由；(2) 必须保持字典序但变量排序可调；(3) 结果需按部分变量字典序排列，其余变量赋值产生的平局可任意排序。我们将看到这种松弛能降低预处理时间。同时简要讨论如何支持含投影的查询。

### 8.1 Unconstrained Lexicographic Orders

### 8.1 无约束字典序

From Theorem 44, we can also get a lower bound for direct access for unconstrained lexicographic order based on fractional hypertree width.

根据定理44，我们还能基于分数超树宽度得到无约束字典序直接访问的下界。

Proposition 45. The fractional hypertree width of a hypergraph $H$ is the minimum incompatibility number of $H$ minimized over all orders $L$ .

命题45. 超图$H$的分数超树宽度等于在所有序$L$上最小化的$H$不兼容数。

Proof. First,we claim that,for any hypertree decomposition $\mathcal{D}$ ,there exists an order $L$ of the variables,such that $\mathcal{D}$ and $L$ have no disruptive trio. Indeed,since $\mathcal{D}$ is acyclic,it has an elimination order, and the reverse of an elimination order is known to not have disruptive trios. From this claim, we conclude that by considering all permutations $L$ and all hypertree decompositions that do not have a disruptive trio with respect to $L$ ,we get all hypertree decompositions of $H$ .

证明. 首先我们断言：对任意超树分解$\mathcal{D}$，存在变量序$L$使得$\mathcal{D}$与$L$不构成破坏性三元组。由于$\mathcal{D}$是无环的，必存在消解序，而消解序的逆序已知不会形成破坏性三元组。由此可得，通过考察所有置换$L$及与之无破坏性三元组的超树分解，就能穷尽$H$的所有超树分解。

Denote the set of all permutations of the vertices by $P$ and the set of all hypertree decompositions of $H$ by $D$ . Given $\mathcal{D} \in  D$ and $L \in  P$ ,denote the fractional width of $D$ by ${w}_{\mathcal{D}}$ ,the set of all hypertree decompositions of $H$ that have no disruptive trio with respect to $L$ by ${D}_{L}$ ,and the incompatibility number of $\mathcal{D}$ and $L$ by $\iota \left( {\mathcal{D},L}\right)$ . Denote also the fractional hypertree width of $H$ by $w$ . Then,

记顶点全排列集合为$P$，$H$的所有超树分解集合为$D$。给定$\mathcal{D} \in  D$和$L \in  P$时，记$D$的分数宽度为${w}_{\mathcal{D}}$，与$L$无破坏性三元组的超树分解集合为${D}_{L}$，$\mathcal{D}$与$L$的不兼容数为$\iota \left( {\mathcal{D},L}\right)$。另记$H$的分数超树宽度为$w$。则有：

$$
w = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\mathcal{D} \in  D}}{w}_{\mathcal{D}} = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{L \in  P}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\mathcal{D} \in  {D}_{L}}}{w}_{\mathcal{D}} = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{L \in  P,\mathcal{D} \in  D}}\iota \left( {\mathcal{D},L}\right) .
$$

Here, the first equality is the definition of fractional hypertree width, the second equality follows from the claim we just proved, and the third equality follows from Proposition 14.

此处第一个等式是分数超树宽度的定义，第二个等式基于我们刚证明的断言，第三个等式由命题14得出。

From Theorem 44 and Proposition 45, we get the following corollary that essentially says that if we allow any lexicographic order for direct access, then the fractional hypertree width determines the exponent of the preprocessing time:

综合定理44与命题45，我们得到以下推论，其核心含义是：若允许任意字典序直接访问，则分数超树宽度决定了预处理时间的指数阶：

COROLLARY 46. Let $Q$ be a join query of fractional hypertree width $w$ .

推论46. 设$Q$为分数超树宽度$w$的连接查询。

- There is an ordering $L$ of the variables of $Q$ such that there is an algorithm that allows lexicographic direct access with respect to the order induced by $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{w}\right)$ and logarithmic access time.

- 存在$Q$变量的一个排序$L$，使得算法能以$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{w}\right)$预处理时间和对数访问时间支持$L$诱导的字典序直接访问。

- Assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture,there are no order $L$ and constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an algorithm allowing lexicographic direct access with respect to the order induced by $L$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{w - \varepsilon }\right)$ and access time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ .

- 假设零团猜想成立，不存在阶数$L$和常数$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对于所有$\delta  > 0$，存在一个算法能在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{w - \varepsilon }\right)$和访问时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$内实现关于$L$诱导序的字典序直接访问。

### 8.2 Unconstrained Orders

### 8.2 无约束序

As we have seen, the complexity of direct access for join queries depends strongly on the order in which we want the query results to be represented. Moreover, we know from Corollary 46 that if we insist on lexicographic orders, then the preprocessing time necessarily degrades with the fractional hypertree width. The next natural question is whether we can get a more efficient algorithm in case we have no constraints on the order at all.

如前所述，连接查询直接访问的复杂度很大程度上取决于我们希望查询结果呈现的序关系。此外，根据推论46可知，若坚持使用字典序，预处理时间必然会随着分数超树宽度而劣化。接下来的核心问题是：当完全不限制序关系时，能否获得更高效的算法。

Let us discuss a model in which we do not require any particular order on the answers in direct access. That is,we only require that there is a bijection $b : \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  Q\left( D\right)$ where $s \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {Q\left( D\right) }\right|$ such that,given a query integer $i \in  \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack$ ,we can efficiently compute the answer $b\left( i\right)$ . We call this model orderless direct access. It is again convenient to extend $b$ to the domain $\mathbb{N}$ ,defining $b\left( i\right)$ to be some fixed error value whenever $i \notin  \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack$ . We again measure the preprocessing time,i.e.,the time to prepare suitable data structures given a database $D$ ,and the query time,which is the time to compute the answer $b\left( i\right)$ given $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ using these data structures. We will see that there are queries in which orderless direct access can be performed in a more efficient manner than direct access with a lexicographic order, proving the following proposition:

让我们讨论一种在直接访问中不要求特定答案序的模型。即仅需存在一个双射$b : \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  Q\left( D\right)$（其中$s \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| {Q\left( D\right) }\right|$），使得给定查询整数$i \in  \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack$时可高效计算出答案$b\left( i\right)$。我们称此模型为无序直接访问。同样可将$b$扩展至域$\mathbb{N}$，当$i \notin  \left\lbrack  s\right\rbrack$时将$b\left( i\right)$定义为固定错误值。我们仍以预处理时间（即给定数据库$D$后准备数据结构的时间）和查询时间（即利用这些数据结构根据$i \in  \mathbb{N}$计算答案$b\left( i\right)$的时间）作为衡量标准。后续将证明存在某些查询，其无序直接访问效率高于任何字典序直接访问，从而验证下列命题：

Proposition 47. Assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture, there exists a query for which orderless direct access can be done with a lower complexity compared to direct access in any lexicographic order.

命题47. 假设零团猜想成立，存在某些查询的无序直接访问复杂度低于任何字典序直接访问。

To prove this, we consider the 4-cycle query

为证明此命题，我们考察4环查询

$$
{Q}^{ \circ  }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) .
$$

It is easy to verify that the query ${Q}^{ \circ  }$ has fractional hypertree width 2 . It follows from Corollary 46 that for any direct access algorithm with a lexicographic order and polylogarithmic access time, the preprocessing time must be essentially quadratic. We next show that this preprocessing time can be improved if we drop the requirement of having a lexicographic order.

易验证查询${Q}^{ \circ  }$的分数超树宽度为2。根据推论46可知，对于任何具有字典序且访问时间为多对数的直接访问算法，其预处理时间本质上必须是平方级。接下来我们将证明，若放弃字典序要求，可改进此预处理时间。

LEMMA 48. There is an algorithm for orderless direct access for ${Q}^{ \circ  }$ with logarithmic access time and preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ .

引理48. 存在针对${Q}^{ \circ  }$的无序直接访问算法，其访问时间为对数级，预处理时间为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$。

Proof. We use a decomposition technique based on degree information as pioneered in Reference [5]. To this end,we split every relation ${R}_{i}^{D}$ into a heavy part ${R}_{i}^{h}$ and a light part ${R}_{i}^{\ell }$ as follows:

证明. 采用基于度数信息的分解技术（参见文献[5]首创）。具体而言，将每个关系${R}_{i}^{D}$划分为重部分${R}_{i}^{h}$和轻部分${R}_{i}^{\ell }$：

$$
{R}_{i}^{\ell } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D} \mid  {\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( a\right)  \leq  {\left| {R}_{i}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}}\right\}  ,
$$

$$
{R}_{i}^{h} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D} \mid  {\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( a\right)  > {\left| {R}_{i}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}}\right\}  ,
$$

where ${\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( a\right)$ denotes the degree of $a$ in ${R}_{i}^{D}$ ,i.e.,

其中${\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( a\right)$表示$a$在${R}_{i}^{D}$中的度数，即

$$
{\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( a\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left| \left\{  {b \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{i}^{D}}\right\}  \right| .
$$

Denote by ${D}^{ * }$ the database we get from $D$ by adding all these relations. Note that $\left| {D}^{ * }\right|  = \Theta \left( \left| D\right| \right)$ . Clearly, ${D}^{ * }$ can be computed in time $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ by sorting the tuples in $D$ in linear time and computing the degrees.

记${D}^{ * }$为通过添加所有这些关系从$D$得到的数据库。注意$\left| {D}^{ * }\right|  = \Theta \left( \left| D\right| \right)$。显然，${D}^{ * }$可在$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$时间内通过线性时间排序元组并计算度数得到。

We have that ${R}_{i}^{D} = {R}_{i}^{\ell } \uplus  {R}_{i}^{h}$ where $\uplus$ denotes disjoint union. Thus,we can rewrite the query ${Q}^{ \circ  }$ as follows,where $\bowtie$ denotes a join:

我们有${R}_{i}^{D} = {R}_{i}^{\ell } \uplus  {R}_{i}^{h}$，其中$\uplus$表示不相交并集。因此，可以将查询${Q}^{ \circ  }$重写如下，其中$\bowtie$表示连接操作：

$$
{Q}^{ \circ  } \equiv  \left( {{R}_{1}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \uplus  {R}_{1}^{h}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) }\right)  \bowtie  \left( {{R}_{2}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \uplus  {R}_{2}^{h}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \bowtie  \left( {{R}_{3}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \uplus  {R}_{3}^{h}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \bowtie  \left( {{R}_{4}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  \uplus  {R}_{4}^{h}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \equiv  \; + \;{R}_{1}^{{o}_{1}}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}^{{o}_{3}}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) 
$$

$$
 \equiv  \underset{\left( {{o1},{o2},{o3},{o4}}\right)  \in  \{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}}{ + }{Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,
$$

where for every tuple $\left( {{o1},{o2},{o3},{o4}}\right)  \in  \{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$ ,we define

其中对于每个元组$\left( {{o1},{o2},{o3},{o4}}\right)  \in  \{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$，我们定义

$$
{Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}^{{o}_{1}}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}^{{o}_{3}}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) .
$$

We can evaluate each individual ${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$ efficiently.

我们可以高效地评估每个单独的${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$。

CLAIM 6. For all $\left( {{o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}}\right)  \in  \{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$ ,there is an algorithm for direct access on ${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$ with logarithmic access time and preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ .

声明6. 对于所有$\left( {{o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}}\right)  \in  \{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$，存在一种对数访问时间和预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$的直接访问算法，适用于${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$。

Proof. The idea is to rewrite ${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$ into an acyclic query on a database of size $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ . We consider three cases.

证明。思路是将${Q}^{\left( o1,o2,o3,o4\right) }$重写为对大小为$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$的数据库进行无环查询。我们考虑三种情况。

Case 1 - there exists $i \in  \{ 1,2\}$ such that ${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2} = \ell$ : We assume that w.l.o.g. $i = 1$ ,since the other case is completely analogous. We rewrite ${Q}^{\left( \ell ,o2,\ell ,o4\right) }$ into an acyclic query as follows: Let ${S}_{1}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  :  - {R}_{1}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$ and ${S}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  :  - {R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{3}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)$ and define a new database ${D}^{\prime }$ with two relations ${S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$ and ${S}_{2}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{2}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$ . Finally,we define the query ${Q}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{S}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)$ . Then,by definition, ${Q}^{\left( \ell ,o2,\ell ,o4\right) }\left( {D}^{ * }\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ , so it suffices to have an algorithm with direct access for ${Q}^{\prime }$ such as those from References $\left\lbrack  {{13},{19}}\right\rbrack$ . It only remains to show that ${D}^{\prime }$ can be computed in time $O\left( \left| {D}^{3/2}\right| \right)$ . We only show this for the computation of ${S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ ,as the case for ${S}_{2}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ is completely analogous. In the join ${R}_{1}^{\ell } \bowtie  {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}$ , every tuple $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {\left( {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$ can by construction of ${\left( {R}_{1}^{\ell }\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$ only be extended in at most ${\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ ways,so overall $\left| {S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  \left| {R}_{4}^{{D}^{ * }}\right|  \cdot  {\left| {R}_{1}^{{D}^{ * }}\right| }^{1/2} \leq  {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}$ and the relation can again be computed efficiently.

案例1 - 存在$i \in  \{ 1,2\}$使得${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2} = \ell$：我们假设不失一般性(w.l.o.g.)$i = 1$，因为另一种情况完全类似。我们将${Q}^{\left( \ell ,o2,\ell ,o4\right) }$重写为无环查询如下：设${S}_{1}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  :  - {R}_{1}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$和${S}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  :  - {R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{3}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)$，并定义包含两个关系${S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$和${S}_{2}^{{D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{2}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$的新数据库${D}^{\prime }$。最后定义查询${Q}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{S}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)$。根据定义，${Q}^{\left( \ell ,o2,\ell ,o4\right) }\left( {D}^{ * }\right)  = {Q}^{\prime }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$成立，因此只需为${Q}^{\prime }$设计直接访问算法（如参考文献$\left\lbrack  {{13},{19}}\right\rbrack$中的算法）。剩下只需证明${D}^{\prime }$可在$O\left( \left| {D}^{3/2}\right| \right)$时间内计算完成。我们仅展示${S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }}$的计算过程，因${S}_{2}^{{D}^{\prime }}$的情况完全类似。在连接运算${R}_{1}^{\ell } \bowtie  {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}$中，根据${\left( {R}_{1}^{\ell }\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$的构造，每个元组$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {\left( {R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$最多只有${\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$种扩展方式，因此总体$\left| {S}_{1}^{{D}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  \left| {R}_{4}^{{D}^{ * }}\right|  \cdot  {\left| {R}_{1}^{{D}^{ * }}\right| }^{1/2} \leq  {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}$，该关系仍可高效计算。

Case 2 - there exists $i \in  \{ 1,2\}$ such that ${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2} = h$ : We only consider the case $i = 1$ ; the other case is analogous. As before,we show how ${Q}^{\left( h,o2,h,o4\right) }$ can be rewritten into an acyclic query,but we regroup the atoms differently. We define ${S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}^{h}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ and ${S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{3}^{h}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$ . Then,we define ${Q}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$ and construct a database ${D}^{\prime }$ by setting ${S}_{1}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$ and ${S}_{2}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{2}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$ . Reasoning similarly to before,it only remains to show that ${D}^{\prime }$ can be constructed in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ . To this end,remark that every element in ${\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$ appears in at least ${\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ tuples of ${R}_{1}^{D}$ ,so $\left| {{\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}}\right|  \leq  {\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ . We get that $\left| {S}_{1}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  \left| {{\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {R}_{2}^{{D}^{ * }}\right|  \leq  {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}$ and the relation can easily be computed in the claimed time.

情形2 - 存在$i \in  \{ 1,2\}$使得${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2} = h$成立：我们仅考虑$i = 1$的情况，另一种情况可类推。同前所述，我们将展示如何将${Q}^{\left( h,o2,h,o4\right) }$重写为非循环查询，但采用不同的原子重组方式。定义${S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}^{h}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{2}^{{o}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$与${S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{3}^{h}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{{o}_{4}}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$后，继而定义${Q}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$并通过设定${S}_{1}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{1}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$和${S}_{2}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {S}_{2}\left( {D}^{ * }\right)$构建数据库${D}^{\prime }$。沿用先前推理思路，只需证明${D}^{\prime }$可在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$时间内构建完成。为此需注意：${\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}$中每个元素至少出现在${\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$个${R}_{1}^{D}$的元组中，故$\left| {{\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}}\right|  \leq  {\left| {R}_{1}^{D}\right| }^{1/2}$。由此可得$\left| {S}_{1}^{\prime {D}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  \left| {{\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{\left( {R}_{1}^{h}\right) }^{{D}^{ * }}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {R}_{2}^{{D}^{ * }}\right|  \leq  {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}$，且该关系可在所述时间内轻松计算得出。

Case 3 - there is no $i \in  \{ 1,2\}$ such that ${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2}$ : In that case,(o1,o2,o3,o4)must be a cyclic shift of $\left( {\ell ,\ell ,h,h}\right)$ ,so we only consider that specific index tuple,as all other cases are completely analogous. We decompose the same way as in Case 2. We only have to show that the relations ${S}_{1}^{\prime }{}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ and ${S}_{2}^{\prime }{}^{{D}^{\prime }}$ can be computed in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ . For ${S}_{2}^{\prime }$ ,this follows immediately as in Case 2, since ${S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  :  - {R}_{3}^{h}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{h}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$ . For ${S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  :  - {R}_{1}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{2}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ ,the reasoning is analogous to Case 1.

情形3 - 不存在$i \in  \{ 1,2\}$使得${o}_{i} = {o}_{i + 2}$：在此情况下，(o1,o2,o3,o4)必然是$\left( {\ell ,\ell ,h,h}\right)$的循环移位，因此我们仅需考虑该特定索引元组，因为其他所有情形完全类似。我们按照情形2的方式进行分解。只需证明关系${S}_{1}^{\prime }{}^{{D}^{\prime }}$和${S}_{2}^{\prime }{}^{{D}^{\prime }}$可在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$时间内计算。对于${S}_{2}^{\prime }$，由于${S}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  :  - {R}_{3}^{h}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{4}^{h}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$，其证明过程与情形2完全相同。对于${S}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  :  - {R}_{1}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{2}^{\ell }\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$，推理过程与情形1类似。

Applying one of the direct-access algorithms from References [13, 19] in all cases yields the claim for all(o1,o2,o3,o4).

在所有情况下应用参考文献[13,19]中的直接访问算法之一，即可为所有(o1,o2,o3,o4)得出主张结论。

We now complete the proof of Lemma 48. We order $\{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$ in an arbitrary way. Then,we order the answers in ${Q}^{ \circ  }\left( D\right)$ by first ordering them with respect to which ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ they appear in and then with respect to the order of the algorithm from Claim 6. This is the order that our algorithm for ${Q}^{ \circ  }$ uses,working in the following way: In the preprocessing phase,we first compute ${D}^{\prime }$ and then perform the preprocessing for all ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$ on input ${D}^{\prime }$ . We also compute the values $\left| {{Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$ for all queries ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$ ,which we can do with a simple binary search using the respective direct access algorithms. Using these answer counts,given $j \in  \mathbb{N}$ in the query phase,we first compute in which ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ the $j$ th answer lies and compute the corresponding index ${j}^{\prime }$ for which the ${j}^{\prime }$ th answer in ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$ is the $j$ th answer in ${Q}^{ \circ  }\left( D\right)$ . Finally,we use the direct access algorithm for ${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$ to compute the desired answer.

现在完成引理48的证明。我们以任意方式对$\{ \ell ,h{\} }^{4}$进行排序。然后通过以下两个标准对${Q}^{ \circ  }\left( D\right)$中的答案排序：首先按它们所属的${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$排序，其次根据主张6所述算法的顺序排序。我们的${Q}^{ \circ  }$算法采用该排序方式，其工作流程如下：预处理阶段首先计算${D}^{\prime }$，然后对所有${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$基于输入${D}^{\prime }$执行预处理。同时通过简单的二分搜索结合相应直接访问算法，计算所有查询${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$对应的$\left| {{Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right) }\right|$值。在查询阶段，利用这些答案计数和给定的$j \in  \mathbb{N}$，首先确定第$j$个答案所在的${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$，然后计算出对应索引${j}^{\prime }$，使得该${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }\left( {D}^{\prime }\right)$中的第${j}^{\prime }$个答案即为${Q}^{ \circ  }\left( D\right)$中的第$j$个答案。最后使用${Q}^{\left( {o}_{1},{o}_{2},{o}_{3},{o}_{4}\right) }$的直接访问算法获取目标答案。

We remark that the technique used in the proof of Lemma 48 has a rich history composed of algorithms based on splitting using degree information. In fact, it is one of the basic techniques for worst-case optimal join algorithms (see, e.g., Reference [34]). Notice that simply applying a worst-case optimal algorithm during preprocessing is not possible in our setting, since the output can be too big to allow this in the desired runtime bounds. Thus, the rewriting into an acyclic instance that we do in Claim 6 is a second crucial ingredient.

我们注意到，引理48证明中所采用的技术拥有丰富的历史渊源，其核心是基于度数信息分割的算法集合。事实上，这是最坏情况下最优连接算法的基本技术之一（参见文献[34]）。需要指出的是，在我们的应用场景中，预处理阶段直接采用最坏情况最优算法并不可行，因为输出规模可能超出预期时间复杂度允许的范围。因此，如主张6所述将问题重写为无环实例，成为第二个关键要素。

For finding cycles of fixed length in graphs, splitting on degree information was first used in Reference [5]. For conjunctive queries, the technique is also part of different algorithms based on so-called submodular width, which is a measure capturing more general classes of tractable queries than fractional hypertree width for decision problems (see References [28, 31]); for the special case of cycles, this is also discussed in Reference [35]. In this line of work, it is, in particular, also known that submodular width can be used for efficient enumeration algorithms [12]. Note that our splitting is slightly stronger than that generally guaranteed by the techniques based on submodular width, as it guarantees that the subinstances that we split into are disjoint. For the algorithms in References $\left\lbrack  {{12},{28},{31}}\right\rbrack$ ,this is generally not the case,so they cannot directly be adapted to work for direct access as in the proof of Lemma 48. In fact, it is an open question whether disjoint splitting is always possible for algorithms based on submodular width (see Reference [27]), and, in particular, it is an open question to which extent submodular width can be used for counting algorithms. Since direct access algorithms directly imply counting algorithms by binary search, this means that the question to which extent Lemma 48 can be generalized to additional queries is also open.

在图中寻找固定长度环的问题上，基于度数信息的分割方法最早见于文献[5]。对于合取查询，该技术也是基于次模宽度（submodular width）的各类算法的组成部分——这种度量指标能比决策问题中的分数超树宽度捕获更广泛的易处理查询类别（参见文献[28,31]）；关于环状结构的特殊情况，文献[35]亦有讨论。该研究脉络特别指出，次模宽度可用于高效枚举算法[12]。值得注意的是，我们的分割方法比次模宽度技术通常保证的更强，因为它确保分割后的子实例互不相交。而文献$\left\lbrack  {{12},{28},{31}}\right\rbrack$中的算法通常不具备此特性，因此无法直接适配引理48证明中的直接访问需求。事实上，基于次模宽度的算法是否总能实现不相交分割仍是开放性问题（见文献[27]），特别是次模宽度在计数算法中的应用范围尚不明确。由于直接访问算法通过二分搜索可直接推导出计数算法，这意味着引理48能否推广到更多查询类型也悬而未决。

### 8.3 Projections and Partial Lexicographic Orders

### 8.3 投影与偏字典序

In this section, we discuss two extensions. First, we would like to support conjunctive queries (with projections) instead of only join queries. Second, we would like to support partial lexicographic orders. These are specified by a permutation $L$ of a subset of the non-projected variables,and the answers are ordered lexicographically by this permutation as before; the order between answers that agree on all variables in $L$ is not specified as part of the problem definition. This way,we define a preorder on the query answers, and turning it into a (complete) order is left to the discretion of the algorithm. More formally,we say that an order $\leq$ of the query answers is compatible with a partial lexicographic order if it is a refinement of that preorder.

本节讨论两个扩展功能：首先支持含投影的合取查询（而非仅连接查询），其次支持偏字典序。后者由非投影变量子集的排列$L$定义，查询结果按该排列字典序排列；对于$L$中所有变量取值相同的记录，问题定义不指定其顺序。由此我们定义了查询结果的预序，而将其转化为全序则由算法自行决定。更形式化地说，若查询结果的排序$\leq$是该预序的细化，则称其与偏字典序兼容。

To achieve these extensions, we use the connection between our work and the notion of elimination orders. Since an order $L$ of the variables of a query $q$ is the reverse of an elimination order if and only if $Q$ is acyclic and $L$ contains no disruptive trio with $Q$ ,the preprocessing we suggested in Theorem 10 can be seen as constructing an equivalent query and corresponding database that have the reverse of $L$ as an elimination order. For more information on the connection between elimination orders and decompositions, see Reference [4].

为实现这些扩展，我们利用研究工作与消元序概念间的关联。当且仅当查询$q$的变量序$L$是无环结构且不含破坏性三元组时，该变量序才是消元序的逆序。定理10所述的预处理过程，可视为构建具有逆消元序的等价查询及对应数据库。关于消元序与分解的更多关联，参见文献[4]。

To support projections, we use a folklore construction that lets us eliminate any prefix of an elimination order while constructing an equivalent corresponding database in linear time. One way of doing that is to eliminate one variable at a time: First, filter the one atom that contains this variable and all its neighbors according to all other atoms that contain this variable (this is the semijoin operator), and then remove the variable from the query and the corresponding columns from the relations (this is the projection operation). To support partial orders, we can complete them into a full order and use the previous algorithm. This leads us to the following definition of the incompatibility number of conjunctive queries and partial lexicographic orders:

为支持投影操作，我们采用经典构造法：在线性时间内构建等价数据库时，可消除消元序的任意前缀。具体实现可逐变量处理：先筛选包含目标变量及其所有邻接点的原子（半连接操作），再从查询中移除该变量并从关系中删除对应列（投影操作）。对于偏序支持，可将其补全为全序后应用现有算法。由此引出合取查询与偏字典序不兼容数的定义：

Definition 49. Let $Q$ be a conjunctive query and $L$ an ordering of a subset of the free variables of $Q$ . Let ${\mathcal{L}}_{Q}^{ + }$ be the set of all orderings of the variables of $Q$ that start with $L$ and end with the projected variables. The incompatibility number of $Q$ and $L$ ,denoted $\iota$ ,is defined as the minimum over all incompatibility numbers of $Q$ and an ordering in ${\mathcal{L}}_{Q}^{ + }$ .

定义49. 设$Q$为合取查询，$L$是其自由变量子集的排序。令${\mathcal{L}}_{Q}^{ + }$为所有以$L$开头、投影变量结尾的变量序集合，则$Q$与$L$的不兼容数$\iota$定义为：该集合中各变量序与查询不兼容数的最小值。

Using this definition, we get an extension of Theorem 10.

基于此定义，我们得到了定理10的扩展形式。

THEOREM 50. Given a conjunctive query and an ordering $L$ of a subset of its free variables with incompatibility number $\iota$ ,direct access with an order compatible with the partial lexicographic order of $L$ can be achieved with $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{l}\right)$ preprocessing and logarithmic access time.

定理50. 给定一个合取查询及其自由变量子集的一个序$L$（不兼容数为$\iota$），若要求访问顺序与$L$的部分字典序兼容，则可通过$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{l}\right)$预处理时间实现对数级访问耗时。

Proof. Let ${L}^{\prime }$ be the ordering of the query variables yielding the incompatibility number according to Definition 49. We perform the preprocessing of the algorithm of Theorem 10 to construct a database ${D}^{\prime }$ corresponding to a disruption-free decomposition for $Q$ with respect to ${L}^{\prime }$ . This takes $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$ time. We then eliminate the projected variables to be left with an equivalent join query with no projections and no disruptive trios. This can be done in $O\left( \left| {D}^{\prime }\right| \right)$ ,since the projected variables appear at the end of an elimination order. We can now use the existing direct-access algorithm from Theorem 1 on the prefix of ${L}^{\prime }$ obtained by removing the projected variables. This takes a further $O\left( \left| {D}^{\prime }\right| \right)$ during preprocessing followed by logarithmic access time.

证明. 设${L}^{\prime }$是根据定义49得到不兼容数的查询变量序。我们执行定理10算法的预处理，构建对应于<b2></b0>关于${L}^{\prime }$的无冲突分解的数据库${D}^{\prime }$，耗时$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\iota }\right)$。随后消除投影变量，得到等价的无投影且无冲突三元组的连接查询。此步骤耗时$O\left( \left| {D}^{\prime }\right| \right)$，因投影变量总出现在消除序末端。此时可对去除投影变量后的${L}^{\prime }$前缀应用定理1的现有直接访问算法，预处理阶段额外耗时$O\left( \left| {D}^{\prime }\right| \right)$，之后即获得对数级访问耗时。

The next natural question is whether this algorithm provides optimal time guarantees for the task at hand. In contrast to queries without projections and complete lexicographic orders, the answer is negative. An example for this is the 4-cycle query ${Q}^{ \circ  }$ from the previous section. There, we saw that if the partial lexicographic order is empty, then we can perform direct-access faster than any completion to a full lexicographic order. If all variables of this query were projected, then it would have at most one answer, and the existence of this answer can be determined in time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ ,since the algorithm of Lemma 48,in particular,lets us decide the existence of an answer. This runtime is again faster than any completion to a full order and so better than what we get from Theorem 50.

接下来的核心问题是：该算法是否为当前任务提供了最优时间保证？与无投影查询和完整字典序情形不同，答案是否定的。例如前文的4-环查询${Q}^{ \circ  }$，当部分字典序为空时，直接访问速度可快于任何完整字典序补全。若该查询所有变量均为投影变量，则至多一个解，且引理48的算法能在$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{3/2}\right)$时间内判定解的存在性——该耗时仍快于任何完整序补全，优于定理50的结果。

We remark that, when only considering linear preprocessing and logarithmic delay, all queries and partial orders that can be solved within this time guarantee can be solved this way through a completion to a full lexicographic order [18]. In contrast, as we have seen here, this is no longer the case when considering larger preprocessing times.

需说明的是，若仅考虑线性预处理和对数延迟，所有可在此时间保证内解决的查询和偏序，均可通过完整字典序补全来实现[18]。但如本文所示，当预处理时间更长时，情况则不再如此。

## 9 Lower Bounds for Enumeration of Cyclic Joins

## 9 循环连接枚举的下界证明

In this section, we show another application of the Zero-Clique Conjecture. Specifically, we prove enumeration lower bounds for cyclic (i.e., non-acyclic) joins, assuming the hardness of Zero-Clique. These lower bounds reprove a dichotomy from Reference [13] under a different complexity assumption.

本节展示零团猜想的另一应用：假设零团问题难解，我们证明循环连接（即非无环连接）的枚举下界。这些下界在不同复杂度假设下，复现了文献[13]的二分定理。

First,in Section 9.1,we prove a lower bound for a variation of $k$ -Set-Intersection. In Section 9.2, we prove hardness of enumerating Loomis-Whitney joins, and then we use a reduction from Loomis-Whitney joins to general cyclic joins in Section 9.3.

首先在第9.1节证明$k$-集合交变体的下界；第9.2节证明Loomis-Whitney连接枚举的困难性；进而第9.3节通过从Loomis-Whitney连接到一般循环连接的归约完成证明。

### 9.1 Hardness of Set-Intersection-Enumeration

### 9.1 集合交枚举的困难性

We start by defining an offline variant of $k$ -Set-Intersection,in which we want to enumerate all elements of all queried intersections.

我们首先定义$k$-集合交的离线变体，其目标是枚举所有查询交集的元素。

Definition 51. In the $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration problem,we are given an instance $\mathcal{I}$ consisting of a universe $U$ and families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ ,where we denote the sets in family ${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$ by ${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$ . We are also given a set of queries $Q$ ,where each query specifies indices $\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$ . The goal is to enumerate all answers to all queries,that is,to enumerate all pairs(q,u)such that $q = \left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$ and $u \in  {S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$ .

定义51. 在$k$-集合交枚举问题中，给定一个由全域$U$和集合族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$组成的实例$\mathcal{I}$，其中用${S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }$表示族${\mathcal{A}}_{i}$中的集合。同时给定查询集$Q$，每个查询指定索引$\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)$。目标是枚举所有查询的答案，即枚举所有满足$q = \left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k}}\right)  \in  Q$且$u \in  {S}_{1,{j}_{1}} \cap  \cdots  \cap  {S}_{k,{j}_{k}}$的(q,u)对。

As usual for enumeration problems,we assume algorithms for $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration work in two phases: In a first phase, we preprocess the input into an index data structure. In a second phase, we enumerate the answers one after the other, measuring the efficiency of the algorithm in the delay, i.e., the time between two consecutive answers.

按照枚举问题的常规处理方式，我们假设$k$-集合交枚举算法分两个阶段工作：第一阶段将输入预处理为索引数据结构；第二阶段逐个枚举答案，并以延迟（即两个连续答案之间的时间）衡量算法效率。

We prove the following analog of Theorem 27.

我们证明以下与定理27对应的结论。

LEMMA 52. Assuming the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture,for every constant $\varepsilon  > 0$ there exists a constant $\delta  > 0$ such that no randomized algorithm solves $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration on universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ with at most $n$ sets in preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and delay $O\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ .

引理52. 在零$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想成立的前提下，对于任意常数$\varepsilon  > 0$，存在常数$\delta  > 0$使得：当全域规模为$\left| U\right|  = n$且集合数量不超过$n$时，不存在随机算法能以$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$延迟解决$k$-集合交枚举问题。

We prove Lemma 52 by contradiction in the rest of this subsection. So,assume that $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration on universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ can be solved in preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$ and delay $O\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$ for some $\varepsilon  > 0$ and $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$ .

本节余下部分将通过反证法证明引理52。假设对于某些$\varepsilon  > 0$和$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$，全域规模$\left| U\right|  = n$上的$k$-集合交枚举问题可在$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {n}^{\delta }\right)$延迟内解决。

We closely follow the proof of Theorem 27, and we only describe the differences. We need an additional source of randomness in the new edge weights ${w}^{\prime }$ ,therefore,for every $v \in  {V}_{1}$ ,we pick a random ${y}_{v} \in  {\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ ,we subtract ${y}_{v}$ from every edge weight ${w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)$ for each $u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ ,and we add ${y}_{v}$ to every edge weight ${w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)$ for each $u \in  {V}_{2}$ . This does not change the weight of a clique $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ . In more detail,we now choose uniformly and independently at random from ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ :

我们严格遵循定理27的证明过程，仅说明差异点。由于新边权重${w}^{\prime }$需要额外的随机源，因此对每个$v \in  {V}_{1}$随机选取${y}_{v} \in  {\mathbb{F}}_{p}$：从每个$u \in  {V}_{k + 1}$对应的边权重${w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)$中减去${y}_{v}$，并向每个$u \in  {V}_{2}$对应的边权重${w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)$添加${y}_{v}$。这不会改变团$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$的权重。更具体而言，现在从${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中均匀独立随机选择：

- one value $x$ ,

- 一个值$x$，

- for all $v \in  {V}_{k + 1}$ and all $j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ a value ${y}_{v}^{j}$ ,and

- 对于所有$v \in  {V}_{k + 1}$及所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$，存在值${y}_{v}^{j}$，且

- for each $v \in  {V}_{1}$ a value ${y}_{v}$ .

- 对于每个$v \in  {V}_{1}$，存在值${y}_{v}$。

We then define the new weight function ${w}^{\prime }$ by setting for any $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  {k + 1}\right\rbrack$ and any $v \in  {V}_{i}$ and $u \in  {V}_{j} :$

随后我们通过设定任意$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  {k + 1}\right\rbrack$与任意$v \in  {V}_{i}$及$u \in  {V}_{j} :$，定义新的权重函数${w}^{\prime }$

$$
{w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)  = x \cdot  w\left( {v,u}\right)  + \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} {y}_{u}^{1} - {y}_{v}, & \text{ if }i = 1,j = k + 1 \\  {y}_{u}^{i} - {y}_{u}^{i - 1}, & \text{ if }2 \leq  i < k,j = k + 1 \\   - {y}_{u}^{k - 1}, & \text{ if }i = k,j = k + 1 \\  {y}_{v}, & \text{ if }i = 1,j = 2 \\  0, & \text{ otherwise. } \end{array}\right.  \tag{7}
$$

With this minor modification of ${w}^{\prime }$ ,we follow the rest of the construction verbatim,in particular, we pick the same parameter $\rho  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ ,and for every weight interval tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$ ,we construct the instance of $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration given by

在对${w}^{\prime }$进行此细微修改后，我们严格遵循后续构造步骤，特别是选取相同参数$\rho  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$，并为每个权重区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)  \in  S$构建由以下给出的$k$-集合交枚举实例

$$
{\mathcal{A}}_{i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {{S}_{i,v} \mid  v \in  {V}_{i}}\right\}  \text{ where }{S}_{i,v} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {u \in  {V}_{k + 1} \mid  {w}^{\prime }\left( {v,u}\right)  \in  {I}_{i}}\right\}  
$$

for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,and the set of queries

对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，以及查询集合

$$
Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k} \mid  {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)  \in  {I}_{0}}\right\}  .
$$

Note that we have for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ that $\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  = \left| {V}_{i}\right|$ ,so $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$ . We then run the assumed $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration algorithm on $\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$ and $Q$ . If this enumeration at some point returns an index corresponding to a zero-clique, then we return this zero-clique. If the enumeration ends without producing a zero-clique, then we return "no."

注意对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$都有$\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  = \left| {V}_{i}\right|$，因此$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$。接着我们在$\mathcal{I} = \left( {{\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k}}\right)$和$Q$上运行假设的$k$-集合交枚举算法。若枚举过程中返回零团的对应索引，则返回该零团；若枚举结束未产生零团，则返回"无"。

By the same arguments as in the proof of Theorem 27, since every zero-clique has a weight interval tuple in $S$ ,every zero-clique appears as an answer to some query $q \in  Q$ . Thus,by enumerating all answers, we enumerate all zero-cliques. In particular, if there exists a zero-clique, then the algorithm will find one. Since we filter the results for zero-cliques, it also holds that if there is no zero-clique, then the algorithm will return "no." This proves correctness.

根据定理27证明中的相同论证，由于每个零团在$S$中都有对应的权重区间元组，所有零团都会作为某个查询$q \in  Q$的答案出现。因此通过枚举全部答案，我们就能枚举所有零团。特别地，若存在零团，算法必能找到。由于我们对结果进行了零团筛选，故当不存在零团时算法也将返回"无"，这证明了正确性。

It remains to analyze the running time. In total over all $\left| S\right|  = O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$ instances,we need preprocessing time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon  + {k\rho }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$ for our choice of $\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ . To analyze the total delay, consider the following claim:

仍需分析运行时间。在所有$\left| S\right|  = O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$实例中，我们为选定$\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$需要$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon  + {k\rho }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)$的预处理时间。关于总延迟分析，考虑以下声明：

CLAIM 7. With probability at least .99,the number of cliques $C = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ such that $C$ is not a zero-clique and the weight interval tuple of $C$ lies in $S$ is bounded by $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$ .

声明7. 以至少99%的概率，满足$C$非零团且其权重区间元组属于$S$的团$C = \left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$数量不超过$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$。

Let us first finish the correctness proof and later prove the claim. Each clique as in the claim is a "false positive," that is, an answer to our queries that does not correspond to a zero-clique. It follows that with probability at least .99,all our $k$ -Set-Intersection-Enumeration instances together enumerate $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$ answers before seeing the first answer that corresponds to a zero-clique. The total query time is then bounded by $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho  + \delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$ for our choice of $\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$ and $\delta  = \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$ . Together with the preprocessing,this solves Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique in time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)  + O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)  =$ $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$ ,which contradicts the Zero- $\left( {k + 1}\right)$ -Clique Conjecture.

让我们先完成正确性证明，随后再验证该主张。该主张中的每个团都是一个“假阳性”，即对我们查询的应答，并不对应一个零团。由此可知，在至少99%的概率下，我们所有的$k$-集合交枚举实例在首次出现对应零团的答案前，总共会枚举出$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$个答案。根据我们选择的$\rho  = \frac{\varepsilon }{2k}$和$\delta  = \frac{\varepsilon }{4k}$参数，总查询时间将被限制在$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho  + \delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$范围内。结合预处理阶段，这就在$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \varepsilon /2}\right)  + O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)  =$$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$时间内解决了零-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团问题，这与零-$\left( {k + 1}\right)$-团猜想相矛盾。

Note that the time bound here only holds with probability .99 . We can make the time bound hold deterministically by aborting the algorithm after an appropriate time $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$ . Then,the running time is deterministic, and the algorithm still finds a zero-clique with probability at least .99. We can boost the success probability by repeating this algorithm. It remains to prove the claim.

注意此处的时间界限仅在99%概率下成立。我们可以通过设定适当的中止时间$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho /2}\right)$使时间界限具有确定性。这样处理后，运行时间将变为确定性，且算法仍有至少99%的概率能找到零团。通过重复执行该算法可提高成功概率。现在只需完成该主张的证明。

Proof of Claim 7. The proof is similar to the one of Claim 1. Let $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$ be a tuple that is not a zero-clique. We use the shorthand notation ${w}_{i}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and ${w}_{0}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$ . The values ${w}_{0},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$ are defined analogously for the original edge weight function $w$ . Expanding the definition of ${w}^{\prime }$ (see Equation (7)),we obtain

主张7的证明。此证明与主张1的证明类似。设$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  \in  {V}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {V}_{k + 1}$为一个非零团的元组。我们使用简记法${w}_{i}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{i},{v}_{k + 1}}\right)$表示$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$和${w}_{0}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {w}^{\prime }\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k}}\right)$。对于原始边权函数$w$，${w}_{0},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$值的定义方式与之类似。根据${w}^{\prime }$的定义展开（参见方程(7)），我们得到

$$
{w}_{0}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{0} + {y}_{{v}_{1}}
$$

$$
{w}_{1}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{1} + {y}_{v}^{1} - {y}_{{v}_{1}} \tag{8}
$$

$$
{w}_{i}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{i} + {y}_{v}^{i} - {y}_{v}^{i - 1}\text{ for each }i \in  \{ 2,\ldots ,k - 1\} 
$$

$$
{w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  {w}_{k} - {y}_{v}^{k - 1}.
$$

We claim that for fixed ${w}_{0},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$ the above equations induce a bijection sending $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ to $\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$ . Indeed,by Equations (4),we can compute $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ given $\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$ . For the other direction,we use that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ is no zero-clique,that is, $0 \neq  w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  = {w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}$ . By adding up all Equations (4),since each term ${y}_{v}^{i}$ and ${y}_{{v}_{1}}$ appears once positively and once negatively,we obtain ${w}_{0}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  \left( {{w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$ . Hence, given $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,we can compute $x = \left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right) /\left( {{w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$ . Note that,here,we do not divide by 0,since ${w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k} \neq  0$ . Next,we can compute ${y}_{{v}_{1}} = x \cdot  {w}_{0} - {w}_{0}^{\prime }$ by rearranging the first equation of (4) (recall that ${w}_{0}$ is fixed,we are given ${w}_{0}^{\prime }$ ,and we just computed $x$ ,so each variable on the right-hand side is known). Similarly,from $x$ and ${y}_{{v}_{1}}$ ,we can compute ${y}_{v}^{1} = x \cdot  {w}_{1} - {y}_{{v}_{1}} - {w}_{1}^{\prime }$ , and for any $1 < i < k$ ,we can compute ${y}_{v}^{i} = x \cdot  {w}_{i} - {y}_{v}^{i - 1} - {w}_{i}^{\prime }$ . This computes $\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$ given $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ .

我们断言，对于固定的${w}_{0},\ldots ,{w}_{k}$，上述方程导出了一个将$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$映射到$\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$的双射。事实上，根据方程(4)，给定$\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$可计算出$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$。对于反向映射，我们利用$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$非零团的性质，即$0 \neq  w\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)  = {w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}$。通过累加所有方程(4)，由于每项${y}_{v}^{i}$和${y}_{{v}_{1}}$均出现一次正一次负，我们得到${w}_{0}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime } = x \cdot  \left( {{w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$。因此，给定$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$便可计算$x = \left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime } + \cdots  + {w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right) /\left( {{w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k}}\right)$。注意此处不涉及除以零运算，因为${w}_{0} + \cdots  + {w}_{k} \neq  0$。接着，通过重新排列(4)的第一个方程（考虑到${w}_{0}$固定、已知${w}_{0}^{\prime }$且已计算出$x$，右侧所有变量均为已知量），可计算出${y}_{{v}_{1}} = x \cdot  {w}_{0} - {w}_{0}^{\prime }$。同理，由$x$和${y}_{{v}_{1}}$可推导出${y}_{v}^{1} = x \cdot  {w}_{1} - {y}_{{v}_{1}} - {w}_{1}^{\prime }$，且对任意$1 < i < k$都能计算出${y}_{v}^{i} = x \cdot  {w}_{i} - {y}_{v}^{i - 1} - {w}_{i}^{\prime }$。这样就实现了由$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$推导$\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$的计算过程。

Since we showed that there is a bijection sending $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ to $\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$ ,and,since $x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}$ are all chosen independently and uniformly random from ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$ ,we obtain that $\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$ is uniformly random in ${\mathbb{F}}_{p}^{k + 1}$ . Since each weight interval ${I}_{i}$ is of length $O\left( {p{n}^{-\rho }}\right)$ ,we have that ${w}_{i}^{\prime } \in  {I}_{i}$ holds with probability $O\left( \frac{p{n}^{-\rho }}{p}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{-\rho }\right)$ ,for each $i \in  \{ 0,1,\ldots ,k\}$ . In total, $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ has a specific weight interval tuple $\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$ with probability $O\left( {n}^{-\left( {k + 1}\right) \rho }\right)$ . Since there are $O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$ tuples in $S$ ,it follows that $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ has a weight interval tuple in $S$ with probability $O\left( {n}^{-\rho }\right)$ .

由于我们证明了存在一个将$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$映射到$\left( {x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}}\right)$的双射，且由于$x,{y}_{{v}_{1}},{y}_{v}^{1},\ldots ,{y}_{v}^{k - 1}$都是从${\mathbb{F}}_{p}$中独立且均匀随机选取的，因此可得出$\left( {{w}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{w}_{k}^{\prime }}\right)$在${\mathbb{F}}_{p}^{k + 1}$上是均匀随机的。由于每个权重区间${I}_{i}$的长度为$O\left( {p{n}^{-\rho }}\right)$，对于每个$i \in  \{ 0,1,\ldots ,k\}$，${w}_{i}^{\prime } \in  {I}_{i}$成立的概率为$O\left( \frac{p{n}^{-\rho }}{p}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{-\rho }\right)$。总体而言，$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$具有特定权重区间元组$\left( {{I}_{0},\ldots ,{I}_{k}}\right)$的概率为$O\left( {n}^{-\left( {k + 1}\right) \rho }\right)$。由于$S$中存在$O\left( {n}^{k\rho }\right)$个元组，因此$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$在$S$中具有权重区间元组的概率为$O\left( {n}^{-\rho }\right)$。

Since there are $O\left( {n}^{k + 1}\right)$ choices for $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$ ,it follows that the expected number of nonzero-cliques with a weight interval tuple in $S$ is $O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$ . The claim now follows from an application of Markov's inequality.

由于$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{k},v}\right)$有$O\left( {n}^{k + 1}\right)$种选择，因此在$S$中具有权重区间元组的非零团期望数量为$O\left( {n}^{k + 1 - \rho }\right)$。该结论现在可通过应用马尔可夫不等式得出。

### 9.2 Hardness of Loomis-Whitney Joins

### 9.2 Loomis-Whitney连接的困难性

In this section, we prove the hardness of enumerating answers to Loomis-Whitney joins based on the hardness results from the previous section. Loomis-Whitney joins are queries of the form

本节中，我们将基于前文的困难性结果，证明枚举Loomis-Whitney连接答案的困难性。Loomis-Whitney连接是形如下式的查询：

$$
L{W}_{k}\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} {R}_{1}\left( {\bar{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\bar{X}}_{k}\right) ,
$$

where for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,we have that ${\bar{X}}_{i} = {x}_{1},\ldots {x}_{i - 1},{x}_{i + 1}\ldots ,{x}_{k}$ . When $k = 3,L{W}_{3}$ is simply the (edge-colored) triangle query:

其中对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，我们有${\bar{X}}_{i} = {x}_{1},\ldots {x}_{i - 1},{x}_{i + 1}\ldots ,{x}_{k}$。当$k = 3,L{W}_{3}$仅为（边着色）三角形查询时：

$$
L{W}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) .
$$

Loomis-Whitney joins are thus generalizations of the triangle join where instead of 3 nodes in a graph where every 2 share an edge,we are looking for $k$ nodes in a hypergraph where every $k - 1$ nodes share an edge.
