Loomis-Whitney joins are of special interest because,as we explain in Section 9.3, they are in a well-defined sense the obstructions to a query being acyclic.

因此，Loomis-Whitney连接是三角形连接的推广——不同于图中每2个节点共享一条边的3个节点，我们在超图中寻找每$k - 1$个节点共享一条边的$k$个节点。Loomis-Whitney连接具有特殊意义，因为正如第9.3节所述，在明确定义的意义上，它们是查询非循环性的障碍。

There is a naive way of designing constant delay algorithms for Loomis-Whitney joins: One can materialize the query result during preprocessing and then simply read them from memory in the enumeration phase. This solution requires preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right) }\right)$ using the algorithm by Ngo et al. [34] for Loomis-Whitney joins or,since $L{W}_{k}$ has fractional cover size $1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right)$ ,any worst-case optimal join algorithm from Theorem 2 will give the same guarantees. In this section, we show that this naive approach is likely optimal for enumeration of Loomis-Whitney joins.

设计Loomis-Whitney连接(Loomis-Whitney joins)的恒定延迟算法存在一种简单思路：可在预处理阶段物化查询结果，在枚举阶段直接从内存读取。该方案需要借助Ngo等人[34]的算法实现$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right) }\right)$预处理时间，或由于$L{W}_{k}$具有分数覆盖大小$1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right)$，根据定理2，任何最坏情况最优连接算法都能提供相同保证。本节将证明，这种朴素方法很可能是Loomis-Whitney连接枚举的最优解。

THEOREM 53. For every $k \geq  3$ and every $\varepsilon  > 0$ there is a $\delta  > 0$ such that there is no enumeration algorithm for $L{W}_{k}$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + \frac{1}{k - 1} - \varepsilon }\right)$ and delay $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ ,assuming the Zero-k-Clique Conjecture.

定理53. 对于任意$k \geq  3$和$\varepsilon  > 0$，存在$\delta  > 0$使得在零k团猜想(Zero-k-Clique Conjecture)成立的前提下，不存在具有$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + \frac{1}{k - 1} - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$延迟的$L{W}_{k}$枚举算法。

Proof. We show that a fast enumeration algorithm for $L{W}_{k}$ yields a fast algorithm for(k - 1)- Set-Intersection-Enumeration,which by Lemma 52 breaks the Zero-k-Clique Conjecture.

证明. 我们证明若存在$L{W}_{k}$的快速枚举算法，则可推导出(k-1)-集合交枚举((k-1)-Set-Intersection-Enumeration)的快速算法，根据引理52这将打破零k团猜想。

Assume that there are $k \geq  3$ and $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that for all $\delta  > 0$ there is an enumeration algorithm for $L{W}_{k}$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right)  - \varepsilon }\right)$ and delay $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$ . Consider an instance $\mathcal{I}$ of(k - 1)-Set-Intersection-Enumeration with universe size $\left| U\right|  = n$ and $O\left( n\right)$ sets as in the statement of Lemma 52. That is,we are given families ${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k - 1} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$ ,where we write ${\mathcal{A}}_{i} = \left\{  {{S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }}\right\}$ and $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$ ,and a set of queries $Q$ ,where each query is of the form $\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k - 1}}\right)$ with ${j}_{i} \in  \left\lbrack  \left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| \right\rbrack$ . We construct a database $D$ as follows: The domain of $D$ is $U \cup$ $\left\lbrack  {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }\right\rbrack$ . Note that $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$ ,so the overall domain size of $D$ is $O\left( n\right)$ .

假设存在$k \geq  3$和$\varepsilon  > 0$使得对于所有$\delta  > 0$，都有具备$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right)  - \varepsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)$延迟的$L{W}_{k}$枚举算法。考虑引理52陈述中论域大小为$\left| U\right|  = n$、具有$O\left( n\right)$个集合的(k-1)-集合交枚举实例$\mathcal{I}$。即给定族${\mathcal{A}}_{1},\ldots ,{\mathcal{A}}_{k - 1} \subseteq  {2}^{U}$（记作${\mathcal{A}}_{i} = \left\{  {{S}_{i,1},\ldots ,{S}_{i,\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }}\right\}$和$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$）及查询集$Q$（每个查询形式为$\left( {{j}_{1},\ldots ,{j}_{k - 1}}\right)$且满足${j}_{i} \in  \left\lbrack  \left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| \right\rbrack$）。我们按如下方式构建数据库$D$：其定义域为$U \cup$$\left\lbrack  {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right| }\right\rbrack$。注意$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\left| {\mathcal{A}}_{i}\right|  \leq  n$，故$D$的总定义域大小为$O\left( n\right)$。

For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,let ${i}^{ + }$ be the unique value in $\left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ with $i + 1 \equiv  {i}^{ + }{\;\operatorname{mod}\;\left( {k - 1}\right) }$ . Note that the atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{X}}_{i}\right)$ of $L{W}_{k}$ contains the variables ${x}_{{i}^{ + }}$ and ${x}_{k}$ . We define the relations:

对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$，令${i}^{ + }$表示$\left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$中满足$i + 1 \equiv  {i}^{ + }{\;\operatorname{mod}\;\left( {k - 1}\right) }$的唯一值。注意$L{W}_{k}$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{X}}_{i}\right)$包含变量${x}_{{i}^{ + }}$和${x}_{k}$。我们定义如下关系：

$$
{R}_{i}^{\prime D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {j,v}\right)  \mid  v \in  {S}_{{i}^{ + },j}}\right\}  \;\text{ for each }i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  ,
$$

$$
{R}_{k}^{D} \mathrel{\text{:=}} Q\text{.}
$$

When interpreted on $D$ ,the join query

当在$D$上解释时，连接查询

$$
L{W}_{k}^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{{1}^{ + }},{x}_{k}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k - 1}^{\prime }\left( {{x}_{{\left( k - 1\right) }^{ + }},{x}_{k}}\right) ,{R}_{k}\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right) 
$$

computes the conjunction ${x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{1}^{ + },{x}_{{1}^{ + }}} \land  \ldots  \land  {x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{\left( k - 1\right) }^{ + },{x}_{{\left( k - 1\right) }^{ + }}}$ and $q = \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right)  \in  Q$ , because the join query (on $D$ ) formally means $\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{i}^{ + },{x}_{{i}^{ + }}} \land  \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right)  \in  Q$ . Therefore, it computes exactly the answers to $q \in  Q$ for the(k - 1)-Set-Intersection-Enumeration instance. To embed $L{W}_{k}^{\prime }$ into $L{W}_{k}$ ,we create(k - 1)-ary relations ${R}_{j}^{D}$ ,for $j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,by simply adding the columns for the missing attributes ${x}_{i},i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {j,{j}^{ + }}\right\}$ in the relations ${R}_{j}^{\prime D}$ . To this end,we extend each 2-tuple in ${R}_{j}^{\prime }$ by all possible combinations of assignments to $\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ to the $k - 3$ attributes in $\left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {j,{j}^{ + }}\right\}$ . Clearly,

计算合取式${x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{1}^{ + },{x}_{{1}^{ + }}} \land  \ldots  \land  {x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{\left( k - 1\right) }^{ + },{x}_{{\left( k - 1\right) }^{ + }}}$与$q = \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right)  \in  Q$，因为（在$D$上的）连接查询形式化表示$\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{k} \in  {S}_{{i}^{ + },{x}_{{i}^{ + }}} \land  \left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right)  \in  Q$。因此，它精确计算出(k-1)-集合交枚举实例中$q \in  Q$的答案。为了将$L{W}_{k}^{\prime }$嵌入$L{W}_{k}$，我们通过简单地为关系${R}_{j}^{\prime D}$中缺失的属性${x}_{i},i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {j,{j}^{ + }}\right\}$添加列，创建(k-1)元关系${R}_{j}^{D}$（针对$j \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$）。为此，我们通过为$\left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {j,{j}^{ + }}\right\}$中的$k - 3$属性分配$\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$的所有可能组合，来扩展${R}_{j}^{\prime }$中的每个二元组。显然，

$$
L{W}_{k}^{\prime }\left( D\right)  = L{W}_{k}\left( D\right) 
$$

so running the assumed enumeration algorithm for $L{W}_{k}$ on the instance $D$ solves(k - 1)-Set-Intersection-Enumeration.

因此在实例$D$上运行假设的$L{W}_{k}$枚举算法，即可解决(k-1)-集合交枚举问题。

It remains to analyze the running time of the resulting algorithm. First observe that all relations in $D$ have arity $k - 1$ . Since the domain size of $D$ is $O\left( n\right)$ ,the relation sizes are at most $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ , and so $\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ . Moreover, $D$ can easily be constructed in time $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ . Thus,the overall preprocessing time is $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ plus the preprocessing time of the algorithm for $L{W}_{k}$ ,which is

还需分析所得算法的运行时间。首先观察到$D$中所有关系的元数均为$k - 1$。由于$D$的域大小为$O\left( n\right)$，关系大小至多为$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$，因此$\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$。此外，$D$可在$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$时间内轻松构建。因此，总预处理时间为$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$加上$L{W}_{k}$算法的预处理时间，即

$$
O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} + {\left| D\right| }^{1 + 1/\left( {k - 1}\right)  - \varepsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{k - {\varepsilon }^{\prime }}\right) ,
$$

for ${\varepsilon }^{\prime } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \{ 1,\left( {k - 1}\right) \varepsilon \}$ . For any desired ${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$ ,by setting $\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\delta }^{\prime }/\left( {k - 1}\right)$ ,we obtain delay $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{\left( {k - 1}\right) \delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$ . By Lemma 52,this violates the Zero- $k$ -Clique Conjecture.

对于任意期望的${\delta }^{\prime } > 0$，通过设定$\delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\delta }^{\prime }/\left( {k - 1}\right)$，我们可获得延迟$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{\left( {k - 1}\right) \delta }\right)  = O\left( {n}^{{\delta }^{\prime }}\right)$。根据引理52，这违背了零-$k$-团猜想。

### 9.3 Hardness of Cyclic Joins

### 9.3 循环连接的困难性

In this section, we describe the implication of hardness of Loomis-Whitney joins on the hardness of other cyclic joins and derive an enumeration lower bound for self-join-free cyclic joins based of Zero- $k$ -Clique. A known characterization of cyclic hypergraphs is that they contain a chordless cycle or a non-conformal clique (i.e., a set of pairwise neighbors that are not contained in an edge); see, e.g., the survey in Reference [14]. By considering a minimal non-conformal clique, Brault-Baron [13] used this property to claim that every cyclic query contains either a chordless cycle or a set of $k$ variables such that there is no atom that contains all of them,but for every subset of size $k - 1$ there is such an atom. In other words,by removing all occurrences of some variables,and removing some atoms whose variables are contained in other atoms, we can obtain either a Loomis-Whitney join or a chordless cycle join. In case of a chordless cycle, we can always use it to solve a chordless cycle of length 3 (also called a triangle), which can be seen as a Loomis-Whitney join of size $k = 3$ . Stated in different words,Brault-Baron [13] essentially proved the following lemma:

本节阐述Loomis-Whitney连接困难性对其他循环连接的影响，并基于零-$k$-团推导出无自连接循环连接的枚举下界。循环超图的已知特征是其包含无弦环或非共形团（即不被任何边完全包含的成对邻接变量集），详见参考文献[14]的综述。通过考察极小非共形团，Brault-Baron[13]利用该性质证明：每个循环查询要么包含无弦环，要么存在$k$个变量的集合——这些变量不被任何原子谓词完全包含，但任意$k - 1$大小的子集都存在包含该子集的原子谓词。换言之，通过删除某些变量的所有出现项及变量被其他原子包含的原子谓词，我们可得到Loomis-Whitney连接或无弦环连接。对于无弦环情形，总能将其转化为长度为3的无弦环（即三角形），这可视为大小为$k = 3$的Loomis-Whitney连接。Brault-Baron[13]实质上证明了以下引理：

LEMMA 54. Let $Q$ be a self-join-free cyclic join. There exists $k \geq  3$ such that there is an exact reduction from $L{W}_{k}$ to $Q$ .

引理54. 设$Q$为无自连接的循环连接，存在$k \geq  3$使得从$L{W}_{k}$到$Q$存在精确归约。

Since enumeration in linear preprocessing time and constant delay is closed under exact reductions, by combining Lemma 54 with Theorem 53, we conclude the enumeration hardness of cyclic joins based on the hardness of Zero- $k$ -Clique: They have no enumeration algorithm with linear preprocessing time and constant delay.

由于线性预处理时间与常数延迟的枚举在精确归约下封闭，结合引理54与定理53，我们基于零-$k$-团的困难性得出循环连接的枚举困难性结论：不存在具有线性预处理时间和常数延迟的枚举算法。

THEOREM 55. Let $Q$ be a self-join-free cyclic join. Assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture,there exists an $\varepsilon  > 0$ such that there is no enumeration algorithm for $Q$ with preprocessing time $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + \varepsilon }\right)$ and delay $O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\varepsilon }\right)$ .

定理55. 设$Q$为无自连接的循环连接。在零-团猜想成立的前提下，存在$\varepsilon  > 0$使得$Q$不存在预处理时间$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{1 + \varepsilon }\right)$且延迟$O\left( {\left| D\right| }^{\varepsilon }\right)$的枚举算法。

Proof. Let $k$ be the constant from Lemma 54. Then,apply Theorem 53 with $\varepsilon  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( {\frac{1}{2\left( {k - 1}\right) },\delta }\right)$ .

证明. 令$k$为引理54中的常数，对$\varepsilon  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \min \left( {\frac{1}{2\left( {k - 1}\right) },\delta }\right)$应用定理53即得证。

## 10 Conclusion

## 10 结论

In this article, we identified a parameter that we call the incompatibility number of a join query and an order of its variables. We proposed an algorithm for direct access to the answers of a join query in the lexicographic order induced by the variable order using a reduction to the acyclic case without disruptive trios that had been studied in Reference [18]. The exponent of the preprocessing phase of this algorithm is exactly the incompatibility number. We then complemented this upper bound by a matching lower bound that shows that the incompatibility number cannot be beaten as the exponent of the preprocessing for any join query, assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture from fine-grained complexity theory. Due to the relationship of the incompatibility number and fractional hypertree width, it follows that efficient direct access in any lexicographic order requires preprocessing that is exponential in the fractional hypertree width of the query. As part of the lower bound proof, we established the complexity equivalence between queries with the same structure with and without self-joins for direct access.

本文提出了连接查询及其变量序的不兼容数参数。通过归约至参考文献[18]研究的无破坏性三元组无环情形，我们设计了按变量序字典序直接访问连接查询结果的算法，其预处理阶段的指数阶数恰为不兼容数。基于细粒度复杂性理论中的零-团猜想，我们进一步证明该下界是紧的——任何连接查询的预处理都不可能突破不兼容数对应的指数阶数。由于不兼容数与分数超树宽度的关联性，这意味着所有字典序下的高效直接访问都需要查询分数超树宽度指数级的预处理。在下界证明过程中，我们建立了含自连接与不含自连接的同构查询在直接访问问题上的计算等价性。

With only a minor change to the proof of our direct-access lower bounds, we also showed lower bounds for enumeration. First, we showed that, for Loomis-Whitney joins, we cannot significantly improve upon a simple algorithm that computes all answers during preprocessing, assuming the Zero-Clique Conjecture. Then, under the same assumption, we showed that acyclic queries are the only self-join-free join queries whose answers can be enumerated in constant delay after linear preprocessing. This gives further evidence to a dichotomy that was already shown using a different complexity hypothesis.

仅需对我们的直接访问下界证明稍作修改，我们便进一步得出了枚举问题的下界。首先证明在零团猜想（Zero-Clique Conjecture）成立的前提下，对于Loomis-Whitney连接运算，我们无法显著优于在预处理阶段计算所有结果的简单算法。随后在同一假设下，我们证明无环查询是唯一能在线性预处理后实现常数延迟枚举答案的无自连接查询，这为基于不同复杂性假设已揭示的二分现象提供了新的佐证。

We have also presented several extensions of our main results on direct access where we weakened the order requirements or allowed projections. In these settings, we have seen that our approach does not yield optimal algorithms. It would be interesting to understand the complexity landscape for these extensions better, and we leave this as an open question for future work.

我们还对直接访问的主要研究成果进行了多项扩展，包括放宽排序要求或允许投影操作。在这些设定下，我们发现现有方法无法产生最优算法。深入理解这些扩展的复杂性图景将极具研究价值，我们将其列为未来工作的开放性问题。

On a more technical level, we have seen that the Zero-Clique Conjecture is tightly linked to $k$ -Set-Intersection and $k$ -Set-Disjointness,which can be seen as variants of a join query. Thus,it seems plausible that there are additional applications of the Zero-Clique Conjecture in database theory, and it would be interesting to further explore such connections.

在技术层面，我们发现零团猜想与$k$集合相交及$k$集合互斥问题存在紧密关联——这些问题可视为连接查询的变体。这表明零团猜想在数据库理论中可能存在更多应用场景，值得进一步探索此类关联性。
